# @molecule/app-ui-angular

Angular UI components for molecule.dev.

Provides standalone Angular components at parity with `@molecule/app-ui-react`:
every React export exists here under the same name, with the same props, the
same rendered DOM (ClassMap classes, attributes, inline styles, text) and the
same behaviour. Molecule's conformance tests render each component in React and
Angular and compare the two.

React-named exports include `Button`, `Card` (+ `CardHeader`, `CardTitle`,
`CardDescription`, `CardContent`, `CardFooter`), `Input`, `Select`,
`Textarea`, `Checkbox`, `RadioGroup`, `Switch`, `Accordion`, `Alert`,
`Avatar`, `Badge`, `EmptyState`, `Icon`, `Label`, `Progress`, `Separator`,
`Skeleton`, `Spinner`, `Tabs`, `Table`, `Pagination`, `Tooltip`, `Modal`,
`Toast` (+ `ToastContainer`, `ToastProvider`), `ConfirmDialog`,
`PromptDialog`, `Dropdown` (+ `DropdownLabel`, `DropdownSeparator`),
`Form`, `FormField`, `LanguagePicker`, `PageHeader`, `PageShell`,
`ResponsiveAppShell` (+ its `TopBar`, `Sidebar`, `Drawer`, `Content`
parts), `SidebarUserCard`, `UserMenu`, `UserMenuPopover` (+ its `Trigger`,
`Panel`, `SignOut` parts), `AuthGuard`, `ConfirmButton`,
`OptimisticToggleButton`, `ThemeToggle`, and the hooks `useToast`,
`usePanelClose`, `useIsDesktop` and `useUserMenuPopoverClose`.

> **Auto-generated, AI-first package reference** for the [molecule.dev](https://molecule.dev) ecosystem.
> It is written to be read by coding agents as much as by people, and is generated from this
> package's source — edit `src/index.ts` JSDoc, not this file.

## Quick Start

```ts
import { Component } from '@angular/core'
import { setClassMap } from '@molecule/app-ui'
import { classMap } from '@molecule/app-ui-tailwind'
import { Button, Input } from '@molecule/app-ui-angular'

// Once at startup, before the first component renders:
setClassMap(classMap)

@Component({
  selector: 'app-save-panel',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Button, Input],
  template: `
    <input mol-input placeholder="Name" [value]="name" (change)="onName($event)" />
    <button mol-button color="primary" [loading]="saving" (click)="save()">Save</button>
  `,
})
class SavePanel {
  name = ''
  saving = false
  onName(event: Event): void {
    this.name = (event.target as HTMLInputElement).value
  }
  save(): void {}
}
```

## Type

`framework`

## Installation

```bash
npm install @molecule/app-ui-angular @angular/common @angular/core @angular/platform-browser @molecule/app-angular @molecule/app-i18n @molecule/app-icons @molecule/app-ui
```

## API

### Interfaces

#### `CSSProperties`

Framework-agnostic CSS properties.
Mirrors React.CSSProperties but without React dependency.

```typescript
interface CSSProperties {
  [key: string]: string | number | undefined
}
```

#### `KitEvent`

The event a UI kit hands a component's handler: the members that behave the same on every kit. React's kit
passes React's event (so `stopPropagation()` stops React parent handlers), the other kits pass the DOM event;
both satisfy this type. A handler that needs a member only one of them has (`nativeEvent`, `composedPath()`,
`isComposing`) takes the component's ref element (see {@link BaseProps}) and listens on it.

`type` is left out on purpose: kits report different event types for the same moment (a text field's
`onChange` is React's `'change'` and the DOM's `'input'`).

```typescript
interface KitEvent {
  /** The element the event started on (`(event.target as HTMLInputElement).value` reads a field). */
  readonly target: EventTarget | null
  /** The element of the component the handler was passed to. */
  readonly currentTarget: EventTarget | null
  /** Whether a handler already called {@link KitEvent.preventDefault}. */
  readonly defaultPrevented: boolean
  /** Cancels the browser's default action (a form's submit, a checkbox's toggle). */
  preventDefault(): void
  /** Stops handlers of ancestor components of the same kit. */
  stopPropagation(): void
}
```

#### `KitModifierKeys`

The modifier keys held during a mouse or keyboard event.

```typescript
interface KitModifierKeys {
  /** Alt (Option) was held. */
  readonly altKey: boolean
  /** Control was held. */
  readonly ctrlKey: boolean
  /** Meta (Command, Windows) was held. */
  readonly metaKey: boolean
  /** Shift was held. */
  readonly shiftKey: boolean
}
```

#### `KitMouseEvent`

A mouse event as every kit hands it to a handler.

```typescript
interface KitMouseEvent extends KitEvent, KitModifierKeys {
  /** The button that changed state (0 main, 1 auxiliary, 2 secondary). */
  readonly button: number
  /** The buttons held, as a bit mask. */
  readonly buttons: number
  /** The pointer's x position in the viewport. */
  readonly clientX: number
  /** The pointer's y position in the viewport. */
  readonly clientY: number
  /** The pointer's x position in the document. */
  readonly pageX: number
  /** The pointer's y position in the document. */
  readonly pageY: number
  /** The click count of a click event. */
  readonly detail: number
  /** The element the pointer came from or went to, for enter and leave events. */
  readonly relatedTarget: EventTarget | null
}
```

#### `KitKeyboardEvent`

A keyboard event as every kit hands it to a handler.

```typescript
interface KitKeyboardEvent extends KitEvent, KitModifierKeys {
  /** The key's value (`'Enter'`, `'a'`). */
  readonly key: string
  /** The physical key (`'KeyA'`). */
  readonly code: string
  /** Whether the key is held down and auto-repeating. */
  readonly repeat: boolean
}
```

#### `KitFocusEvent`

A focus event as every kit hands it to a handler.

```typescript
interface KitFocusEvent extends KitEvent {
  /** The element losing focus (on focus) or gaining it (on blur). */
  readonly relatedTarget: EventTarget | null
}
```

#### `BaseProps`

Base props shared by all components.

Ref element: a component that renders one main element gives the caller that element, the one
that takes `className` and `style`. Each UI kit hands it over in its framework's own way: React
and Solid a `ref`, Vue a template ref's `$el`, Svelte `bind:ref`, Angular the component's
`element()` signal. It is empty while the element is not rendered (a closed Tooltip, Dropdown or
Modal). A component's own doc names the element when it is not the outermost one.

```typescript
interface BaseProps {
  /**
   * Additional CSS class name(s).
   */
  className?: string

  /**
   * Inline styles.
   */
  style?: CSSProperties

  /**
   * Test ID for automated testing.
   */
  testId?: string

  /**
   * Automation ID for AI agents and E2E tests. Maps to the `data-mol-id`
   * HTML attribute. Use `molId()` from `./automation.js` to generate
   * semantic IDs. (Tooling only — screen readers do not expose `data-*`
   * attributes; accessible names come from labels/`aria-*`.)
   */
  automationId?: string

  /**
   * Disables the controls the component renders: a disabled Button, Input, Textarea, Select,
   * Checkbox, Switch, RadioGroup, Tabs, Accordion or Pagination takes no input, a disabled
   * Dropdown never opens, and a disabled Card never calls `onClick` (an interactive one is
   * announced as disabled and leaves the tab order). A component that renders no control
   * (Badge, Spinner, Skeleton, a layout box) ignores it.
   */
  disabled?: boolean
}
```

#### `HTMLElementProps`

Base props for HTML elements.
Framework bindings should extend this with framework-specific attributes.

```typescript
interface HTMLElementProps extends BaseProps {
  id?: string
  title?: string
  tabIndex?: number
  role?: ElementRole
  'aria-label'?: string
  'aria-labelledby'?: string
  'aria-describedby'?: string
  'aria-hidden'?: boolean
  'aria-disabled'?: boolean
  'aria-expanded'?: boolean
  'aria-selected'?: boolean
  'aria-checked'?: boolean | 'mixed'
  'aria-pressed'?: boolean | 'mixed'
  'aria-invalid'?: boolean
  'aria-required'?: boolean
  'aria-readonly'?: boolean
  'aria-busy'?: boolean
  'aria-live'?: 'off' | 'polite' | 'assertive'
  onClick?: MouseEventHandler
  onDoubleClick?: MouseEventHandler
  onMouseEnter?: MouseEventHandler
  onMouseLeave?: MouseEventHandler
  onFocus?: FocusEventHandler
  onBlur?: FocusEventHandler
  onKeyDown?: KeyboardEventHandler
  onKeyUp?: KeyboardEventHandler
  onKeyPress?: KeyboardEventHandler
}
```

#### `InputElementProps`

Base props for input elements.

```typescript
interface InputElementProps extends HTMLElementProps {
  name?: string
  value?: string | number | readonly string[]
  defaultValue?: string | number | readonly string[]
  placeholder?: string
  required?: boolean
  readOnly?: boolean
  autoFocus?: boolean
  autoComplete?: string
  maxLength?: number
  minLength?: number
  pattern?: string
  onChange?: ChangeEventHandler
  onInput?: FormEventHandler
}
```

#### `ButtonElementProps`

Base props for button elements.

```typescript
interface ButtonElementProps extends HTMLElementProps {
  /**
   * Defaults to `'button'` in every framework binding, so a button never submits a
   * form by accident. A form's primary action must set `type="submit"`: Enter then
   * submits the form, and tests that select `button[type="submit"]` find it.
   */
  type?: 'button' | 'submit' | 'reset'
  name?: string
  value?: string
  form?: string
}
```

```typescript
interface InputProps extends InputElementProps {
```

#### `RadioGroupProps`

Props for the RadioGroup component.

```typescript
interface RadioGroupProps<T = string> extends BaseProps {
  /**
   * Radio options.
   */
  options: RadioOption<T>[]

  /**
   * Current value.
   */
  value?: T

  /**
   * Change handler.
   */
  onChange?: (value: T) => void

  /**
   * Radio size.
   */
  size?: Size

  /**
   * Group label.
   */
  label?: string

  /**
   * Shared `name` attribute for the group's radio inputs (used for native
   * form submission). When omitted, a unique per-instance name is generated
   * so separate groups never merge — the visible `label` is deliberately
   * NOT used as the name, because two groups with the same label (e.g. two
   * "Size" pickers) would otherwise form ONE native radio group and
   * deselect each other.
   */
  name?: string

  /**
   * Layout direction.
   */
  direction?: 'horizontal' | 'vertical'

  /**
   * Error message.
   */
  error?: string
}
```

```typescript
   * Tab items.

  items: TabItem<T>[]

  /**
   * Current active tab.
   */
  value?: T

  /**
   * Default active tab.
   */
  defaultValue?: T

  /**
   * Change handler.
   */
  onChange?: (value: T) => void

  /**
   * Tab variant.
   */
  variant?: 'line' | 'enclosed' | 'soft-rounded' | 'solid-rounded'

  /**
   * Tab size.
   */
  size?: Size

  /**
   * Whether tabs are fitted (take full width).
   */
  fitted?: boolean
}
```

#### `AccordionItem`

A single item in an Accordion component.

```typescript
interface AccordionItem<T = string> {
  /**
   * Item value/id.
   */
  value: T

  /**
   * Item header/trigger.
   */
  header: Children

  /**
   * Item content.
   */
  content: Children

  /**
   * Whether the item is disabled.
   */
  disabled?: boolean
}
```

#### `AccordionProps`

Props for the Accordion component.

```typescript
interface AccordionProps<T = string> extends BaseProps {
  /**
   * Accordion items.
   */
  items: AccordionItem<T>[]

  /**
   * Expanded item(s).
   */
  value?: T | T[]

  /**
   * Default expanded item(s).
   */
  defaultValue?: T | T[]

  /**
   * Change handler.
   */
  onChange?: (value: T | T[]) => void

  /**
   * Whether multiple items can be expanded.
   */
  multiple?: boolean

  /**
   * Whether items can be collapsed.
   */
  collapsible?: boolean
}
```

#### `TableColumn`

Table column definition.

```typescript
interface TableColumn<T> {
  /**
   * Column key (data property).
   */
  key: keyof T | string

  /**
   * Column header.
   */
  header: Children

  /**
   * Custom cell renderer.
   */
  render?: (value: unknown, row: T, index: number) => Children

  /**
   * Column width.
   */
  width?: string | number

  /**
   * Whether the column is sortable.
   */
  sortable?: boolean

  /**
   * Text alignment.
   */
  align?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right'
}
```

#### `TableProps`

Props for the Table component.

Ref element: the `<table>`.

```typescript
interface TableProps<T> extends HTMLElementProps {
  /**
   * Table data.
   */
  data: T[]

  /**
   * Column definitions.
   */
  columns: TableColumn<T>[]

  /**
   * Row key extractor.
   */
  rowKey?: keyof T | ((row: T) => string | number)

  /**
   * Whether to show borders.
   */
  bordered?: boolean

  /**
   * Whether rows are striped.
   */
  striped?: boolean

  /**
   * Whether rows are hoverable.
   */
  hoverable?: boolean

  /**
   * Table size.
   */
  size?: Size

  /**
   * Empty state content.
   */
  emptyContent?: Children

  /**
   * Loading state.
   */
  loading?: boolean

  /**
   * Sort configuration.
   */
  sort?: { key: string; direction: 'asc' | 'desc' }

  /**
   * Sort change handler.
   */
  onSort?: (key: string, direction: 'asc' | 'desc') => void

  /**
   * Row click handler.
   */
  onRowClick?: (row: T, index: number) => void
}
```

#### `PaginationProps`

Props for the Pagination component (page navigation with current page, total, page size, and change callbacks).

```typescript
interface PaginationProps extends BaseProps {
  /**
   * Current page (1-indexed).
   */
  page: number

  /**
   * Total number of pages.
   */
  totalPages: number

  /**
   * Page change handler.
   */
  onChange: (page: number) => void

  /**
   * Number of sibling pages to show.
   */
  siblings?: number

  /**
   * Number of boundary pages to show.
   */
  boundaries?: number

  /**
   * Pagination size.
   */
  size?: Size

  /**
   * Whether to show first/last buttons.
   */
  showFirstLast?: boolean

  /**
   * Whether to show previous/next buttons.
   */
  showPrevNext?: boolean

  /**
   * Accessible labels for pagination controls.
   */
  labels?: {
    nav?: string
    first?: string
    previous?: string
    next?: string
    last?: string
    goToPage?: (page: number) => string
  }
}
```

#### `SkeletonProps`

Props for the Skeleton loading placeholder component.

```typescript
interface SkeletonProps extends BaseProps {
  /**
   * Skeleton width.
   */
  width?: string | number

  /**
   * Skeleton height.
   */
  height?: string | number

  /**
   * Whether the skeleton is circular.
   */
  circle?: boolean

  /**
   * Border radius.
   */
  borderRadius?: string | number

  /**
   * Animation type.
   */
  animation?: 'pulse' | 'wave' | 'none'
}
```

#### `AccordionItemData`

One accordion item (React's `AccordionItem`).

```typescript
interface AccordionItemData {
  /** The item's value (identity for expansion state). */
  value: string
  /** The header (text, or a TemplateRef from a node value). */
  header?: unknown
  /** The body (text, or a TemplateRef from a node value). */
  content?: unknown
  /** Whether the item's trigger is disabled. */
  disabled?: boolean
}
```

#### `AuthGuardProps`

Props for {@link AuthGuard} (React's `AuthGuardProps`, in Angular's form).

The fallback UI is a `string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>` input or a
projected `[slot=loadingFallback]`; the authenticated children are projected
content, so they have no member.

```typescript
interface AuthGuardProps {
  /** Element rendered while the auth status is `loading`. */
  loadingFallback?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** i18n key for the default loading-tag text (default `common.loading`). */
  loadingKey?: string
  /** Fallback text if the i18n key is missing (default `Loading...`). */
  loadingDefault?: string
  /** Path to redirect to when unauthenticated (default `/login`). */
  loginPath?: string
  /** Fired when `isAuthenticated` becomes true (also when true at mount). */
  onAuthenticated?: () => void
}
```

#### `ConfirmButtonProps`

Props for {@link ConfirmButton} (React's `ConfirmButtonProps`, in Angular's
form).

The three labels are node-valued in React; here each is a
`string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>` input, and a caller may project the
same label as `[slot=label]` / `[slot=confirmLabel]` / `[slot=pendingLabel]`
instead. The button members React keeps from `ButtonProps` (`variant`,
`color`, `size`, `className`, `style`, `testId`, `automationId`,
`disabled`) are all accepted here too.

```typescript
interface ConfirmButtonProps {
  /**
   * Fired ONLY on the confirming (second) click — i.e. when the action
   * actually commits.
   */
  onConfirm?: () => void | Promise<void>
  /** Visible label in the idle (armed-to-be-clicked) state. */
  label?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Visible label in the armed state (defaults to a localized "Confirm?"). */
  confirmLabel?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Visible label while {@link ConfirmButtonProps.onConfirm} is pending. */
  pendingLabel?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Seconds before the armed state auto-disarms (`0` disables auto-disarm). */
  disarmSeconds?: number
  /** Disables the control entirely (idle and confirming states). */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** `data-testid` for the button. */
  testId?: string
  /** Extra classes joined onto the button classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** Button variant (passed through to the button classes). */
  variant?: ButtonVariant
  /** Button color; the armed state always shows the error color. */
  color?: ColorVariant
  /** Button size. */
  size?: Size
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
}
```

#### `ConfirmDialogProps`

Props for {@link ConfirmDialog} (React's `ConfirmDialogProps`, in Angular's
form).

Node-valued members are `string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>` inputs; a
caller may project the same content as `[slot=title]` / `[slot=description]`
instead. `onClose` and `onConfirm` keep React's names and signatures here;
the component emits them through its `close` and `confirm` outputs.

```typescript
interface ConfirmDialogProps {
  /** Whether the dialog is open. */
  open: boolean
  /** Called when the dialog should close (carried by the `close` output). */
  onClose: () => void
  /** Title. */
  title?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Body / warning text. */
  description?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Confirm-button label. */
  confirmLabel?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Cancel-button label. */
  cancelLabel?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Called when the user confirms (carried by the `confirm` output). */
  onConfirm: () => void | Promise<void>
  /** Is the confirm action destructive? Defaults to true. */
  destructive?: boolean
  /** Disables both buttons (the caller owns the pending state). */
  loading?: boolean
}
```

#### `ReactFormProps`

Props for {@link Form} (React's `ReactFormProps`, in Angular's form).

React's form adds one prop to the core `FormProps`: a failure managed by an
external form controller (`useForm().formState.submitError`). Binding the
`submitError` input is the Angular equivalent — the form then renders the
same accessible server-error list instead of the failure it captured.

```typescript
interface ReactFormProps extends FormProps {
  /** A rejected submission, for example useForm().formState.submitError. */
  submitError?: unknown
}
```

#### `IconProps`

Props for {@link Icon} (React's `IconProps`, in Angular's form).

React extends `SVGProps<SVGSVGElement>` so any SVG attribute reaches the
`<svg>`; the host IS the `<svg>`, so extra `data-*`, `aria-*`, `role` and
event attributes spread onto it exactly as React spreads them. The named
props are typed here.

```typescript
interface IconProps {
  /** The glyph name from the bonded icon set. */
  name: IconName
  /** Width and height in pixels (default 20). */
  size?: number
  /** Class name forwarded to the `<svg>`. */
  className?: string
  /** Explicit `aria-hidden` (React keeps `false` as the string `"false"`). */
  'aria-hidden'?: boolean | string
}
```

#### `LanguagePickerProps`

Props for {@link LanguagePicker} (React's `LanguagePickerProps`, in
Angular's form).

React's `renderTrigger` is a render function receiving the open action;
here it is a `TemplateRef<unknown>` whose context carries `open` (also as
`$implicit`). Extra `<button>` attributes reach the trigger through the
host (React's rest spread).

```typescript
interface LanguagePickerProps {
  /** i18n key for the trigger label / aria-label (default `footer.language`). */
  labelKey?: string
  /** Fallback label if the i18n key is missing (default `Language`). */
  labelDefault?: string
  /** i18n key for the modal title (default `languagePicker.modalTitle`). */
  modalTitleKey?: string
  /** Fallback modal title if the i18n key is missing (default `Choose language`). */
  modalTitleDefault?: string
  /** Icon glyph before the current locale name (default `globe`). */
  icon?: IconName
  /** Pixel size for the rendered icon (default 16). */
  iconSize?: number
  /** What the trigger renders: the locale name, its code, or the icon only. */
  display?: 'name' | 'code' | 'icon'
  /** Extra classes appended to the trigger button. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object for the trigger button (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** Optional trigger replacement (a node value rendered as a template). */
  renderTrigger?: TemplateRef<unknown>
}
```

#### `LoadErrorBannerProps`

Props for {@link LoadErrorBanner} (React's `LoadErrorBannerProps`, in
Angular's form). Rest props (`data-*`, `aria-*`, `id`) land on the host, so
they are not enumerated here.

```typescript
interface LoadErrorBannerProps {
  /** The primary error line. */
  message?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Secondary line under the message. */
  detail?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Called when Retry is clicked; a returned promise keeps the button pending. */
  onRetry?: () => void | Promise<void>
  /** Retry-button label. */
  retryLabel?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** `data-mol-id` for the retry button (default `load-error-retry`). */
  retryMolId?: string
  /** Extra classes for the root (React's rest `className`). */
  className?: string
}
```

#### `OptimisticToggleButtonProps`

Props for {@link OptimisticToggleButton} (React's
`OptimisticToggleButtonProps`, in Angular's form).

The labels and icons are node-valued in React; here each is a
`string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>` input, and the labels may also be
projected as `[slot=activeLabel]` / `[slot=inactiveLabel]` (icons as
`[slot=leftIcon]` / `[slot=rightIcon]`).

```typescript
interface OptimisticToggleButtonProps {
  /** The loaded server value. */
  value: boolean
  /** Saves the desired boolean and returns the server-confirmed boolean. */
  commit: (desired: boolean) => Promise<boolean>
  /** Label when the displayed value is true. */
  activeLabel: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Label when the displayed value is false. */
  inactiveLabel: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Receives a failed save after the controller has restored the confirmed value. */
  onError?: (error: unknown) => void
  /** Button variant. */
  variant?: ButtonVariant
  /** Button color. */
  color?: ColorVariant
  /** Button size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Whether the button stretches to fill its container. */
  fullWidth?: boolean
  /** Left icon (node value). */
  leftIcon?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Right icon (node value). */
  rightIcon?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the button (permissions, never a pending save). */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
}
```

#### `PanelCloseProviderProps`

Props for {@link PanelCloseProvider} (React's `PanelCloseProviderProps`, in
Angular's form). The panel content is projected content, so it has no
member.

```typescript
interface PanelCloseProviderProps {
  /** Callback that dismisses the enclosing drawer/modal. */
  close: () => void
}
```

#### `ProgressProps`

Props for {@link Progress} (React's `ProgressProps`, in Angular's form).

`React.CSSProperties` arrives as a plain style record, the same shape every
component in this kit takes for its `style` input.

```typescript
interface ProgressProps {
  /** Progress value (0–`max`). */
  value: number
  /** Maximum value. Defaults to 100. */
  max?: number
  /** Progress size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Progress color. */
  color?: ColorVariant
  /** Whether the numeric value label renders. */
  showValue?: boolean
  /** Accessible label. */
  label?: string
  /** Whether the progress is indeterminate. */
  indeterminate?: boolean
  /** Extra classes joined onto the wrapper classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
}
```

#### `PromptDialogProps`

Props for {@link PromptDialog} (React's `PromptDialogProps`, in Angular's
form).

Node-valued members are `string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>` inputs; the
title may also be projected as `[slot=title]`. `onClose` keeps React's name
and signature and is carried by the component's `close` output.

```typescript
interface PromptDialogProps {
  /** Whether the dialog is open. */
  open: boolean
  /** Called when the dialog should close (carried by the `close` output). */
  onClose: () => void
  /** Dialog heading. */
  title?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Explanatory line under the title. */
  description?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Input placeholder. */
  placeholder?: string
  /** Value the input starts from every time the dialog opens. */
  initialValue?: string
  /** Called with the entered value on confirm. */
  onSubmit: (value: string) => void | Promise<void>
  /** Confirm-button label. */
  confirmLabel?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Cancel-button label. */
  cancelLabel?: string | number | TemplateRef<unknown>
  /** Renders the confirm button in the error color. */
  destructive?: boolean
  /** `data-mol-id` for the text input. */
  inputMolId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the confirm button. */
  confirmMolId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the cancel button. */
  cancelMolId?: string
}
```

#### `RadioOption`

One radio option.

```typescript
interface RadioOption {
  /** The option's value. */
```

```typescript
}
```

#### `UserMenuPopoverPanelProps`

Props for {@link UserMenuPopoverPanel} (React's
`UserMenuPopoverPanelProps`, in Angular's form). The panel body is
projected content, so it has no member.

```typescript
interface UserMenuPopoverPanelProps {
  /** i18n key for the panel's aria-label (default `userMenu.panelLabel`). */
  ariaLabelKey?: string
  /** Fallback aria-label (default `Account menu`). */
  ariaLabelDefault?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the panel (default `user-menu-panel`). */
  dataMolId?: string
  /** Extra classes for the panel. */
  className?: string
}
```

#### `UserMenuPopoverSignOutProps`

Props for {@link UserMenuPopoverSignOut} (React's
`UserMenuPopoverSignOutProps`, in Angular's form).

```typescript
interface UserMenuPopoverSignOutProps {
  /** i18n key for the button label (default `userMenu.signOut`). */
  labelKey?: string
  /** Fallback label (default `Sign out`). */
  labelDefault?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` for the button (default `user-menu-sign-out`). */
  dataMolId?: string
  /** Extra classes for the button. */
  className?: string
}
```

#### `UserMenuProps`

Props for {@link UserMenu} (React's `UserMenuProps`, in Angular's form).
The panel content is projected content, so it has no member.

```typescript
interface UserMenuProps {
  /** i18n key for the trigger's aria-label (default `userMenu.open`). */
  ariaLabelKey?: string
  /** Fallback aria-label (default `Open user menu`). */
  ariaLabelDefault?: string
  /** Icon name for the trigger (default `user`). */
  triggerIcon?: IconName
  /** Pixel size for the trigger icon (default 20). */
  triggerIconSize?: number
  /** `data-mol-id` for the trigger (default `user-menu`). */
  dataMolId?: string
  /** Extra classes composed onto the trigger. */
  className?: string
  /** Whether the trigger is disabled. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** Overrides the trigger click (called instead of opening the panel). */
  onClick?: () => void
  /** Which side the panel opens from (default `right`). */
  side?: 'left' | 'right'
}
```

#### `DomContext`

One context: what React's `createContext` returns, in Angular's form.

```typescript
interface DomContext<T> {
  /**
   * Registers the value on a provider's host element.
   *
   * @param host - The provider component's host element.
   * @param value - The context value.
   */
  provide(host: Element, value: T): void
  /**
   * Removes the registration (on provider destroy).
   *
   * @param host - The provider component's host element.
   */
  remove(host: Element): void
  /**
   * Finds the nearest enclosing value for a consumer's element. The element's
   * own registration is ignored: a provider's children see the provider, the
   * provider does not see itself.
   *
   * @param start - The consumer's host element.
   * @returns The nearest registered value, or `undefined` when there is none.
   */
  find(start: Element | null): T | undefined
}
```

#### `HasElement`

A component that gives callers its ref element.

Implemented by every component whose React export forwards a ref. The
generic is the element React's `forwardRef<E>` names.

```typescript
interface HasElement<E extends Element> {
  /** The ref element, or `null` while it is not rendered. */
  readonly element: Signal<E | null>
}
```

#### `FormFieldContext`

The values `FormField` injects into its single child field.

```typescript
interface FormFieldContext {
  /** The field id (`FormField`'s `name`, or a generated id). */
  id?: string
  /** The id of the error/hint paragraph the field should describe itself with. */
  describedBy?: string
  /** Whether the field is invalid (`FormField` render an error). */
  invalid?: boolean
}
```

#### `OptimisticValueOptions`

Options for {@link createOptimisticValue}.

```typescript
interface OptimisticValueOptions<T> {
  /** The loaded server value. */
  value: T
  /** Saves the desired value; resolve with the confirmed value. */
  commit: OptimisticCommit<T>
  /** Equality override (defaults to {@link shallowEqual}). */
  equals?: (a: T, b: T) => boolean
  /** Called with a failed save after rollback. */
  onError?: (error: unknown, value: T) => void
}
```

#### `OptimisticValueState`

The externally visible state.

```typescript
interface OptimisticValueState<T> {
  /** The value to render. */
  value: T
  /** Whether a save is in flight. */
  pending: boolean
  /** The newest failure, when the newest save failed. */
  error: unknown
}
```

#### `OptimisticValue`

The controller.

```typescript
interface OptimisticValue<T> {
  /**
   * Gets the current state.
   * @returns The state snapshot.
   */
  getState(): OptimisticValueState<T>
  /**
   * Sets the desired value (starts or queues a save).
   * @param next - The desired value.
   */
  set(next: T): void
  /**
   * Updates the desired value from the current desired one.
   * @param change - Maps current to next.
   */
  update(change: (current: T) => T): void
  /**
   * Syncs a newer server value.
   * @param value - The confirmed server value.
   */
  sync(value: T): void
  /**
   * Subscribes to state changes.
   * @param listener - Called on every publish.
   * @returns Unsubscribe.
   */
  subscribe(listener: (state: OptimisticValueState<T>) => void): () => void
  /**
   * Resolves when the current save settles.
   * @returns The settled state.
   */
  settled(): Promise<OptimisticValueState<T>>
}
```

#### `ResolvedIcon`

The icon data plus the resolved class name, precomputed so the template
only binds (recomputing `getIcon` per CD pass would be wasteful and can
throw mid-template).

```typescript
interface ResolvedIcon {
  /** The bonded icon data. */
  icon: IconData
  /** Class for the `<svg>` (already joined, possibly empty). */
  className: string
  /** Whether the icon carries stroke styling. */
  stroked: boolean
  /** `0 0 20 20` fallback applied when the icon data has no viewBox. */
  viewBox: string
}
```

#### `ReapplyRestAttributesOptions`

Options for {@link reapplyRestAttributes}.

```typescript
interface ReapplyRestAttributesOptions {
  /**
   * Move `aria-describedby` too. The default keeps it on the host: a
   * component that MERGES the host's `aria-describedby` with its own target
   * (the input/select/textarea family, see below) has already applied it on
   * an inner binding, and re-moving the re-bound value would erase that
   * merge. A component that spreads `aria-describedby` straight onto its
   * `...rest` target has no merge to protect, so it opts in.
   */
  includeDescribedBy?: boolean
}
```

### Types

#### `Children`

Framework-agnostic child content.
Use `unknown` to allow any framework's node type (ReactNode, VNode, etc.).

```typescript
type Children = unknown
```

#### `EventHandler`

Framework-agnostic event handler. An unannotated handler's event is `E`. It is declared as a
method (as React types its own handlers), so TypeScript compares its parameter both ways: a
handler written for the event a kit passes, such as `(event: Event) => void` on the Vue, Solid,
Svelte and Angular kits or `(event: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void` on React's,
is accepted too.

```typescript
type EventHandler<E extends KitEvent = KitEvent> = {
  handle(event: E): void
}['handle']
```

- `event` — The event, as the kit hands it (see {@link KitEvent}).

#### `MouseEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic mouse event handler.

```typescript
type MouseEventHandler = EventHandler<KitMouseEvent>
```

#### `KeyboardEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic keyboard event handler.

```typescript
type KeyboardEventHandler = EventHandler<KitKeyboardEvent>
```

#### `FocusEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic focus event handler.

```typescript
type FocusEventHandler = EventHandler<KitFocusEvent>
```

#### `ChangeEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic change event handler.

```typescript
type ChangeEventHandler = EventHandler<KitEvent>
```

#### `FormEventHandler`

Framework-agnostic form event handler.

```typescript
type FormEventHandler = EventHandler<KitEvent>
```

#### `Size`

Standard size scale used across all molecule UI components (buttons, inputs, badges, etc.).

```typescript
type Size = 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl'
```

#### `ColorVariant`

Semantic color variants used across components for status indication.

```typescript
type ColorVariant = 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error' | 'info'
```

#### `ButtonVariant`

Button visual variant styles.

```typescript
type ButtonVariant = 'solid' | 'outline' | 'ghost' | 'link'
```

#### `InputType`

Allowed HTML input type attribute values for the Input component.

```typescript
type InputType =
  | 'text'
  | 'email'
  | 'password'
  | 'number'
  | 'tel'
  | 'url'
  | 'search'
  | 'date'
  | 'time'
  | 'datetime-local'
```

#### `ModalSize`

Modal size variants including full-screen.

```typescript
type ModalSize = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | 'full'
```

#### `TooltipPlacement`

Position where a tooltip renders relative to its trigger element (top, bottom, left, right, and corner variants).

```typescript
type TooltipPlacement =
  'top' | 'bottom' | 'left' | 'right' | 'top-start' | 'top-end' | 'bottom-start' | 'bottom-end'
```

#### `ShellSidebarWidth`

Semantic sidebar width — the same stack-agnostic presets React's
`ShellSidebarWidth` uses: `sm` → 208px, `md` → 240px (default), `lg` →
256px. A raw `number` is an exact pixel width.

```typescript
type ShellSidebarWidth = 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | number
```

#### `OptimisticCommit`

What a commit reports back.

```typescript
type OptimisticCommit<T> = (
  next: T,
  previous: T,
  confirm: (change: (current: T) => T) => void,
) => Promise<T | void>
```

### Classes

#### `Accordion`

Accordion component.

The host is the `div.accordionRoot` React renders. Items carry their
header/content as text or TemplateRef node values; expansion state is
internal unless the `value` input controls it (React's controlled /
uncontrolled pair), and ids are generated per item value so replacing the
`items` array keeps them stable (React's `useId` per item component).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-accordion]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg, NgTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'items',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'multiple',
    'collapsible',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'disabled',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `@for (item of items ?? []; track item.value) {
    <div [class]="cm().accordionItem" [attr.data-state]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'open' : 'closed'">
      <button
        type="button"
        [attr.id]="ids(item.value).trigger"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().accordion({ variant: 'default' }), cm().accordionTriggerBase)"
        (click)="toggle(item.value)"
        [attr.aria-expanded]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [attr.aria-controls]="ids(item.value).content"
        [attr.data-state]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'open' : 'closed'"
        [disabled]="item.disabled || disabled"
      >
        @if (asTemplate(item.header); as tpl) {
          <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="tpl" />
        } @else {
          {{ nodeText(item.header) }}
        }
        <svg mol-icon [data]="chevron()" />
      </button>
      <div
        [attr.id]="ids(item.value).content"
        role="region"
        [attr.aria-labelledby]="ids(item.value).trigger"
        [attr.data-state]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'open' : 'closed'"
        [class]="cm().accordionContent"
        [style.display]="isOpen(item.value) ? 'block' : 'none'"
      >
        <div [class]="cm().accordionContentInner">
          @if (asTemplate(item.content); as tpl) {
            <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="tpl" />
          } @else {
            {{ nodeText(item.content) }}
          }
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  }`,
})
export class Accordion implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** The items to render. */
  items?: AccordionItemData[]
  /** Controlled expanded value(s). */
  value?: string | string[]
  /** Uncontrolled initial expanded value(s). */
  defaultValue?: string | string[]
  /** Whether several items may stay expanded. */
  multiple?: boolean
  /** Whether the last expanded item may collapse. */
  collapsible?: boolean
  /** Extra classes joined onto the root classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the root. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string
  /** Disables every header, whatever an item says (React's `disabled`). */
  disabled?: boolean

  /** Template helpers (templates see only class members). */
  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  /** Renders a plain-string node value. */
  readonly nodeText = nodeText

  /** Fires the new expansion state (single value, or array in multiple mode). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<string | string[]>()

  private internalExpanded: string[] | null = null
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)
  private readonly idMap = new Map<string, { trigger: string; content: string }>()
  private idCount = 0

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().accordionRoot, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The chevron glyph every trigger renders.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  chevron(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('chevron-down', this.cm().accordionChevron)
  }

  /**
   * Normalizes a value prop to the expanded list (React's `normalizeValue`).
   *
   * @param v - The single value, array, or undefined.
   * @returns The expanded values.
   */
  private normalize(v: string | string[] | undefined): string[] {
    if (v === undefined) return []
    return Array.isArray(v) ? v : [v]
  }

  /**
   * The expanded items: the controlled `value` when given, else the internal
   * state (seeded from `defaultValue` at first use).
   * @returns The expanded values.
   */
  expandedItems(): string[] {
    if (this.value !== undefined) return this.normalize(this.value)
    if (this.internalExpanded === null) this.internalExpanded = this.normalize(this.defaultValue)
    return this.internalExpanded
  }

  /**
   * Whether one item is expanded.
   *
   * @param value - The item value.
   * @returns True when expanded.
   */
  isOpen(value: string): boolean {
    return this.expandedItems().includes(value)
  }

  /**
   * The stable per-item ids (React generates them per item component; keying
   * by item value keeps them stable across an `items` replacement).
   *
   * @param value - The item value.
   * @returns The trigger and content ids.
   */
  ids(value: string): { trigger: string; content: string } {
    let ids = this.idMap.get(value)
    if (!ids) {
      ids = { trigger: `t${this.idCount}`, content: `c${this.idCount}` }
      this.idCount++
      this.idMap.set(value, ids)
    }
    return ids
  }

  /**
   * React's `toggleItem`: collapse guards `collapsible`, expand replaces or
   * appends per `multiple`, internal state updates only when uncontrolled,
   * and `onChange` receives the new state (single value or array).
   *
   * @param itemValue - The toggled item's value.
   */
  toggle(itemValue: string): void {
    const current = this.expandedItems()
    let next: string[]
    if (current.includes(itemValue)) {
      if (this.collapsible === false && current.length === 1) return
      next = current.filter((v) => v !== itemValue)
    } else {
      next = this.multiple ? [...current, itemValue] : [itemValue]
    }
    if (this.value === undefined) this.internalExpanded = next
    if (this.multiple) this.change.emit(next)
    else this.change.emit(next[0] ?? '')
  }
}
```

#### `Alert`

Alert component.

`role` follows `live` (assertive `alert` by default, polite `status` for a
banner that is part of the page's normal content).

React renders `{(icon || defaultIcon) && <span className={alertIconWrapper}>…}`:
the icon wrapper span exists only when a custom icon is given or the status
has a default glyph, and its content is the custom icon XOR the default
glyph. Here the span and the default glyph both always exist in the
template (projection capture points) and are taken out of the DOM when
React would not render them (`setElementPresent`); the decision is made in
the placement function, where projected content is current.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-alert]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: [
    'title',
    'status',
    'variant',
    'live',
    'dismissible',
    'dismissLabel',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  outputs: ['dismiss'],
  host: {
    // `title` is this component's heading, which React renders as text; Angular would also keep a
    // static `title="…"` on the host, where the browser shows it as a tooltip.
    '[attr.title]': 'null',
    '[attr.role]': 'live ? "alert" : "status"',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `<span [class]="cm().alertIconWrapper" #iconSlot>
      <svg mol-icon [data]="resolvedDefaultIcon()" #defaultGlyph />
      <ng-content select="[slot=icon]"></ng-content>
    </span>
    <div [class]="cm().alertContent">
      @if (title) {
        <h5 [class]="cm().alertTitle">{{ title }}</h5>
      }
      <div [class]="cm().alertDescription"><ng-content></ng-content></div>
    </div>
    @if (dismissible) {
      <button
        type="button"
        [class]="cm().alertDismiss"
        [attr.aria-label]="dismissText()"
        (click)="dismiss.emit($event)"
      >
        <svg mol-icon [data]="closeIcon()" />
      </button>
    }`,
})
export class Alert implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** Alert title. */
  title?: string
  /** Status color. */
  status: ColorVariant = 'info'
  /** Visual variant. */
  variant?: 'solid' | 'subtle' | 'outline' | 'left-accent'
  /** Whether the alert announces assertively (default true). */
  live = true
  /** Whether a dismiss button renders. */
  dismissible?: boolean
  /** Accessible label for the dismiss button. */
  dismissLabel?: string
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /** Fires the raw click event when the dismiss button is pressed. */
  @Output() readonly dismiss = new EventEmitter<MouseEvent>()

  /** The icon wrapper span (projection capture point). */
  @ViewChild('iconSlot') iconSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The default status glyph (detached when a custom icon is projected). */
  @ViewChild('defaultGlyph', { read: ElementRef }) defaultGlyph?: ElementRef<SVGSVGElement>

  private hasCustomIcon = false

  /** Places the icon wrapper and the default glyph as React renders them. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.syncIcon(hasContent))
  }

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The class-map variant for the status.
   * @returns The mapped variant.
   */
  get alertVariant(): 'default' | 'info' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error' {
    return STATUS_VARIANT[this.status] || 'default'
  }

  /**
   * The default status glyph (none for the `default` variant, matching
   * React's `statusIconMap` gap).
   * @returns The resolved icon, or null when the variant has no glyph.
   */
  defaultIcon(): ResolvedIcon | null {
    const name = STATUS_ICON[this.alertVariant]
    return name ? resolveIcon(name, this.cm().iconMd) : null
  }

  /**
   * A non-null glyph to bind (an empty resolved icon when the variant has
   * none; that svg is detached from the DOM anyway).
   * @returns The resolved icon data.
   */
  resolvedDefaultIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return (
      this.defaultIcon() ?? {
        icon: { paths: [] },
        className: '',
        stroked: false,
        viewBox: '0 0 20 20',
      }
    )
  }

  /**
   * The dismiss button's X glyph.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  closeIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconSm)
  }

  /**
   * The dismiss button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  dismissText(): string {
    return (
      this.dismissLabel ?? this.i18n.t('ui.alert.dismiss', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Dismiss' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the alert class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().alert({ variant: this.alertVariant }),
      this.variant === 'left-accent' && this.cm().alertLeftAccent({ variant: this.alertVariant }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{(icon || defaultIcon) && <span>{icon || defaultIcon}</span>}`.
   *
   * @param hasContent - Reads whether a slot has content.
   */
  private syncIcon(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    const span = this.iconSlot?.nativeElement
    const glyph = this.defaultGlyph?.nativeElement
    // The capture holds the always-mounted default glyph first, then the slot.
    this.hasCustomIcon = hasContent(span, 1)
    setElementPresent(glyph, !this.hasCustomIcon && this.defaultIcon() !== null)
    setElementPresent(span, this.hasCustomIcon || this.defaultIcon() !== null)
  }
}
```

#### `AuthGuard`

Route-element guard for authenticated sections of a routing tree.

While the auth status is `'loading'` it renders the `loadingFallback` input
(or its `[slot=loadingFallback]` projection, or the default
`data-mol-id="auth-guard-loading"` tag); while
`'unauthenticated'` off the login path it renders nothing and redirects to
`loginPath` (state `from` carries the attempted location); otherwise it
renders the projected children, or `<mol-routed-view />` without them (the
matched child route, through whichever routing bond the app wired; it throws
when the bond has none: project the page then). React's root is a fragment
(or nothing at all while redirecting), so everything the host holds renders
in front of the host and the host itself leaves the page. On the login path
itself the guard renders its children, so a guard wrapping the whole route
tree does not redirect in a loop.

The projected children (and the loading fallback's content) need one stable
capture point, so each lives in an always-mounted wrapper whose content is
rendered in its own place and whose element leaves the page: React renders
them bare, never inside a wrapper element.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-auth-guard',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolTemplateOutlet, RoutedView],
  inputs: ['loadingFallback', 'loadingKey', 'loadingDefault', 'loginPath', 'onAuthenticated'],
  template: `<div data-mol-id="auth-guard-loading" #defaultLoading>{{ loadingText() }}</div>
    <span #dataFallback
      ><ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(loadingFallback)"></ng-container
      >{{ nodeText(loadingFallback) }}</span
    >
    <span #fallbackWrap><ng-content select="[slot=loadingFallback]"></ng-content></span>
    <span #childrenWrap><ng-content></ng-content></span>
    @if (routed()) {
      <mol-routed-view />
    }`,
})
export class AuthGuard {
  /**
   * Element rendered while the auth status is `loading` (a node value, or the
   * projected `[slot=loadingFallback]`). Overrides the default loading tag.
   */
  loadingFallback?: AuthGuardProps['loadingFallback']
  /**
   * i18n key for the default loading-tag text (default `common.loading`).
   * Ignored when a `loadingFallback` is given.
   */
  loadingKey?: AuthGuardProps['loadingKey']
  /** Fallback text if the i18n key is missing (default `Loading...`). */
  loadingDefault?: AuthGuardProps['loadingDefault']
  /** Path to redirect to when unauthenticated (default `/login`). */
  loginPath?: AuthGuardProps['loginPath']
  /** Fired when `isAuthenticated` becomes true (also when true at mount). */
  onAuthenticated?: AuthGuardProps['onAuthenticated']

  /** Template helpers (templates see only class members). */
  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  /** Renders a plain-string/number node value. */
  readonly nodeText = nodeText

  /** The default loading tag. */
  @ViewChild('defaultLoading') defaultLoading?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The node-valued fallback (shown when the input is given). */
  @ViewChild('dataFallback') dataFallback?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The `loadingFallback` slot capture (its content renders in its own place). */
  @ViewChild('fallbackWrap') fallbackWrap?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The children capture (their content renders in its own place). */
  @ViewChild('childrenWrap') childrenWrap?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  /** Auth state, read in the component's injection context. */
  readonly auth = useAuth()
  /** Router state and actions, read in the component's injection context. */
  readonly router = useRouter()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)
  private hasFallback = false
  /** Whether the guard was given children to project (read in the placement function). */
  private hasChildren = false
  /** Whether the view queries have resolved, so the children capture can be read. */
  private viewReady = false
  /**
   * Whether the routed view renders: the children branch with no projected children. A signal
   * the template reads, so the routed view is created only then (it throws without one).
   */
  readonly routed = signal(false)
  private lastAuthenticated = false
  /** The login path the guard navigated to for the current redirect state. */
  private redirectedTo: string | null = null

  /**
   * Re-evaluates the guard whenever the auth status, the location or the
   * projected slots change. The placement function reads both signals, so its
   * effect tracks them even though no template binding does.
   */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.reconcile(hasContent))
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's branch for the current auth status and location, fires
   * `onAuthenticated` on its rising edge, and redirects once per entry into
   * the redirect state.
   *
   * @param hasContent - Reads whether a slot has content.
   */
  private reconcile(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.hasFallback = hasContent(this.fallbackWrap?.nativeElement)
    this.hasChildren = hasContent(this.childrenWrap?.nativeElement)
    this.viewReady = this.childrenWrap !== undefined
    const status = this.auth.status()
    const authenticated = this.auth.isAuthenticated()
    if (this.onAuthenticated && authenticated && !this.lastAuthenticated) this.onAuthenticated()
    this.lastAuthenticated = authenticated

    const pathname = this.router.location().pathname
    const target = this.loginPath ?? '/login'
    const redirecting = status === 'unauthenticated' && pathname !== loginPathname(target)
    if (redirecting) {
      if (this.redirectedTo !== target) {
        this.redirectedTo = target
        this.router.navigate(target, {
          replace: true,
          state: { from: { ...this.router.location() } },
        })
      }
    } else {
      this.redirectedTo = null
    }

    const branch = status === 'loading' ? 'loading' : redirecting ? 'redirect' : 'children'
    this.renderBranch(branch)
  }

  /**
   * Shows exactly one branch's content, the way React's early returns do.
   *
   * React's branch elements render in front of the host, which leaves the
   * page; each capture that holds projected content renders that content in its
   * own place and leaves the page, so the projected nodes are bare, as React
   * renders them.
   *
   * @param branch - The branch to render.
   */
  private renderBranch(branch: 'loading' | 'redirect' | 'children'): void {
    const host = this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement
    // React's root is a fragment (or nothing): everything the host holds renders in front of it.
    placeContentBefore(host, host)
    setElementPresent(host, false)
    this.routed.set(this.viewReady && branch === 'children' && !this.hasChildren)
    const loading = branch === 'loading'
    const hasNode = isGiven(this.loadingFallback)
    this.placeSlot(this.childrenWrap?.nativeElement, branch === 'children')
    this.placeSlot(this.fallbackWrap?.nativeElement, loading && !hasNode && this.hasFallback)
    this.placeSlot(this.dataFallback?.nativeElement, loading && hasNode)
    setElementPresent(this.defaultLoading?.nativeElement, loading && !hasNode && !this.hasFallback)
  }

  /**
   * Renders a capture's content in the capture's own place while `shown`, and
   * leaves the capture element itself out of the page either way: React
   * renders the content bare, never inside the capture's span.
   *
   * @param capture - The capture element (its own template content, projected or not).
   * @param shown - Whether React would render that content now.
   */
  private placeSlot(capture: HTMLElement | undefined, shown: boolean): void {
    if (!capture) return
    if (shown) placeContentBefore(capture, capture)
    else placeContent(capture, null)
    setElementPresent(capture, false)
  }

  /**
   * The localized default loading text.
   * @returns The translated text.
   */
  loadingText(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.loadingKey ?? 'common.loading',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.loadingDefault ?? 'Loading...',
      },
    )
  }
}
```

#### `Avatar`

Avatar component.

Renders the image while it loads; an image error (or no `src`) swaps to the
fallback: projected `[slot=fallback]` content, the name's initials, or a
user glyph. The image/fallback swap is a signal-driven `@if` (an error
re-renders); the three fallback variants are always-mounted capture points
taken out of the DOM per React's ternary, since projected-content presence
is read in the placement function (`setElementPresent`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-avatar]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: [
    'src',
    'alt',
    'name',
    'size',
    'rounded',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `@if (!showFallback()) {
      <img
        [src]="src"
        [attr.alt]="altText()"
        [class]="cm().avatarImage"
        (error)="imageError.set(true)"
      />
    } @else {
      <div [class]="cm().avatarFallback" #fallbackEl>
        <span [class]="cm().avatarInitials" #initialsEl>{{ initials() }}</span>
        <svg mol-icon [data]="userIcon()" #userGlyph />
        <ng-content select="[slot=fallback]"></ng-content>
      </div>
    }`,
})
export class Avatar implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** Image source. */
  src?: string
  /** Image alt text. */
  alt?: string
  /** Display name (drives the initials fallback and default alt). */
  name?: string
  /** Avatar size preset or an exact pixel size. */
  size: 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | number = 'md'
  /** Whether the avatar is fully rounded (default true). */
  rounded = true
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /** Set when the image fails to load. */
  readonly imageError = signal(false)

  /** The fallback container (React renders it only on the fallback branch). */
  @ViewChild('fallbackEl') fallbackEl?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The initials span (detached unless the fallback branch picks it). */
  @ViewChild('initialsEl') initialsEl?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The user glyph (detached unless the fallback branch picks it). */
  @ViewChild('userGlyph', { read: ElementRef }) userGlyph?: ElementRef<SVGSVGElement>

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Applies the fallback ternary; projected content wins over initials and glyph. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.reconcile(hasContent))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Whether the fallback branch renders.
   * @returns True without a src or after an image error.
   */
  showFallback(): boolean {
    return !this.src || this.imageError()
  }

  /**
   * The image alt text (name or the localized default).
   * @returns The alt text.
   */
  altText(): string {
    return (
      this.alt || this.name || this.i18n.t('ui.avatar.alt', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Avatar' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The initials for the given name.
   * @returns Up to two uppercase characters.
   */
  initials(): string {
    return getInitials(this.name)
  }

  /**
   * The fallback user glyph.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  userIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('user', this.cm().avatarFallbackIcon)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the avatar class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const avatarSize = typeof this.size === 'number' ? undefined : this.size
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().avatar({ size: avatarSize }),
      !this.rounded && this.cm().avatarSquare,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style (an exact pixel size inline).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const customSize =
      typeof this.size === 'number' ? { width: this.size, height: this.size } : undefined
    return styleText({ ...this.style, ...customSize })
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's fallback ternary (`fallback ? fallback : name ? initials
   * : glyph`). The capture holds the initials span and the user glyph first,
   * then the projected fallback.
   *
   * @param hasContent - Reads whether a slot has content.
   */
  private reconcile(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    const fallbackHost = this.fallbackEl?.nativeElement
    if (!fallbackHost) return
    const projected = hasContent(fallbackHost, 2)
    setElementPresent(this.initialsEl?.nativeElement, !projected && !!this.name)
    setElementPresent(this.userGlyph?.nativeElement, !projected && !this.name)
  }
}
```

#### `Badge`

Badge component.

The host element is the `<span>` React renders (`span[mol-badge]`); the
color variant maps to `badge({ variant })` exactly as React's `Badge`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'span[mol-badge]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'color',
    'variant',
    'size',
    'rounded',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Badge implements HasElement<HTMLSpanElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<span>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Named color variant (`primary`, `success`, …). */
  color: ColorVariant = 'primary'
  /** Visual style (kept for React prop parity; the class map keys off `color`). */
  variant: 'solid' | 'outline' | 'subtle' = 'solid'
  /** Badge size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Whether the badge is fully rounded (default) or square. */
  rounded = true
  /** Extra classes joined onto the badge classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the badge. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the badge class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().badge({ variant: this.color, size: this.size }),
      !this.rounded && this.cm().badgeSquare,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }
}
```

#### `SlotWrapper` _(deprecated)_

> **Deprecated:** Use setElementPresent from `@molecule/app-angular`.

Attaches or detaches an always-mounted projection wrapper element from its
parent. React renders `{cond && <span>{slot}</span>}` — nothing when the
condition is false. Angular needs the `<ng-content>` capture point to stay
mounted, so the wrapper span always exists in the template and this helper
removes it from the DOM whenever React would not render it.

It is a thin delegate to `setElementPresent`, kept for callers' signatures.

```typescript
class SlotWrapper {
  /**
   * Creates the wrapper helper.
   *
   * @param _host - Kept for callers' constructor signature.
   * @param _renderer - Kept for callers' constructor signature.
   */
  constructor(_host: ElementRef<HTMLElement>, _renderer: Renderer2) {}

  /**
   * Attaches or detaches the element.
   *
   * @param el - The wrapper element.
   * @param shouldAttach - Whether it should be in the DOM.
   * @param _parent - Ignored; the element's own place holds it.
   */
  sync(el: Element | undefined | null, shouldAttach: boolean, _parent?: Node): void {
    setElementPresent(el, shouldAttach)
  }
}
```

#### `Button`

Button component.

While `loading` is true, the native button is disabled and marked busy.
The host element is the `<button>` itself (`button[mol-button]`), matching
React's root exactly. Icon slots are projected as `[slot=leftIcon]` /
`[slot=rightIcon]`; each wrapping `<span>` renders only while the slot
carries nodes AND the button is not loading — React's
`{!loading && !!leftIcon && <span>…}` — implemented by detaching the
wrapper from the DOM (the projected nodes must have an always-mounted
capture point, so the wrapper lives in the template unconditionally).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-button]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [Spinner],
  inputs: [
    'variant',
    'color',
    'size',
    'loading',
    'loadingText',
    'fullWidth',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'type',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
    '[attr.type]': 'type',
    '[disabled]': 'disabled || loading',
    '[attr.aria-busy]': 'loading ? "true" : "false"',
  },
  template: `@if (loading) {
      <div mol-spinner [size]="'sm'" [className]="cm().buttonIconLeft"></div>
    }
    <span [class]="cm().buttonIconLeft" #leftSlot
      ><ng-content select="[slot=leftIcon]"></ng-content
    ></span>
    @if (loading && loadingText) {
      <ng-container>{{ loadingText }}</ng-container>
    } @else {
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    }
    <span [class]="cm().buttonIconRight" #rightSlot
      ><ng-content select="[slot=rightIcon]"></ng-content
    ></span>`,
})
export class Button implements HasElement<HTMLButtonElement> {
  /** Button variant. */
  variant: ButtonVariant = 'solid'
  /** Button color. */
  color: ColorVariant = 'primary'
  /** Button size. */
  size: Size = 'md'
  /** Whether the button is in its loading (busy) state. */
  loading = false
  /** Text shown instead of children while loading. */
  loadingText?: string
  /** Whether the button stretches to fill its container. */
  fullWidth = false
  /** Extra classes joined onto the button classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the button. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /**
   * A `data-mol-id` passed as a plain rest attribute (React spreads it onto
   * the root); `automationId` wins when both are given.
   */
  dataMolId?: string
  /** Disables the button. */
  disabled = false
  /** The button's `type` attribute. */
  type: 'button' | 'submit' | 'reset' = 'button'

  /** The left icon wrapper (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('leftSlot') leftSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The right icon wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('rightSlot') rightSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<button>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value: `automationId` when given, else a caller's own
   * `data-mol-id` (the `dataMolId` prop or a `[attr.data-mol-id]` binding on
   * the element), matching React's `rootProps` precedence.
   * @returns The attribute value, or undefined.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the button class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().button({
        variant: this.variant,
        color: this.color,
        size: this.size,
        fullWidth: this.fullWidth,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  private hasLeft = false
  private hasRight = false

  /** Detaches the icon wrappers React would not render. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.syncWrappers(hasContent))
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{!loading && !!icon}` wrapper conditions.
   *
   * @param hasContent - Reads whether a slot has content.
   */
  private syncWrappers(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    const left = this.leftSlot?.nativeElement
    const right = this.rightSlot?.nativeElement
    this.hasLeft = left ? hasContent(left) : false
    this.hasRight = right ? hasContent(right) : false
    setElementPresent(left, this.hasLeft && !this.loading)
    setElementPresent(right, this.hasRight && !this.loading)
  }
}
```

#### `Card`

Card component.

An interactive card renders `role="button"` and joins the keyboard tab
order; rest props (`id`, `title`, `aria-*`, `data-*`) and event bindings
(`(focusin)`, `(dblclick)`, `(keydown)`, …) land on the host, and a
caller's `(keydown)` runs before the Enter/Space activation.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-card]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'variant',
    'interactive',
    'padding',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'disabled',
    'onClick',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
    '[attr.role]': 'interactive ? "button" : null',
    '[attr.tabindex]': 'interactive ? (disabled ? -1 : 0) : null',
    '[attr.aria-disabled]': 'interactive && disabled ? "true" : null',
    '(click)': 'handleClick($event)',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Card implements OnInit, HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)
  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly destroyRef = inject(DestroyRef)

  /** Card variant. */
  variant: 'default' | 'elevated' | 'outlined' | 'filled' | 'ghost' = 'elevated'
  /** Whether the card is keyboard-interactive. */
  interactive?: boolean
  /** Padding preset (`none` for no padding class). */
  padding: 'none' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' = 'md'
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string
  /** A disabled card never calls `onClick` (React's `disabled`). */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** React's `onClick` prop: the card's click handler (never called while disabled). */
  onClick?: MouseEventHandler

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the card class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const paddingClass = this.padding === 'none' ? '' : this.cm().cardPadding(this.padding)
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().card({ variant: VARIANT_MAP[this.variant] || 'default' }),
      paddingClass,
      this.interactive && this.cm().cardInteractive,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Attaches the keyboard activation. A host listener would run before the
   * `(keydown)` a caller binds on the card (Angular registers a component's
   * host listeners first); attached here, after the template's bindings, the
   * caller's handler runs first and its preventDefault() cancels the
   * activation, as React's Card calls `onKeyDown` before activating.
   */
  ngOnInit(): void {
    const unlisten = this.renderer.listen(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      'keydown',
      (event: KeyboardEvent) => this.handleKeyDown(event),
    )
    this.destroyRef.onDestroy(unlisten)
  }

  /**
   * An interactive card is announced as a button and sits in the tab order, so
   * Enter and Space activate it the way they activate a native button: by
   * clicking it, which runs the consumer's click handler with a real click
   * event. Keys pressed in a control inside the card belong to that control,
   * and a card that is not interactive ignores keys.
   *
   * @param event - The key event.
   */
  handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if (event.defaultPrevented || !this.interactive || this.disabled) return
    // The element the listener is bound to (the host, whether the binding is
    // the component's own or a caller's); `this.host` is the fallback when the
    // handler is invoked outside a dispatch.
    const target =
      event.currentTarget instanceof HTMLElement ? event.currentTarget : this.host.nativeElement
    if (event.target !== target) return
    if (event.key !== 'Enter' && event.key !== ' ') return
    event.preventDefault()
    target.click()
  }

  /**
   * React passes no `onClick` to a disabled card, so the consumer's handler
   * never runs; an enabled card's handler is called with the click event.
   *
   * @param event - The click event.
   */
  handleClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    if (this.disabled) return
    this.onClick?.(event)
  }
}
```
