#### `Checkbox`

Checkbox component.

The host is the `div.formFieldWrapper` React renders. The error message is
programmatically associated with the input through `aria-describedby`;
the input is React's `...rest` spread target, so rest attributes move
there at view init.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-checkbox]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolControlledChecked],
  inputs: [
    'label',
    'checked',
    'indeterminate',
    'size',
    'error',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'name',
    'id',
    'defaultChecked',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'onClick',
    'onFocus',
    'onBlur',
    'onKeyDown',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().formFieldWrapper',
  },
  template: `<label [class]="cm().controlLabel">
      <input
        type="checkbox"
        [attr.id]="checkboxId()"
        [attr.name]="name"
        [molChecked]="checked"
        [molDefaultChecked]="defaultChecked"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.data-state]="checked ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'"
        [attr.aria-invalid]="error || fieldContext()?.invalid ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [attr.aria-describedby]="mergedDescribedBy()"
        [class]="classes()"
        [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        [attr.data-mol-id]="molId()"
        (click)="handleClick($event)"
        (change)="handleChange($event)"
        (focusin)="onFocus?.($event)"
        (focusout)="onBlur?.($event)"
        (keydown)="onKeyDown?.($event)"
        #input
      />
      <span [class]="cm().cn(cm().controlText, disabled && cm().controlDisabled)" #labelSlot
        >{{ label }}<ng-content select="[slot=label]"></ng-content
      ></span>
    </label>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="errorId()" [class]="cm().cn(cm().formError, cm().sp('mt', 1))">{{ error }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class Checkbox implements AfterViewChecked, HasElement<HTMLInputElement> {
  /** Checkbox label (text, or projected `[slot=label]` nodes). */
  label?: unknown
  /** Whether the checkbox is checked. */
  checked?: boolean
  /** Whether the checkbox renders the indeterminate state. */
  indeterminate?: boolean
  /** Checkbox size (kept for prop parity). */
  size?: unknown
  /** Error message (renders and associates via aria-describedby). */
  error?: string
  /** Extra classes joined onto the checkbox classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the input. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the checkbox. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** Form field name. */
  name?: string
  /** Explicit id (defaults to the name). */
  id?: string
  /** Uncontrolled initial checked state (renders as the checked attribute). */
  defaultChecked?: boolean
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /** A caller's click handler (React's `onClick`, which React spreads onto the input). */
  onClick?: MouseEventHandler

  /** React's `onFocus`, which React spreads onto the <input>: its `currentTarget` is that element. */
  onFocus?: FocusEventHandler
  /** React's `onBlur`, which React spreads onto the <input>: its `currentTarget` is that element. */
  onBlur?: FocusEventHandler
  /** React's `onKeyDown`, which React spreads onto the <input>: its `currentTarget` is that element. */
  onKeyDown?: KeyboardEventHandler

  /** The genuine native `change` event. */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /** The native input (React's `...rest` spread target). */
  private input?: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>
  /** The native input view query; also feeds the caller's ref element. */
  @ViewChild('input')
  set inputQuery(ref: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement> | undefined) {
    this.input = ref
    this.inner.set(ref?.nativeElement ?? null)
  }

  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLInputElement | null>(null)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the native `<input>`. */
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()
  /** The label text wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('labelSlot') labelSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)

  /** The per-instance id used when the caller gives no `id` or `name` (React's `useId`). */
  private readonly generatedId = nextInstanceId()
  /** The `aria-describedby` the caller spread onto the host (React's rest). */
  private restDescribedBy: string | null = null
  /** Whether the rest-describedby value was captured. */
  private restDescribedByCaptured = false
  /** The `data-mol-id` a caller bound on the host (React spreads it onto the input). */
  private externalMolId: string | undefined
  private hasLabel = false
  private syncIndeterminate = false

  /** Applies React's `{!!label && <span>}` label condition. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => {
      this.hasLabel = !!this.label || hasContent(this.labelSlot?.nativeElement)
      setElementPresent(this.labelSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasLabel)
    })
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /** The id of the label-side element (`id` or `name`). */
  checkboxId(): string | undefined {
    return this.id || this.fieldContext()?.id || this.name
  }

  /** The error message id (a stable generated id, renumbered consistently by the DOM normalizer like React's `useId`). */
  errorId(): string {
    return `${this.checkboxId() ?? this.generatedId}-error`
  }

  /** The nearest enclosing `FormField` context (React's cloned props). */
  fieldContext(): FormFieldContext | null {
    return fieldContext(this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement)
  }

  /** The final `aria-describedby`: the enclosing FormField's error/hint target, then this checkbox's own error (React's cloneElement join). */
  mergedDescribedBy(): string | null {
    return (
      this.fieldContext()?.describedBy ??
      this.restDescribedBy ??
      (this.error ? this.errorId() : null)
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the checkbox class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().checkbox({ error: !!this.error }), this.className)
  }

  /** Serializes the inline style for the input. */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /** The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`). */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return this.automationId ?? this.dataMolId ?? this.externalMolId
  }

  /** React's checkbox `onChange` fires on the checkbox's CLICK event (React's change-event plugin maps checkbox/radio changes to the click), so the click event obje… */
  handleClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    this.onClick?.(event)
    this.justClicked = true
    this.change.emit(event)
    queueMicrotask(() => (this.justClicked = false))
  }

  /** A native change that did not come from this tick's click (a synthetic `dispatchEvent`) is forwarded like React would; the event never reaches the host, where t… */
  handleChange(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    if (!this.justClicked) this.change.emit(event)
    if (this.checked !== undefined && this.input) this.input.nativeElement.checked = !!this.checked
  }

  private justClicked = false

  /**
   * Applies the indeterminate DOM flag (a property, not an attribute).
   */
  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    const input = this.input?.nativeElement
    if (input) {
      if (!this.restDescribedByCaptured) {
        this.restDescribedBy = this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('aria-describedby')
        this.restDescribedByCaptured = true
      }
      adoptHostDataMolId(
        this.host.nativeElement,
        input,
        (id) => {
          this.externalMolId = id
        },
        () => this.molId(),
      )
      moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, input)
      if (this.syncIndeterminate !== !!this.indeterminate) {
        input.indeterminate = !!this.indeterminate
        this.syncIndeterminate = !!this.indeterminate
      }
    }
  }
}
```

_(The 9 longest member comments are shortened to their first sentence to fit; the full declaration is in `node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/checkbox.component.d.ts`.)_

#### `ConfirmButton`

ConfirmButton component.

The host is the `<button>` itself (`button[mol-confirm-button]`). Each label
is a node-valued input (`label`, `confirmLabel`, `pendingLabel`) or the
matching projected slot (`[slot=label]`, `[slot=confirmLabel]`,
`[slot=pendingLabel]`); each capture span stays detached from the DOM and
the visible phase's projected children are moved onto the button — React
renders exactly one label and no wrapper element. When neither the input nor
the slot for the armed/pending phase is given, the localized default text
renders instead (React's `confirmLabel ?? t(…)`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-confirm-button]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'variant',
    'color',
    'size',
    'onConfirm',
    'label',
    'confirmLabel',
    'pendingLabel',
    'disarmSeconds',
    'disabled',
    'testId',
    'className',
    'style',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[attr.type]': '"button"',
    '(click)': 'handleClick()',
    '(document:keydown.escape)': 'handleEscape()',
    '[disabled]': 'disabled || phase() === "pending"',
    '[attr.aria-live]': '"polite"',
    '[attr.data-state]': 'phase()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
  },
  template: `@if (phase() === 'armed' && !hasConfirmLabel() && !isGiven(confirmLabel)) {
      Confirm?
    }
    @if (phase() === 'pending' && !hasPendingLabel() && !isGiven(pendingLabel)) {
      Working…
    }
    @if (phase() === 'idle' && isGiven(label)) {
      <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(label)"></ng-container>{{ nodeText(label) }}
    }
    @if (phase() === 'armed' && isGiven(confirmLabel)) {
      <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(confirmLabel)"></ng-container
      >{{ nodeText(confirmLabel) }}
    }
    @if (phase() === 'pending' && isGiven(pendingLabel)) {
      <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(pendingLabel)"></ng-container
      >{{ nodeText(pendingLabel) }}
    }
    <span #labelSlot><ng-content select="[slot=label]"></ng-content></span
    ><span #confirmSlot><ng-content select="[slot=confirmLabel]"></ng-content></span
    ><span #pendingSlot><ng-content select="[slot=pendingLabel]"></ng-content></span>`,
})
export class ConfirmButton implements OnDestroy, HasElement<HTMLButtonElement> {
  /** Button variant (passed through to the button classes). */
  variant?: ConfirmButtonProps['variant']
  /** Button color; the armed state always shows the error color. */
  color?: ConfirmButtonProps['color']
  /** Button size. */
  size?: ConfirmButtonProps['size']
  /** The confirm callback (React's `onConfirm` prop). */
  onConfirm?: ConfirmButtonProps['onConfirm']
  /** Visible label in the idle state (React's `label` node value). */
  label?: ConfirmButtonProps['label']
  /** Visible label in the armed state (React's `confirmLabel` node value). */
  confirmLabel?: ConfirmButtonProps['confirmLabel']
  /** Visible label while the confirm callback is pending. */
  pendingLabel?: ConfirmButtonProps['pendingLabel']
  /** Seconds before the armed state auto-disarms (`0` disables auto-disarm). */
  disarmSeconds?: ConfirmButtonProps['disarmSeconds'] = 4
  /** Disables the control entirely (idle and confirming states). */
  disabled?: ConfirmButtonProps['disabled'] = false
  /** `data-testid` for the button. */
  testId?: ConfirmButtonProps['testId']
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: ConfirmButtonProps['automationId']
  dataMolId?: string
  /** Extra classes joined onto the button classes. */
  className?: ConfirmButtonProps['className']
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: ConfirmButtonProps['style']

  /** Template helpers (templates see only class members). */
  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  /** Renders a plain-string/number node value. */
  readonly nodeText = nodeText
  /** Whether a node-valued prop was given. */
  readonly isGiven = isGiven

  /** The label slot capture span (kept out of the DOM). */
  @ViewChild('labelSlot') labelSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The armed-state label capture span. */
  @ViewChild('confirmSlot') confirmSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The pending-state label capture span. */
  @ViewChild('pendingSlot') pendingSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  /** Whether the armed label slot carries content (a signal the template reads). */
  readonly hasConfirmLabel = signal(false)
  /** Whether the pending label slot carries content (a signal the template reads). */
  readonly hasPendingLabel = signal(false)

  /** The current phase: idle → armed → pending → idle. */
  readonly phase = signal<'idle' | 'armed' | 'pending'>('idle')

  private readonly el: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<button>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.el)
  private disarmTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null

  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.syncLabels(hasContent))
    effect(() => {
      if (this.disarmTimer) {
        clearTimeout(this.disarmTimer)
        this.disarmTimer = null
      }
      const disarmSeconds = this.disarmSeconds ?? 4
      if (this.phase() === 'armed' && disarmSeconds > 0) {
        this.disarmTimer = setTimeout(() => {
          this.disarmTimer = null
          this.phase.set('idle')
        }, disarmSeconds * 1000)
      }
    })
  }

  /** The current class map. */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /** Computes the button class list (the armed state shows the error color). */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().button({
        color: this.phase() === 'armed' ? 'error' : (this.color ?? 'primary'),
        size: this.size,
        variant: this.variant,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /** Serializes the inline style. */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /** The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`). */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.el.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  private async handleClick(): Promise<void> {
    const current = this.phase()
    if (current === 'idle') {
      this.phase.set('armed')
      return
    }
    if (current === 'pending') return
    this.phase.set('pending')
    try {
      await this.onConfirm?.()
    } catch (_error) {
      // A rejected confirm means the action failed: the caller owns that
      // error and shows its own message, and the button returns to idle so
      // the user can retry. Ignoring it here only stops it becoming an
      // unhandled rejection.
    } finally {
      this.phase.set('idle')
    }
  }

  private handleEscape(): void {
    if (this.phase() === 'armed') this.disarm()
  }

  /** React's `disarm`: cancels the timer and returns to idle. */
  private disarm(): void {
    if (this.disarmTimer) {
      clearTimeout(this.disarmTimer)
      this.disarmTimer = null
    }
    this.phase.set('idle')
  }

  /** The slot whose content is currently shown on the button. */
  private visibleFrom: HTMLSpanElement | null = null

  private syncLabels(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.hasConfirmLabel.set(hasContent(this.confirmSlot?.nativeElement))
    this.hasPendingLabel.set(hasContent(this.pendingSlot?.nativeElement))
    for (const span of [this.labelSlot, this.confirmSlot, this.pendingSlot])
      setElementPresent(span?.nativeElement, false)
    const phase = this.phase()
    const target =
      phase === 'idle'
        ? (this.labelSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
        : phase === 'armed' && this.hasConfirmLabel()
          ? (this.confirmSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
          : phase === 'pending' && this.hasPendingLabel()
            ? (this.pendingSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
            : null
    this.showSlot(target)
  }

  private showSlot(span: HTMLSpanElement | null): void {
    const host = this.el.nativeElement
    if (span === this.visibleFrom) return
    if (this.visibleFrom) placeContent(this.visibleFrom, null)
    this.visibleFrom = span
    if (!span) return
    placeContent(span, host)
  }

  /** Clears the auto-disarm timer (React's unmount cleanup). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.disarmTimer) clearTimeout(this.disarmTimer)
  }
}
```

_(Member comments are shortened to their first sentence, and the 6 longest are left out to fit; the full declaration is in `node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/confirm-button.component.d.ts`.)_

#### `ConfirmDialog`

ConfirmDialog component.

Composed from `Modal` and `Button` exactly as React composes them; the
dialog's own title/description/children are projected content (`[slot=title]`,
`[slot=description]`, default) or node-valued inputs. React's root is a
`Modal`, which renders a portal while open and nothing while closed, so the
host element leaves the page and the portal renders in `document.body`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-confirm-dialog',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [Button, Modal, MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: ['open', 'title', 'description', 'confirmLabel', 'cancelLabel', 'destructive', 'loading'],
  outputs: ['close', 'confirm'],
  template: `<mol-modal
    #modal
    [open]="open"
    (close)="close.emit($event)"
    [attr.aria-labelledby]="ariaLabelledBy()"
    [attr.aria-describedby]="ariaDescribedBy()"
    ><div [class]="cm().stack(3)">
      <h2
        #titleSlot
        [attr.id]="ariaLabelledBy()"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().textSize('lg'), cm().fontWeight('bold'))"
      >
        @if (isGiven(title)) {
          <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(title)"></ng-container>{{ nodeText(title) }}
        }
        <ng-content select="[slot=title]"></ng-content>
      </h2>
      <p #descriptionSlot [attr.id]="descriptionId" [class]="cm().textSize('sm')">
        @if (isGiven(description)) {
          <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(description)"></ng-container
          >{{ nodeText(description) }}
        }
        <ng-content select="[slot=description]"></ng-content>
      </p>
      <ng-content></ng-content>
      <div [class]="cm().flex({ justify: 'end', gap: 'sm' })">
        <button mol-button [variant]="'ghost'" [disabled]="!!loading" (click)="close.emit($event)">
          @if (isGiven(cancelLabel)) {
            <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(cancelLabel)"></ng-container
            >{{ nodeText(cancelLabel) }}
          } @else {
            {{ cancelText() }}
          }</button
        ><button
          mol-button
          [variant]="'solid'"
          [color]="destructive === false ? 'primary' : 'error'"
          [disabled]="!!loading"
          (click)="confirm.emit()"
        >
          @if (isGiven(confirmLabel) && !loading) {
            <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(confirmLabel)"></ng-container
            >{{ nodeText(confirmLabel) }}
          } @else {
            {{ confirmText() }}
          }
        </button>
      </div>
    </div></mol-modal
  >`,
})
export class ConfirmDialog {
  /**
   * Per-instance id of the rendered title heading, wired as the dialog's
   * accessible name: the binding on `<mol-modal>` reaches the dialog `<div>`,
   * React's `{...rest}` target (Modal names it with `setHostRoot`) —
   * `aria-labelledby` pointing at the visible `<h2>` (WAI-ARIA dialog
   * pattern; axe `aria-dialog-name`).
   */
  readonly titleId = `mol-confirm-dialog-title-${++confirmDialogInstances}`

  /**
   * Per-instance id of the rendered description paragraph, wired as the
   * dialog's accessible description the same way as the title (only bound when projected description content renders — a
   * reference to an empty paragraph describes nothing, axe
   * `aria-dialog-name`/`aria-valid-attr-value`).
   */
  readonly descriptionId = nextInstanceId()

  /** The rendered description paragraph (its projected nodes decide the wiring). */
  // `static: true` resolves the query during creation, so the projected nodes
  // are readable on the FIRST change-detection pass — a non-static query
  // resolves after view init, one pass too late for the description wiring.
  @ViewChild('descriptionSlot', { static: true })
  descriptionSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLParagraphElement>

  /** The title heading (its projected nodes decide whether the dialog points at it). */
  @ViewChild('titleSlot', { static: true }) titleSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLHeadingElement>

  /** Whether the dialog is open. */
  open: ConfirmDialogProps['open'] = false
  /** Title (node-valued prop). */
  title?: ConfirmDialogProps['title']
  /** Body / warning text (node-valued prop). */
  description?: ConfirmDialogProps['description']
  /** Confirm-button label (node-valued prop). */
  confirmLabel?: ConfirmDialogProps['confirmLabel']
  /** Cancel-button label (node-valued prop). */
  cancelLabel?: ConfirmDialogProps['cancelLabel']
  /** Whether the confirm action is destructive (default true). */
  destructive?: ConfirmDialogProps['destructive'] = true
  /** Disables both buttons (the caller owns the pending state). */
  loading?: ConfirmDialogProps['loading']

  /** Template helpers (templates see only class members). */
  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  /** Renders a plain-string/number node value. */
  readonly nodeText = nodeText
  /** Whether a node-valued prop was given. */
  readonly isGiven = isGiven

  /** Fires when the dialog should close (escape, backdrop, cancel). */
  @Output() readonly close = new EventEmitter<unknown>()
  /** Fires when the user confirms. */
  @Output() readonly confirm = new EventEmitter<void>()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The dialog's accessible description id, or null when no description
   * content renders. A signal the template reads, so the association follows
   * the projected description in the pass where it changes.
   */
  readonly ariaDescribedBy = signal<string | null>(null)

  /**
   * The title heading's id, which the dialog's `aria-labelledby` points at, or null while the title
   * renders nothing (React's `slot(title)`): a heading with no text names nothing.
   */
  readonly ariaLabelledBy = signal<string | null>(null)

  /** The composed Modal's element: what the caller binds on this tag lands there, and Modal passes it to its dialog. */
  @ViewChild('modal', { read: ElementRef }) modalElement?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /**
   * Recomputes {@link ariaLabelledBy} and {@link ariaDescribedBy} from the
   * title and description inputs and their projected nodes in
   * the placement function, and takes the host out of the page (React renders
   * the composed `Modal`'s portal in `document.body`, nothing here).
   */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => {
      this.ariaLabelledBy.set(
        rendersNode(this.title) || hasContent(this.titleSlot?.nativeElement) ? this.titleId : null,
      )
      this.ariaDescribedBy.set(
        rendersNode(this.description) || hasContent(this.descriptionSlot?.nativeElement)
          ? this.descriptionId
          : null,
      )
      setElementPresent(this.host.nativeElement, false)
      setHostRoot(this.host.nativeElement, this.modalElement?.nativeElement)
    })
  }

  /**
   * The cancel button's label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  cancelText(): string {
    return typeof this.cancelLabel === 'string'
      ? this.cancelLabel
      : this.i18n.t('confirm.cancel', {}, { defaultValue: 'Cancel' })
  }

  /**
   * The confirm button's label when no node label is given (React shows an
   * ellipsis while loading).
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  confirmText(): string {
    if (this.loading) return '…'
    return typeof this.confirmLabel === 'string'
      ? this.confirmLabel
      : this.i18n.t('confirm.confirm', {}, { defaultValue: 'Confirm' })
  }
}
```

#### `Dropdown`

Dropdown component.

Implements the WAI-ARIA APG menu-button pattern exactly as React does: a
single-element `trigger` is adopted as the real control (no wrapper) and
cloned with `aria-haspopup`, `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`, `tabindex`
and the open/keyboard handlers; any other trigger (text, several nodes) is
wrapped in a `role="button"` fallback container. The menu is rendered into
`document.body` (React's portal) and carries the position, width and
roving-tabindex behavior.

The React root is a FRAGMENT (trigger + portal), so the trigger renders in
front of the host (`placeContentBefore` / `placeElementBefore`) and the
host itself leaves the DOM. The trigger follows the projected content: an
element that replaces it becomes the new trigger, text next to it switches
to the fallback wrapper.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-dropdown',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolTemplateOutlet],
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  inputs: [
    'trigger',
    'items',
    'placement',
    'open',
    'align',
    'width',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'disabled',
  ],
  outputs: ['select', 'openChange'],
  template: `<div #fallback role="button" aria-haspopup="menu" [class]="cm().dropdownTrigger">
      <span #triggerCapture><ng-content select="[slot=trigger]"></ng-content></span>
    </div>
    <ng-container #menuAnchor></ng-container
    ><ng-template #menuTpl
      ><div
        [attr.id]="menuId"
        role="menu"
        tabindex="-1"
        data-state="open"
        [class]="menuClasses()"
        [style]="menuStyleText()"
        [attr.data-testid]="menuTestId()"
        [attr.data-mol-id]="menuAutomationId()"
        (keydown)="handleMenuKeyDown($event)"
      >
        @for (item of items; track keyForItem(item, $index)) {
          @if (item.separator) {
            <div [class]="cm().dropdownSeparator" role="separator"></div>
          } @else {
            <div
              role="menuitem"
              [attr.tabindex]="itemTabIndex($index)"
              [class]="itemClasses(item)"
              (click)="handleItemClick(item)"
              (keydown)="handleItemKeyDown($event, item)"
              [attr.aria-disabled]="item.disabled ? 'true' : null"
              [attr.data-disabled]="item.disabled ? 'true' : null"
            >
              @if (item.icon) {
                <span [class]="cm().dropdownItemIcon">
                  @if (asTemplate(item.icon); as iconTpl) {
                    <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="iconTpl"></ng-container>
                  } @else {
                    {{ iconText(item.icon) }}
                  }
                </span>
              }
              <span [class]="cm().dropdownItemLabel">{{ item.label }}</span>
              @if (item.shortcut) {
                <span [class]="cm().dropdownItemShortcut">{{ item.shortcut }}</span>
              }
            </div>
          }
        }
      </div></ng-template
    >`,
})
export class Dropdown implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy, HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  trigger?: unknown
  items: DropdownItem<string>[] = []
  placement: DropdownPlacement = 'bottom-start'
  open?: boolean
  align: DropdownAlign = 'start'
  width?: 'trigger' | 'auto' | number | string
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string
  automationId?: string
  disabled?: boolean

  @Output() readonly select = new EventEmitter<string>()
  @Output() readonly openChange = new EventEmitter<boolean>()

  @ViewChild('triggerCapture') triggerCapture?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  @ViewChild('fallback') fallback?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('menuAnchor', { read: ViewContainerRef }) menuAnchor?: ViewContainerRef
  @ViewChild('menuTpl') menuTpl?: TemplateRef<unknown>

  readonly menuId = nextInstanceId()
  private readonly internalOpen = signal(false)
  private readonly activeIndex = signal(-1)
  private readonly position = signal<Position>({ top: 0, left: 0 })

  readonly router = useRouter()

  private readonly el = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()
  private readonly renderer = inject(Renderer2)
  private readonly cdr = inject(ChangeDetectorRef)
  private readonly injector = inject(Injector)
  private readonly host: HTMLElement = this.el.nativeElement
  private triggerEl: HTMLElement | SVGElement | null = null
  private triggerOwnTestId: string | null = null
  private triggerOwnMolId: string | null = null
  private triggerOwnDisabled = false
  private triggerIsFallback = false
  private triggerOwnTabindex: string | null = null
  private triggerListeners: (() => void)[] = []
  private menuView?: EmbeddedViewRef<unknown>
  private menuEl: HTMLDivElement | null = null
  private lastOpen = false
  private lastPlacement: DropdownPlacement = 'bottom-start'
  private lastAlign: DropdownAlign = 'start'
  private menuWidth: number | string | undefined
  private removedListeners: (() => void)[] = []
  private lastRoute: string | null = null
  private pendingOpenFocus: 'first' | 'last' = 'first'
  private focusedItemEl: HTMLElement | null = null
  private checkedItems: DropdownItem<string>[] | null = null
  private itemHadFocus = false
  private readonly routeEffect = effect(() => {
    const location = this.router.location()
    const key = `${location.pathname}${location.search}`
    if (key === this.lastRoute) return
    const mounting = this.lastRoute === null
    this.lastRoute = key
    if (!mounting) this.setOpen(false)
  })

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return value instanceof TemplateRef ? value : null
  }

  keyForItem(item: DropdownItem<string>, index: number): string {
    return item.separator ? `separator-${index}` : item.value
  }

  iconText(value: unknown): string {
    return typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''
  }

  isOpen(): boolean {
    if (this.disabled) return false
    return this.open !== undefined ? this.open : this.internalOpen()
  }

  setOpen(open: boolean): void {
    if (this.open === undefined) this.internalOpen.set(open)
    this.openChange.emit(open)
    this.cdr.markForCheck()
  }

  menuClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().dropdownContent, this.className)
  }

  menuTestId(): string | null {
    return this.testId ? `${this.testId}-menu` : null
  }

  menuAutomationId(): string | null {
    return this.automationId ? `${this.automationId}-menu` : null
  }

  menuStyleText(): string | null {
    return styleText({
      ...this.style,
      position: 'absolute',
      top: this.position().top,
      left: this.position().left,
      width: this.menuWidth,
      zIndex: 50,
    })
  }

  itemClasses(item: DropdownItem<string>): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().dropdownItem, item.disabled && this.cm().dropdownItemDisabled)
  }

  itemTabIndex(index: number): number {
    const item = this.items[index]
    if (item?.disabled) return -1
    return index === this.activeIndex() ? 0 : -1
  }

  handleItemClick(item: DropdownItem<string>): void {
    if (item.disabled) return
    this.select.emit(item.value)
    this.closeAndRefocusTrigger()
  }

  handleItemKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent, item: DropdownItem<string>): void {
    if (event.key !== 'Enter' && event.key !== ' ') return
    event.preventDefault()
    this.handleItemClick(item)
  }

  handleMenuKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    const focusable = this.focusableIndexes()
    if (focusable.length === 0) return
    const currentPos = focusable.indexOf(this.activeIndex())
    switch (event.key) {
      case 'ArrowDown':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.activeIndex.set(focusable[(currentPos + 1 + focusable.length) % focusable.length])
        this.focusActive()
        break
      case 'ArrowUp':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.activeIndex.set(focusable[(currentPos - 1 + focusable.length) % focusable.length])
        this.focusActive()
        break
      case 'Home':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.activeIndex.set(focusable[0])
        this.focusActive()
        break
      case 'End':
        event.preventDefault()
        this.activeIndex.set(focusable[focusable.length - 1])
        this.focusActive()
        break
      case 'Tab':
        this.handleClose()
        break
      default:
        break
    }
  }

  handleTriggerClick(): void {
    if (this.disabled) return
    if (!this.isOpen()) this.pendingOpenFocus = 'first'
    this.setOpen(!this.isOpen())
  }
```

#### `EmptyState`

EmptyState component.

Shown when a list or section has no data: an optional icon badge, a title,
a description, and an optional action slot. React renders the icon/action
wrapper divs only when those node props exist; here the wrappers always
exist as projection capture points and are taken out of the DOM when empty
(`setElementPresent`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-empty-state]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  inputs: [
    'icon',
    'title',
    'description',
    'action',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
  ],
  host: {
    // `title` is this component's heading, which React renders as text; Angular would also keep a
    // static `title="…"` on the host, where the browser shows it as a tooltip.
    '[attr.title]': 'null',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'automationId',
  },
  template: `<div [class]="cm().emptyStateIcon" #iconSlot>
      <ng-content select="[slot=icon]"></ng-content>
    </div>
    <h3 [class]="cm().emptyStateTitle">{{ title }}</h3>
    @if (description) {
      <p [class]="cm().emptyStateDescription">{{ description }}</p>
    }
    <div [class]="cm().emptyStateAction" #actionSlot>
      <ng-content select="[slot=action]"></ng-content>
    </div>`,
})
export class EmptyState implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** An `icon` node prop (TemplateRef), when passed as a prop instead of a slot. */
  icon?: unknown
  /** Heading text. */
  title?: string
  /** Description text. */
  description?: string
  /** An `action` node prop (TemplateRef), when passed as a prop instead of a slot. */
  action?: unknown
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id`. */
  automationId?: string

  /** The icon wrapper (detached unless the slot captured nodes). */
  @ViewChild('iconSlot') iconSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The action wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('actionSlot') actionSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private hasIcon = false
  private hasAction = false

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Detaches the icon and action wrappers React would not render. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.syncWrappers(hasContent))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().emptyState, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{icon && <div>…}` / `{action && <div>…}` conditions.
   *
   * @param hasContent - Reads whether a slot has content.
   */
  private syncWrappers(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.hasIcon =
      this.icon !== undefined && this.icon !== null
        ? true
        : hasContent(this.iconSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasAction =
      this.action !== undefined && this.action !== null
        ? true
        : hasContent(this.actionSlot?.nativeElement)
    setElementPresent(this.iconSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasIcon)
    setElementPresent(this.actionSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasAction)
  }
}
```

#### `FloatingInput`

FloatingInput component.

The host is the `span.floatingInputWrapper` React renders; the input and
its floating label sit adjacent with no whitespace (both are inline
elements, so a space would draw). Works controlled (`value`) and
uncontrolled (internal state seeded from `defaultValue`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'span[mol-floating-input]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'placeholder',
    'className',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'id',
    'onFocus',
    'onBlur',
    'onKeyDown',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().floatingInputWrapper',
  },
  template: `<input
      [attr.id]="inputId()"
      [attr.placeholder]="placeholder"
      [class]="classes()"
      [attr.value]="currentValue()"
      (input)="handleInput($event)"
      (change)="stopPropagation($event)"
      (focusin)="onFocus?.($event)"
      (focusout)="onBlur?.($event)"
      (keydown)="onKeyDown?.($event)"
      #inputEl
    />
    @if (placeholder) {
      <label [attr.for]="inputId()" [class]="cm().floatingLabel">{{ placeholder }}</label>
    }`,
})
export class FloatingInput implements OnInit, AfterViewInit, HasElement<HTMLInputElement> {
  /** Placeholder text, also rendered as the floating label. */
  placeholder?: string
  /** Extra classes joined onto the input classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Controlled value. */
  value?: string
  /** Uncontrolled initial value (seeds the internal state). */
  defaultValue?: string
  /** Explicit input id. */
  id?: string

  /** React's `onFocus`, which React spreads onto the <input>: its `currentTarget` is that element. */
  onFocus?: FocusEventHandler
  /** React's `onBlur`, which React spreads onto the <input>: its `currentTarget` is that element. */
  onBlur?: FocusEventHandler
  /** React's `onKeyDown`, which React spreads onto the <input>: its `currentTarget` is that element. */
  onKeyDown?: KeyboardEventHandler

  /** Fires the raw `input` event (React's text-input onChange). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /** The native input (React's `...rest` spread target). */
  private inputEl?: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>
  /** The native input view query; also feeds the caller's ref element. */
  @ViewChild('inputEl')
  set inputElQuery(ref: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement> | undefined) {
    this.inputEl = ref
    this.inner.set(ref?.nativeElement ?? null)
  }

  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLInputElement | null>(null)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the native `<input>`. */
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The per-instance id used when the caller gives none (React's `useId`). */
  private readonly generatedId = nextInstanceId()
  private internalValue = ''

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The input id (`id` or a stable generated id; the DOM normalizer renumbers
   * generated ids in order of appearance, like React's `useId`).
   * @returns The id value.
   */
  inputId(): string {
    return this.id || this.generatedId
  }

  /**
   * Computes the input class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().input(), this.cm().floatingInput, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The rendered value: the controlled `value`, else the internal state
   * (seeded from `defaultValue`, updated by typing when uncontrolled).
   * @returns The value string.
   */
  currentValue(): string {
    return this.value !== undefined ? String(this.value) : this.internalValue
  }

  /**
   * React's onChange: the raw `input` event, with the internal state updated
   * when uncontrolled and the controlled value restored afterwards. The
   * native change event never reaches the host (its `change` output binding
   * would record it a second time).
   *
   * @param event - The native input event.
   */
  handleInput(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    const input = event.target as HTMLInputElement
    if (this.value === undefined) this.internalValue = input.value
    this.change.emit(event)
    if (this.value !== undefined && input.value !== String(this.value))
      input.value = String(this.value)
  }

  /**
   * Swallows the native change event (React's onChange already fired on the
   * input event).
   *
   * @param event - The native change event.
   */
  stopPropagation(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
  }

  /**
   * Seeds the internal state (inputs are set by now, and the template's
   * value binding evaluates only after this hook).
   */
  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.internalValue = String(this.defaultValue ?? '')
  }

  /**
   * Moves rest attributes (`type`, `name`, `disabled`, `readOnly`,
   * `maxLength`, aria/data) onto the input, where React's `{...props}`
   * spread puts them.
   */
  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, this.inputEl?.nativeElement)
  }
}
```

#### `FormField`

FormField component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-form-field]`). React clones a single
child element, adding `id`, `aria-describedby` and `aria-invalid` to its
props; Angular cannot clone a projected component, so those values are
registered on the host as a {@link FormFieldContext} that the field
components (`Input`, `Select`, `Textarea`, `Checkbox`) read.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-form-field]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  inputs: [
    'label',
    'name',
    'error',
    'hint',
    'required',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <label
        [attr.for]="labelFor() || fieldId()"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: !!required }), cm().labelBlock)"
        >{{ label }}</label
      >
    }
    <span #capture data-mol-field-capture><ng-content></ng-content></span>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="errorId" [class]="cm().formError">{{ error }}</p>
    }
    @if (hint && !error) {
      <p [attr.id]="hintId" [class]="cm().formHint">{{ hint }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class FormField implements OnDestroy, HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** Field label. */
  label?: string
  /** Field name (drives the ids). */
  name?: string
  /** Error message (renders and associates via aria-describedby). */
  error?: string
  /** Hint text (shown without an error). */
  hint?: string
  /** Marks the field required (label styling). */
  required?: boolean
  /** Extra classes for the wrapper. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /** The projected-children capture point. */
  @ViewChild('capture') capture?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  /** The label's `for` target (the single child's control id, read after view init). */
  readonly labelFor = signal('')

  /** A generated id used when `name` is absent (the normalizer renumbers it). */
  private readonly generatedId = nextInstanceId()
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)
  private readonly singleState = signal<boolean | null>(null)
  /** The native control this field patched, with the attributes it had before. */
  private patched: {
    control: HTMLElement
    attributes: Record<(typeof PATCHED_ATTRIBUTES)[number], string | null>
  } | null = null
  /** The first projected node the last adoption saw: a different one is adopted again. */
  private adopted: Node | null = null
  /** Whether the adopted content was a single element child. */
  private single = false

  /** Registers the field context before any projected child resolves it. */
  constructor() {
    FormFieldDomContext.provide(this.host.nativeElement, new FormFieldContextValue(this))
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.reconcile(hasContent))
  }

  /** The current class map. */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /** The field id (`name`, or a generated id). */
  fieldId(): string {
    return this.name || this.generatedId
  }

  /** The error paragraph's id. */
  get errorId(): string {
    return `${this.fieldId()}-error`
  }

  /** The hint paragraph's id. */
  get hintId(): string {
    return `${this.fieldId()}-hint`
  }

  /** The id the field should describe itself with (error wins over hint). */
  describedByTarget(): string | undefined {
    if (this.error) return this.errorId
    if (this.hint) return this.hintId
    return undefined
  }

  /** The wrapper class list. */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().inputWrapper, this.cm().sp('mb', 4), this.className)
  }

  /** Serializes the wrapper's inline style. */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /** The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`). */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  private captureEl(): HTMLElement | null {
    return (
      this.capture?.nativeElement ??
      this.host.nativeElement.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-mol-field-capture]')
    )
  }

  /** Whether the projected content is exactly one element (React clones only a single child). */
  singleChild(): boolean {
    const single = this.singleState()
    if (single !== null) return single
    const capture = this.captureEl()
    if (!capture) return false
    const children = Array.from(capture.childNodes)
    const elements = children.filter((node) => node.nodeType === 1)
    const hasText = children.some((node) => node.nodeType === 3 && (node.textContent ?? '').trim())
    return elements.length === 1 && !hasText
  }

  private reconcile(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    const capture = this.captureEl()
    if (!capture) return
    const children = hasContent.nodes(capture)
    if (children.length === 0) {
      this.adopted = null
      this.single = false
      this.singleState.set(null)
      this.unpatchNativeControl()
      this.labelFor.set(this.fieldId())
      setElementPresent(capture, false)
      return
    }
    const first = children[0]!
    // Angular inserts a list's nodes one at a time, and this runs after each:
    // a second child arriving after the first makes the field "not single" again.
    const single = children.length === 1 && first.nodeType === 1
    if (this.adopted === first && this.single === single) {
      // The adopted control's own id may bind after the first run; keep the
      // label pointed at it as it settles (React adopts the child's id).
      const control = this.single
        ? this.host.nativeElement.querySelector<HTMLElement>(FIELD_SELECTOR)
        : null
      this.labelFor.set(control?.id || this.fieldId())
      setElementPresent(capture, false)
      return
    }
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    this.singleState.set(single)
    this.single = single
    placeContent(capture, host, capture)
    // React renders a child it no longer clones with its own props.
    this.unpatchNativeControl()
    if (single) {
      const child = first as HTMLElement
      const control = child.matches(FIELD_SELECTOR)
        ? child
        : child.querySelector<HTMLElement>(FIELD_SELECTOR)
      if (child.matches(FIELD_SELECTOR)) this.patchNativeControl(child)
      this.labelFor.set(control?.id || this.fieldId())
    } else {
      this.labelFor.set(this.fieldId())
    }
    setElementPresent(capture, false)
    this.adopted = first
  }

  /** Applies React's cloned props to a plain native form control child. */
  private patchNativeControl(control: HTMLElement): void {
    this.patched = {
      control,
      attributes: Object.fromEntries(
        PATCHED_ATTRIBUTES.map((name) => [name, control.getAttribute(name)]),
      ) as Record<(typeof PATCHED_ATTRIBUTES)[number], string | null>,
    }
    control.setAttribute('id', this.fieldId())
    const describedBy = this.describedByTarget()
    if (describedBy) {
      const existing = control.getAttribute('aria-describedby')
      control.setAttribute('aria-describedby', [existing, describedBy].filter(Boolean).join(' '))
    }
    if (this.error) control.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true')
  }

  /** Puts back the attributes a patched control had before this field cloned it. */
  private unpatchNativeControl(): void {
    if (this.patched === null) return
    const { control, attributes } = this.patched
    this.patched = null
    for (const name of PATCHED_ATTRIBUTES) {
      const value = attributes[name]
      if (value === null) control.removeAttribute(name)
      else control.setAttribute(name, value)
    }
  }

  /** Removes the context registration (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    FormFieldDomContext.remove(this.host.nativeElement)
  }
}
```

_(Member comments are shortened to their first sentence, and the 3 longest are left out to fit; the full declaration is in `node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/form-field.component.d.ts`.)_

#### `Form`

Form component.

The host is the `<form>` itself (`form[mol-form]`), exactly React's root.
A rejected async handler renders an accessible server-error list by
default; a new submit clears the previous failure. Native action/method
forms keep native submission.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'form[mol-form]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'onFormSubmit',
    'onSubmit',
    'onReset',
    'submitting',
    'submitError',
    'action',
    'method',
    'noValidate',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'className',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
    '[attr.action]': 'action ?? null',
    '[attr.method]': 'method ?? null',
    '[attr.novalidate]': 'noValidate ? "" : null',
    '(submit)': 'handleSubmit($event)',
    '(reset)': 'handleReset($event)',
  },
  template: `@if (messages().length > 0) {
      <ul role="alert" [class]="cm().formError">
        @for (message of messages(); track $index) {
          <li>{{ message }}</li>
        }
      </ul>
    }
    <fieldset
      [class]="cm().formFieldsetContents"
      [attr.disabled]="submitting || pending() ? '' : null"
    >
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    </fieldset>`,
})
export class Form implements HasElement<HTMLFormElement> {
  /** Collects the form's data on submit (React's `onFormSubmit`). */
  onFormSubmit?: ReactFormProps['onFormSubmit']
  /** Receives the submit event (React's `onSubmit`). */
  onSubmit?: ReactFormProps['onSubmit']
  /** Receives the reset event (React's `onReset`). */
  onReset?: ReactFormProps['onReset']
  /** Whether the form is submitting (disables the fieldset). */
  submitting?: ReactFormProps['submitting']
  /** Native form action. */
  action?: ReactFormProps['action']
  /** Native form method. */
  method?: ReactFormProps['method']
  /** Whether native validation is disabled. */
  noValidate?: ReactFormProps['noValidate']
  /** Extra classes for the form. */
  className?: ReactFormProps['className']
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: ReactFormProps['style']
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: ReactFormProps['testId']
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: ReactFormProps['automationId']
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /** Whether a submit is in flight. */
  readonly pending = signal(false)
  /** The failure the last submit produced. */
  private readonly failure = signal<{ error: unknown } | null>(null)
  /** A submit counter: a later submit supersedes an earlier settling one. */
  private attempt = 0
  /** The controlled failure's current value. */
  private controlledError: unknown
  /** Whether the caller has written `submitError` (React's `Object.hasOwn`). */
  private controlled = false
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLFormElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<form>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /**
   * A failure managed by an external form controller (React's `submitError`
   * prop).
   *
   * An accessor, not a field: Angular writes an input by assigning the
   * property, so the write itself is the "this prop was passed" signal React
   * reads with `Object.hasOwn(props, 'submitError')`. A caller that binds
   * `[submitError]` therefore owns the error UI — an explicit `undefined`
   * clears a failure the form captured itself.
   *
   * @returns The controlled failure, as the caller last wrote it.
   */
  get submitError(): ReactFormProps['submitError'] {
    return this.controlledError
  }

  /**
   * Records the caller's controlled failure (and that the caller passed one).
   *
   * @param value - The caller's failure, or `undefined` to clear the error UI.
   */
  set submitError(value: ReactFormProps['submitError']) {
    this.controlled = true
    this.controlledError = value
  }

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the form's inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * The error messages to announce (React's `submitError`/`failure` branch).
   * @returns The messages.
   */
  messages(): string[] {
    // React's `Object.hasOwn(props, 'submitError')` branch: once the caller
    // binds the input, it owns the error UI — an explicitly-undefined value
    // clears the list instead of falling back to a captured failure.
    const failure = this.controlled
      ? this.controlledError === undefined
        ? null
        : { error: this.controlledError }
      : this.failure()
    if (!failure) return []
    return formSubmitErrorMessages(
      failure.error,
      this.i18n.t('ui.form.submitError', undefined, {
        defaultValue: 'Unable to submit. Please try again.',
      }),
    )
  }

  /**
   * Handles a submit event: collects `FormData` for `onFormSubmit`, calls
   * both handlers, and tracks failure/pending (React's `handleSubmit`).
   *
   * @param event - The submit event.
   */
  async handleSubmit(event: Event): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.onFormSubmit && !this.onSubmit) return
    const current = ++this.attempt
    this.failure.set(null)
    this.pending.set(true)
    try {
      const submissions: Promise<void>[] = []
      const call = (handler: () => void | Promise<void>): void => {
        try {
          submissions.push(Promise.resolve(handler()))
        } catch (error) {
          submissions.push(Promise.reject(error))
        }
      }
      const form = event.currentTarget instanceof HTMLFormElement ? event.currentTarget : null
      if (this.onFormSubmit) {
        event.preventDefault()
        const formData = new FormData(form ?? undefined)
        const data: Record<string, unknown> = {}
        formData.forEach((value, key) => {
          data[key] = value
        })
        call(() => this.onFormSubmit?.(data))
      }
      call(() => this.onSubmit?.(event))
      await Promise.all(submissions)
    } catch (error) {
      if (this.attempt === current) this.failure.set({ error })
    } finally {
      if (this.attempt === current) this.pending.set(false)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Calls the reset handler (React's `onReset` through the rest spread).
   *
   * @param event - The reset event.
   */
  handleReset(event: Event): void {
    this.onReset?.(event)
  }
}
```

#### `Icon`

Icon component.

Renders an SVG glyph looked up by `name` from the bonded icon set. The host
IS the `<svg>` (`svg[mol-icon-root]`). Extra attributes (`data-mol-id`,
`aria-label`, `role`, …) land on the host — React's spread target — and
switch the glyph out of decorative mode (no `aria-hidden`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'svg[mol-icon-root]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['name', 'size', 'className', 'ariaHidden: aria-hidden'],
  host: {
    '[attr.width]': 'attrSize()',
    '[attr.height]': 'attrSize()',
    '[attr.viewBox]': 'failed ? "0 0 20 20" : icon().viewBox || "0 0 20 20"',
    '[attr.fill]': 'failed ? "none" : fill()',
    '[attr.stroke]': 'strokeAttrs().stroke',
    '[attr.stroke-width]': 'strokeAttrs().strokeWidth',
    '[attr.stroke-linecap]': 'strokeAttrs().strokeLinecap',
    '[attr.stroke-linejoin]': 'strokeAttrs().strokeLinejoin',
    '[class]': 'className',
    '[attr.aria-hidden]': 'ariaHiddenAttr()',
    '[attr.focusable]': 'decorative() ? "false" : null',
  },
  template: `@if (failed) {
      <svg:circle cx="10" cy="10" r="8" fill="currentColor" opacity="0.15" />
    } @else {
      @for (p of paths(); track $index) {
        <svg:path
          [attr.d]="p.d"
          [attr.fill]="p.fill"
          [attr.fill-rule]="p.fillRule"
          [attr.clip-rule]="p.clipRule"
          [attr.opacity]="p.opacity"
        />
      }
    }`,
})
export class Icon implements OnChanges {
  /** The glyph name from the bonded icon set. */
  name!: IconProps['name']
  /** Width and height in pixels (default 20). */
  size?: IconProps['size'] = 20
  /** Class name forwarded to the `<svg>`. */
  className?: IconProps['className']
  /**
   * Explicit `aria-hidden` (React stringifies `false` to `"false"` rather
   * than dropping the attribute).
   */
  ariaHidden?: IconProps['aria-hidden']

  /** Whether the name is missing from the bonded set (placeholder renders). */
  failed = false

  /**
   * The rendered size (the default applies when a later-bound input writes
   * `undefined` over the field default, matching React's absent-prop default).
   * @returns The pixel size.
   */
  attrSize(): number {
    return this.size ?? 20
  }

  /**
   * Resolves the icon, warning once per render pass like React does.
   */
  ngOnChanges(): void {
    this.failed = false
    this.resolved = undefined
    try {
      this.resolved = getIcon(this.name)
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn(
        `Icon: no bonded glyph for "${String(this.name)}" — rendering a placeholder.`,
        error,
      )
      this.failed = true
    }
    const svg = this.resolved?.svg
    if (svg) (this.host.nativeElement as SVGSVGElement).innerHTML = svg
  }

  private resolved?: IconData

  /**
   * The resolved icon data (throws nothing; placeholder branch covers gaps).
   * @returns The icon data.
   */
  icon(): IconData {
    return this.resolved ?? { paths: [] }
  }

  /**
   * The fill attribute value (`none` for `icon.svg`-backed glyphs without an
   * explicit fill, matching React's two branches).
   * @returns The fill.
   */
  fill(): string {
    const icon = this.icon()
    if (icon.svg) return icon.fill || 'none'
    return icon.stroke ? 'none' : icon.fill || 'currentColor'
  }

  /**
   * Stroke attribute values (null when the icon is not stroked).
   * @returns The stroke attribute bag.
   */
  strokeAttrs(): {
    stroke: string | null
    strokeWidth: number | null
    strokeLinecap: string | null
    strokeLinejoin: string | null
  } {
    const icon = this.icon()
    if (!icon.stroke) {
      return { stroke: null, strokeWidth: null, strokeLinecap: null, strokeLinejoin: null }
    }
    return {
      stroke: icon.stroke ?? null,
      strokeWidth: icon.strokeWidth ?? null,
      strokeLinecap: icon.strokeLinecap ?? null,
      strokeLinejoin: icon.strokeLinejoin ?? null,
    }
  }

  /**
   * The glyph paths (empty for `icon.svg`-backed glyphs).
   * @returns The path list.
   */
  paths(): IconData['paths'] {
    return this.icon().svg ? [] : this.icon().paths
  }

  /**
   * Whether the glyph renders in decorative mode (no caller accessibility
   * props on the host).
   * @returns True when `aria-hidden` applies.
   */
  decorative(): boolean {
    const el = this.host.nativeElement
    return (
      !el.hasAttribute('aria-label') &&
      !el.hasAttribute('aria-labelledby') &&
      !el.hasAttribute('role')
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `aria-hidden` attribute value: an explicit value wins (React keeps
   * `aria-hidden={false}` as `"false"`), otherwise decorative mode applies.
   * @returns The attribute text or null.
   */
  ariaHiddenAttr(): string | null {
    if (this.ariaHidden !== undefined)
      return this.ariaHidden === true
        ? 'true'
        : this.ariaHidden === false
          ? 'false'
          : String(this.ariaHidden)
    return this.decorative() ? 'true' : null
  }

  /** The svg host element. */
  readonly host: ElementRef<SVGSVGElement> = inject(ElementRef)
}
```

#### `Input`

Input component.

The host is the `div.inputWrapper` React renders. React's text-input
`onChange` fires on `input` events; a controlled `value` is restored after
user input exactly like React's controlled-input state restore.

The adornment wrappers React renders conditionally
(`{!!leftElement && <div …>}` / `{(rightElement || (clearable && value)) && …}`)
are always-mounted `<ng-content>` capture points that are taken out of the
DOM whenever React would render nothing (`setElementPresent`).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-input]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolIconSvg, NgTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'type',
    'size',
    'align',
    'label',
    'error',
    'hint',
    'leftElement',
    'rightElement',
    'clearable',
    'clearLabel',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'required',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'placeholder',
    'name',
    'id',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'onFocus',
    'onBlur',
    'onKeyDown',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().inputWrapper',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <label
        [attr.for]="inputId()"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: !!required }), cm().labelBlock)"
        >{{ label }}</label
      >
    }
    <div [class]="cm().inputInner">
      <div [class]="cm().inputLeftElement" #leftSlot>
        <ng-content select="[slot=leftElement]"></ng-content>
        @if (asTemplate(leftElement); as tpl) {
          <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="tpl" />
        } @else {
          {{ asText(leftElement) }}
        }
      </div>
      <input
        [attr.type]="type ?? 'text'"
        [attr.id]="inputId()"
        [attr.name]="name"
        [attr.value]="value ?? defaultValue"
        [attr.placeholder]="placeholder"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.required]="required ? '' : null"
        [attr.aria-invalid]="error || fieldContext()?.invalid ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [attr.aria-describedby]="mergedDescribedBy()"
        [class]="classes()"
        [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        [attr.data-mol-id]="molId()"
        (input)="handleInput($event)"
        (change)="handleChange($event)"
        (focusin)="onFocus?.($event)"
        (focusout)="onBlur?.($event)"
        (keydown)="onKeyDown?.($event)"
        #inputEl
      />
      <div [class]="cm().inputRightElement" #rightSlot>
        @if (clearable && value) {
          <button
            type="button"
            (click)="clear.emit($event)"
            [class]="cm().inputClearButton"
            [attr.aria-label]="clearText()"
          >
            <svg mol-icon [data]="clearIcon()" />
          </button>
        }
        <span [class]="cm().textMuted" #rightInner
          ><ng-content select="[slot=rightElement]"></ng-content>
          @if (asTemplate(rightElement); as tpl) {
            <ng-container [ngTemplateOutlet]="tpl" />
          } @else {
            {{ asText(rightElement) }}
          }
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="inputId() + '-error'" [class]="cm().formError">{{ error }}</p>
    }
    @if (hint && !error) {
      <p [attr.id]="inputId() + '-hint'" [class]="cm().formHint">{{ hint }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class Input implements AfterViewChecked, HasElement<HTMLInputElement> {
  type?: string
  size?: Size
  align?: 'left' | 'center' | 'right'
  label?: string
  error?: string
  hint?: string
  leftElement?: unknown
  rightElement?: unknown
  clearable?: boolean
  clearLabel?: string
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string
  disabled?: boolean
  required?: boolean
  value?: string | number
  defaultValue?: string | number
  placeholder?: string
  name?: string
  id?: string
  automationId?: string
  dataMolId?: string

  onFocus?: FocusEventHandler
  onBlur?: FocusEventHandler
  onKeyDown?: KeyboardEventHandler

  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()
  @Output() readonly clear = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  inputEl?: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>
  @ViewChild('inputEl')
  set inputElQuery(ref: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement> | undefined) {
    this.inputEl = ref
    this.inner.set(ref?.nativeElement ?? null)
  }

  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLInputElement | null>(null)
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()
  @ViewChild('leftSlot') leftSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('rightSlot') rightSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('rightInner') rightInner?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly hasLeft = signal(false)
  private readonly hasRight = signal(false)

  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.syncAdornments(hasContent))
  }

  private readonly generatedId = nextInstanceId()

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private movedRest = false
  private restDescribedBy: string | null = null
  private externalMolId: string | undefined

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  inputId(): string {
    return this.id || this.fieldContext()?.id || this.name || this.generatedId
  }

  describedBy(): string | null {
    if (this.error) return `${this.inputId()}-error`
    if (this.hint) return `${this.inputId()}-hint`
    return null
  }

  fieldContext(): FormFieldContext | null {
    return fieldContext(this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement)
  }

  mergedDescribedBy(): string | null {
    const target = this.fieldContext()?.describedBy
    if (target) return [this.restDescribedBy, target].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
    return this.restDescribedBy ?? this.describedBy()
  }

  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().input({
        error: !!this.error,
        size: this.size ?? 'md',
        align: this.align === 'right' ? undefined : this.align,
      }),
      this.hasLeft() && this.cm().inputPadLeft,
      (this.hasRight() || this.clearable) && this.cm().inputPadRight,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  clearText(): string {
    return this.clearLabel ?? this.i18n.t('ui.input.clear', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Clear' })
  }

  clearIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconSm)
  }

  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  molId(): string | undefined {
    return this.automationId ?? this.dataMolId ?? this.externalMolId
  }

  handleInput(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    this.change.emit(event)
    const input = this.inputEl?.nativeElement
    if (this.value !== undefined && input && input.value !== String(this.value))
      input.value = String(this.value)
  }

  handleChange(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
  }

  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    adoptHostDataMolId(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      this.inputEl?.nativeElement ?? this.host.nativeElement.querySelector('input'),
      (id) => {
        this.externalMolId = id
      },
      () => this.molId(),
    )
  }

  private syncAdornments(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    const left = this.leftSlot?.nativeElement
    const right = this.rightSlot?.nativeElement
    const inner =
      this.rightInner?.nativeElement ??
      (right ? (right.querySelector('span') as HTMLElement | null) : null) ??
      undefined
    const hasLeft = this.isGiven(this.leftElement) || hasContent(left)
    const hasRight = this.isGiven(this.rightElement) || hasContent(inner)
    this.hasLeft.set(hasLeft)
    this.hasRight.set(hasRight)
    const clear = !!(this.clearable && this.value)
    setElementPresent(left, hasLeft)
    setElementPresent(inner, hasRight && !clear)
    setElementPresent(right, hasRight || clear)
  }
```
