#### `Label`

Label component.

A `<label>` with the class map's label styles; `required` switches to the
required variant and `htmlFor` renders the `for` attribute.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'label[mol-label]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['required', 'className', 'htmlFor'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[attr.for]': 'htmlFor',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Label implements HasElement<HTMLLabelElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLLabelElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<label>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Whether the label renders its required marker. */
  required?: boolean
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** The associated control's id (`for` attribute). */
  htmlFor?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the label class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().label({ required: !!this.required }), this.className)
  }
}
```

#### `LanguagePicker`

LanguagePicker component.

React's root is a FRAGMENT (trigger button + locale modal), so everything
the host holds renders in front of the host (`placeContentBefore`) and the
host itself leaves the page: the rendered tree is React's, with no wrapper
element around the trigger. Rest props (`title`,
`data-*`, `aria-*`, native event handlers) are spread onto the trigger
button in React; here they land on the host and are moved onto it.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-language-picker',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [Icon, Modal, MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'labelKey',
    'labelDefault',
    'modalTitleKey',
    'modalTitleDefault',
    'icon',
    'iconSize',
    'display',
    'className',
    'style',
    'renderTrigger',
  ],
  template: `@if (asTemplate(renderTrigger); as customTrigger) {
      <ng-container
        [molTemplateOutlet]="customTrigger"
        [molTemplateOutletContext]="triggerContext"
      ></ng-container>
    } @else {
      <button
        #trigger
        type="button"
        data-mol-id="language-picker-trigger"
        [attr.aria-label]="ariaLabel()"
        (click)="open()"
        [class]="classes()"
        [style]="hostStyle()"
      >
        <svg mol-icon-root [name]="icon ?? 'globe'" [size]="iconSize ?? 16"></svg>
        @if (triggerLabel(); as label) {
          <span>{{ label }}</span>
        }
      </button>
    }
    <mol-modal
      [open]="isOpen()"
      (close)="isOpen.set(false)"
      [title]="modalTitle()"
      [size]="'lg'"
      data-mol-id="language-picker-modal"
      ><div [class]="cm().languageGrid">
        @for (locale of sortedLocales(); track locale.code) {
          <button
            type="button"
            [attr.data-mol-id]="'language-picker-option-' + locale.code"
            [attr.data-active]="locale.code === currentLocale() ? 'true' : null"
            [attr.aria-pressed]="locale.code === currentLocale() ? 'true' : 'false'"
            (click)="pick(locale.code)"
            [class]="locale.code === currentLocale() ? cm().languageActive : cm().languageOption"
          >
            {{ locale.name }}
          </button>
        }
      </div></mol-modal
    >`,
})
export class LanguagePicker {
  /** i18n key for the trigger label / aria-label (default `footer.language`). */
  labelKey?: LanguagePickerProps['labelKey']
  /** Fallback label if the i18n key is missing (default `Language`). */
  labelDefault?: LanguagePickerProps['labelDefault']
  /** i18n key for the modal title (default `languagePicker.modalTitle`). */
  modalTitleKey?: LanguagePickerProps['modalTitleKey']
  /** Fallback modal title if the i18n key is missing (default `Choose language`). */
  modalTitleDefault?: LanguagePickerProps['modalTitleDefault']
  /** Icon glyph before the current locale name (default `globe`). */
  icon?: LanguagePickerProps['icon']
  /** Pixel size for the rendered icon (default 16). */
  iconSize?: LanguagePickerProps['iconSize']
  /** What the trigger renders: the locale name, its code, or the icon only. */
  display?: LanguagePickerProps['display'] = 'name'
  /** Extra classes appended to the trigger button. */
  className?: LanguagePickerProps['className']
  /** Inline style object for the trigger button (React camelCase). */
  style?: LanguagePickerProps['style']
  /** Optional trigger replacement (a node value rendered as a template). */
  renderTrigger?: LanguagePickerProps['renderTrigger']

  /**
   * The context a `renderTrigger` template receives: `open` (also as
   * `$implicit`), React's `renderTrigger(open)` argument.
   */
  readonly triggerContext: Record<string, unknown> = {
    $implicit: (): void => this.open(),
    open: (): void => this.open(),
  }

  /** The default trigger button. */
  @ViewChild('trigger') trigger?: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>

  /** Whether the locale modal is open. */
  readonly isOpen = signal(false)

  /**
   * i18n state: locale, locale list and the async setter. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /**
   * Renders everything the host holds (the trigger, or the caller's
   * `renderTrigger` output, and the locale modal) in front of the host, which
   * leaves the page: React's root is a fragment. What the caller binds on the
   * tag lands on the trigger button, React's `{...rest}` target.
   */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement(() => {
      const host = this.host.nativeElement
      placeContentBefore(host, host)
      setElementPresent(host, false)
      setHostRoot(host, this.trigger?.nativeElement)
    })
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  ariaLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.labelKey ?? 'footer.language',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.labelDefault ?? 'Language',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The modal title.
   * @returns The title text.
   */
  modalTitle(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.modalTitleKey ?? 'languagePicker.modalTitle',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.modalTitleDefault ?? 'Choose language',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().languagePickerTrigger, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the trigger's inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Narrows a node-valued prop to a TemplateRef.
   *
   * @param value - The prop value.
   * @returns The template, or null.
   */
  asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return value instanceof TemplateRef ? value : null
  }

  /**
   * The active locale code.
   * @returns The code.
   */
  currentLocale(): string {
    return this.i18n.locale()
  }

  /**
   * The registered locales, sorted by native name (React sorts a copy).
   * @returns The sorted locales.
   */
  sortedLocales(): { code: string; name: string }[] {
    return [...this.i18n.locales()].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's text (React's `display` switch).
   * @returns The text, or null for the icon-only display.
   */
  triggerLabel(): string | null {
    if (this.display === 'icon') return null
    if (this.display === 'code') return this.currentLocale()
    const current = this.sortedLocales().find((locale) => locale.code === this.currentLocale())
    return current?.name ?? this.currentLocale()
  }

  /** Opens the locale modal. */
  open(): void {
    this.isOpen.set(true)
  }

  /**
   * Switches the locale and closes the modal (React's option click).
   * @param code - The locale code.
   */
  async pick(code: string): Promise<void> {
    await this.i18n.setLocale(code)
    this.isOpen.set(false)
  }
}
```

#### `Container`

Container component.

Centers a max-width content column; rest props land on the host element.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-container]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'maxWidth',
    'centered',
    'paddingX',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Container implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Maximum width preset. */
  maxWidth: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | '2xl' | 'full' | string = 'lg'
  /** Whether the column is centered. */
  centered = true
  /** Horizontal padding (accepted for React parity, not rendered). */
  paddingX?: unknown
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the container class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().container({
        size: this.maxWidth as 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | '2xl' | 'full',
      }),
      this.centered && this.cm().mxAuto,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }
}
```

#### `Flex`

Flex container component.

A numeric or non-preset string gap becomes an inline style.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-flex]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'direction',
    'justify',
    'align',
    'wrap',
    'gap',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Flex implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Flex direction. */
  direction: 'row' | 'column' | 'row-reverse' | 'column-reverse' = 'row'
  /** Main-axis justification. */
  justify?: string
  /** Cross-axis alignment. */
  align?: string
  /** Wrapping behavior. */
  wrap?: string
  /** Gap (preset name, number in px, or any CSS value). */
  gap?: number | string
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the flex class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const cmGap =
      typeof this.gap === 'string' && GAP_NAMES.includes(this.gap)
        ? (this.gap as 'none' | 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl')
        : undefined
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({
        direction: DIRECTION_MAP[this.direction] || 'row',
        align: this.align as never,
        justify: this.justify as never,
        wrap: this.wrap as never,
        gap: cmGap,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style (numeric or custom gaps inline).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const gapStyle =
      typeof this.gap === 'number'
        ? { gap: this.gap }
        : typeof this.gap === 'string' && !GAP_NAMES.includes(this.gap)
          ? { gap: this.gap }
          : undefined
    return styleText({ ...this.style, ...gapStyle })
  }
}
```

#### `Grid`

Grid container component.

Numeric `columns` maps to the class map; a string is a `grid-template-columns`
value. `gap`/`columnGap`/`rowGap` follow the same preset-or-CSS rule as Flex.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-grid]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'columns',
    'rows',
    'gap',
    'columnGap',
    'rowGap',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class Grid implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Column count or `grid-template-columns` value. */
  columns?: number | string
  /** `grid-template-rows` value. */
  rows?: string
  /** Gap (preset name, number in px, or any CSS value). */
  gap?: number | string
  /** Column gap. */
  columnGap?: number | string
  /** Row gap. */
  rowGap?: number | string
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the grid class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const cmGap =
      typeof this.gap === 'string' && GAP_NAMES.includes(this.gap)
        ? (this.gap as 'none' | 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl')
        : undefined
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().grid({
        cols: typeof this.columns === 'number' ? this.columns : undefined,
        gap: cmGap,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    // Only the values this Grid sets, as React's Grid does: an undefined key would erase the same
    // property from the caller's `style`.
    const own: Record<string, unknown> = {}
    if (typeof this.columns === 'string') own.gridTemplateColumns = this.columns
    if (typeof this.rows === 'string') own.gridTemplateRows = this.rows
    if (
      typeof this.gap === 'number' ||
      (typeof this.gap === 'string' && !GAP_NAMES.includes(this.gap))
    )
      own.gap = this.gap
    if (typeof this.columnGap === 'number' || typeof this.columnGap === 'string')
      own.columnGap = this.columnGap
    if (typeof this.rowGap === 'number' || typeof this.rowGap === 'string') own.rowGap = this.rowGap
    return styleText({ ...this.style, ...own })
  }
}
```

#### `Spacer`

Spacer component.

A preset size uses the class map; a number (px) or custom CSS value uses
inline width/height.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-spacer]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'size',
    'horizontal',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'ariaHidden: aria-hidden',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
    '[attr.aria-hidden]': 'ariaHiddenAttr()',
  },
  template: '',
})
export class Spacer implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Spacer size (preset name, number in px, or CSS value). */
  size: 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' | number | string = 'md'
  /** Whether the spacer takes width instead of height. */
  horizontal?: boolean
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** Explicit `aria-hidden` (React stringifies booleans). */
  ariaHidden?: boolean | string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * The `aria-hidden` attribute value (React renders `true` as `"true"`).
   * @returns The attribute text or null.
   */
  ariaHiddenAttr(): string | null {
    if (this.ariaHidden === undefined) return null
    return this.ariaHidden === true
      ? 'true'
      : this.ariaHidden === false
        ? 'false'
        : String(this.ariaHidden)
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Whether `size` is one of the class map's named sizes.
   * @returns True for preset names.
   */
  get isNamedSize(): boolean {
    return typeof this.size === 'string' && SPACER_SIZES.includes(this.size)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the spacer class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.isNamedSize
        ? this.cm().spacer({
            size: this.size as 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl',
            horizontal: !!this.horizontal,
          })
        : this.horizontal
          ? this.cm().displayInlineBlock
          : this.cm().displayBlock,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const sizeStyle =
      typeof this.size === 'number'
        ? this.horizontal
          ? { width: this.size, height: '1px' }
          : { height: this.size, width: '1px' }
        : typeof this.size === 'string' && !this.isNamedSize
          ? this.horizontal
            ? { width: this.size }
            : { height: this.size }
          : undefined
    return styleText({ ...this.style, ...sizeStyle })
  }
}
```

#### `LoadErrorBanner`

LoadErrorBanner component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-load-error-banner]`); rest props
(`className`, `data-*`, `aria-*`, `id`) land on it exactly as React spreads
them. `message`, `detail` and `retryLabel` are node-valued props, projected
as `[slot=message]` / `[slot=detail]` / `[slot=retryLabel]`. The detail
paragraph and the retry row render only when React would render them.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-load-error-banner]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [Alert, MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: ['message', 'detail', 'retryLabel', 'retryMolId', 'onRetry'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'className',
  },
  template: `<div mol-alert [status]="'error'" [live]="false">
    <div [class]="cm().stack(2)">
      <p [class]="cm().textSize('sm')">
        <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(message)"></ng-container
        ><ng-content select="[slot=message]"></ng-content>{{ nodeText(message) }}
      </p>
      <p #detailSlot [class]="detailClasses()">
        <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(detail)"></ng-container
        ><ng-content select="[slot=detail]"></ng-content>{{ nodeText(detail) }}
      </p>
      <ng-content></ng-content>
      <div #retrySlot [class]="cm().flex({ justify: 'start' })">
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="retryClasses()"
          [attr.aria-busy]="'false'"
          [disabled]="pending()"
          [attr.data-mol-id]="retryMolId"
          (click)="handleRetry()"
        >
          <span #retryLabelSlot><ng-content select="[slot=retryLabel]"></ng-content></span>
          @if (!hasRetryLabel() && isGiven(retryLabel)) {
            <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(retryLabel)"></ng-container
            >{{ nodeText(retryLabel) }}
          } @else if (!hasRetryLabel()) {
            {{ retryText() }}
          }
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>`,
})
export class LoadErrorBanner {
  /** The primary error line (node-valued prop). */
  message?: LoadErrorBannerProps['message']
  /** Secondary line under the message (node-valued prop). */
  detail?: LoadErrorBannerProps['detail']
  /** Retry-button label (node-valued prop). */
  retryLabel?: LoadErrorBannerProps['retryLabel']
  /** `data-mol-id` for the retry button (default `load-error-retry`). */
  retryMolId?: LoadErrorBannerProps['retryMolId'] = 'load-error-retry'
  /** Called when Retry is clicked; a returned promise keeps the button pending. */
  onRetry?: LoadErrorBannerProps['onRetry']
  /** Extra classes for the root (React's rest `className`). */
  className?: LoadErrorBannerProps['className']

  /** Template helpers (templates see only class members). */
  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  /** Renders a plain-string/number node value. */
  readonly nodeText = nodeText
  /** Whether a node-valued prop was given. */
  readonly isGiven = isGiven

  /** Whether a retry is in flight. */
  readonly pending = signal(false)

  /** The detail paragraph (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('detailSlot') detailSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLParagraphElement>
  /** The retry row (always in the template; detached when no callback). */
  @ViewChild('retrySlot') retrySlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The retry label capture point. */
  @ViewChild('retryLabelSlot') retryLabelSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /** Whether the projected retry label exists (a signal the placement function sets). */
  private readonly hasRetryLabel = signal(false)

  /** Reconciles the detail/retry wrappers and the projected retry label. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.reconcile(hasContent))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The detail paragraph classes.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  detailClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().textSize('xs'), this.cm().textMuted)
  }

  /**
   * The retry button classes (React renders `Button variant="outline" size="sm"`).
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  retryClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().button({ variant: 'outline', color: 'primary', size: 'sm' })
  }

  /**
   * The retry button's text: the projected label, or the localized default.
   * @returns The default text (empty while a projected label is shown).
   */
  retryText(): string {
    return this.pending()
      ? this.i18n.t('loadError.retrying', {}, { defaultValue: 'Retrying…' })
      : this.i18n.t('loadError.retry', {}, { defaultValue: 'Retry' })
  }

  /**
   * Runs the retry callback with React's pending semantics: disabled and
   * "Retrying…" while the promise is unsettled; a rejection is not swallowed
   * — the banner stays for the caller.
   */
  handleRetry(): void {
    if (!this.onRetry || this.pending()) return
    this.pending.set(true)
    void Promise.resolve(this.onRetry())
      .catch((_error: unknown) => undefined)
      .finally(() => this.pending.set(false))
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's detail/retry conditions and moves the projected retry
   * label onto the retry button.
   *
   * @param hasContent - Reads whether a slot has content.
   */
  private reconcile(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    const retryLabelSlot = this.retryLabelSlot?.nativeElement
    this.hasRetryLabel.set(hasContent(retryLabelSlot))
    setElementPresent(retryLabelSlot, false)
    setElementPresent(this.detailSlot?.nativeElement, this.detailPresent(hasContent))
    // React renders the retry row exactly when `onRetry` is given.
    setElementPresent(this.retrySlot?.nativeElement, !!this.onRetry)
    this.showRetryLabel()
  }

  /**
   * Whether React would render the detail paragraph (a given node value or
   * projected slot content).
   *
   * @param hasContent - Reads whether a slot has content.
   * @returns True when the detail renders.
   */
  private detailPresent(hasContent: SlotReader): boolean {
    return isGiven(this.detail) || hasContent(this.detailSlot?.nativeElement)
  }

  /** Moves the projected retry label onto the retry button (React: no wrapper). */
  private showRetryLabel(): void {
    const host = this.retrySlot?.nativeElement?.querySelector('button')
    const span = this.retryLabelSlot?.nativeElement
    if (!host || !span) return
    if (!this.hasRetryLabel()) {
      placeContent(span, null)
      return
    }
    placeContent(span, host)
  }
}
```

#### `Modal`

Modal component.

React's root is a FRAGMENT portaled into `document.body` (an overlay
`<div>` and a centering wrapper `<div>`), so the host element leaves the
page and both elements are moved into the body while open: the rendered
tree is React's, with no wrapper at the component's place. Implements the
WAI-ARIA APG modal dialog pattern (focus
into the dialog on open, trapped Tab, focus restored on close, Escape for
the topmost dialog only, reference-counted body scroll lock).

Projected content is created with the consumer's view, so a closed modal's body and footer exist
while hidden: their components, effects and fetches run. React mounts them only while shown, and
again each time they show. Write content with state as `*molLazy` (`MolLazy` from
`@molecule/app-angular`): it is created when it shows and destroyed when it hides, as React's is.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-modal',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: [
    'open',
    'title',
    'size',
    'showCloseButton',
    'closeOnOverlayClick',
    'closeOnEscape',
    'footer',
    'centered',
    'closeLabel',
    'preventScroll',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'onDoubleClick',
  ],
  outputs: ['close'],
  template: `<div #overlay [class]="cm().dialogOverlay" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    <div
      #wrapper
      [class]="cm().dialogWrapper"
      [style]="wrapperStyle()"
      role="presentation"
      (click)="handleOverlayClick($event)"
    >
      <div
        #dialog
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        [attr.aria-labelledby]="title ? titleId : null"
        tabindex="-1"
        [class]="dialogClasses()"
        [style]="dialogStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        [attr.data-mol-id]="molId()"
        (dblclick)="onDoubleClick?.($event)"
      >
        @if (title) {
          <div [class]="cm().dialogHeader">
            <h2 [id]="titleId" [class]="cm().dialogTitle">{{ title }}</h2>
            @if (showCloseButton) {
              <button
                type="button"
                (click)="requestCloseFromButton($event)"
                [class]="cm().dialogClose"
                data-mol-id="modal-close"
                [attr.aria-label]="closeText()"
              >
                <svg mol-icon [data]="closeIcon()" />
              </button>
            }
          </div>
        } @else {
          @if (showCloseButton) {
            <button
              type="button"
              (click)="requestCloseFromButton($event)"
              [class]="cm().dialogCloseFloating"
              data-mol-id="modal-close"
              [attr.aria-label]="closeText()"
            >
              <svg mol-icon [data]="closeIcon()" />
            </button>
          }
        }
        <div [class]="cm().dialogBody"><ng-content></ng-content></div>
        <div #footerSlot [class]="cm().dialogFooter">
          <ng-content select="[slot=footer]"></ng-content>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`,
})
export class Modal
  implements AfterViewInit, AfterViewChecked, DoCheck, OnDestroy, HasElement<HTMLDivElement>
{
  open = false
  title?: string
  size: ModalSize = 'md'
  showCloseButton = true
  closeOnOverlayClick = true
  closeOnEscape = true
  footer?: unknown
  centered = true
  closeLabel?: string
  preventScroll = true
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string
  automationId?: string
  dataMolId?: string
  onDoubleClick?: MouseEventHandler

  @Output() readonly close = new EventEmitter<MouseEvent | undefined>()

  @ViewChild('overlay') overlay?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('wrapper') wrapper?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('dialog') dialog?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('footerSlot') footerSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  readonly titleId = `mol-modal-title-${++modalInstance}`
  private readonly stackId = modalInstance

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly injector = inject(Injector)
  private readonly environmentInjector = inject(EnvironmentInjector)
  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()

  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.reconcileFooter(hasContent))
  }

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly sync = createScrollLock()
  private lastOpen = false
  private lastFooter = false
  private previouslyFocused: HTMLElement | null = null
  private listeners: (() => void)[] = []
  private locked = false
  private externalMolId: string | undefined

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  dialogClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().modal({ size: this.size }), this.className)
  }

  dialogStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  molId(): string | undefined {
    return this.automationId ?? this.dataMolId ?? this.externalMolId
  }

  wrapperStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.centered ? undefined : { alignItems: 'flex-start' })
  }

  closeText(): string {
    return this.closeLabel ?? this.i18n.t('ui.modal.close', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Close' })
  }

  closeIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconMd)
  }

  handleOverlayClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    if (event.target !== event.currentTarget) {
      event.stopPropagation()
      return
    }
    if (this.closeOnOverlayClick) this.requestClose()
  }

  requestClose(): void {
    this.close.emit()
  }

  requestCloseFromButton(event: MouseEvent): void {
    this.close.emit(event)
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    const overlay = this.overlay?.nativeElement
    const wrapper = this.wrapper?.nativeElement
    const dialog = this.dialog?.nativeElement
    if (dialog) {
      adoptHostDataMolId(
        this.host.nativeElement,
        dialog,
        (id) => {
          this.externalMolId = id
        },
        () => this.molId(),
      )
    }
    const open = !!this.open
    if (open) {
      this.previouslyFocused = (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null) ?? null
      pushModalStack(this.stackId)
      if (overlay && wrapper) this.attachPortal()
      this.focusFirstInDialog()
      this.addListeners()
    } else if (overlay && wrapper) {
      this.detachPortal()
    }
    this.syncScrollLock()
    this.lastOpen = open
  }
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    const open = !!this.open
    if (open !== this.lastOpen) {
      this.lastOpen = open
      this.transition(open)
    }
    this.syncScrollLock()
  }

  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    adoptHostDataMolId(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      this.dialog?.nativeElement,
      (id) => {
        this.externalMolId = id
      },
      () => this.molId(),
    )
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.removeListeners()
    popModalStack(this.stackId)
    const toRestore = this.lastOpen ? this.previouslyFocused : null
    if (toRestore && !this.environmentInjector.destroyed)
      afterNextRender(
        () => {
          if (document.contains(toRestore)) toRestore.focus()
        },
        { injector: this.environmentInjector },
      )
    if (this.locked) {
      this.sync.unlock()
      this.locked = false
    }
  }

  private reconcileFooter(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.lastFooter = hasContent(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement)
    setElementPresent(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement, this.lastFooter)
    setElementPresent(this.host.nativeElement, false)
    setHostRoot(this.host.nativeElement, this.dialog?.nativeElement, { except: ['data-mol-id'] })
  }

  private transition(open: boolean): void {
    if (open) {
      this.previouslyFocused = (document.activeElement as HTMLElement | null) ?? null
      pushModalStack(this.stackId)
      this.attachPortal()
      this.focusFirstInDialog()
      this.addListeners()
    } else {
      popModalStack(this.stackId)
      this.removeListeners()
      this.detachPortal()
      const toRestore = this.previouslyFocused
      if (toRestore)
        afterNextRender(
          () => {
            if (document.contains(toRestore)) toRestore.focus()
          },
          { injector: this.injector },
        )
    }
    this.syncScrollLock()
  }

  private focusFirstInDialog(): void {
    afterNextRender(
      () => {
        const dialog = this.dialog?.nativeElement
        if (!dialog) return
        const focusables = getFocusableElements(dialog)
        ;(focusables[0] ?? dialog).focus()
      },
      { injector: this.injector },
    )
  }
// … truncated — 6 more members: attachPortal, detachPortal, syncScrollLock, addListeners, removeListeners, handleKeyDown; the full declaration is in node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/modal.component.d.ts
```

#### `MoleculeAlert`

Angular Alert UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-alert',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div role="alert" [class]="alertClasses" [style]="style" [attr.data-testid]="testId">
      @if (showIcon) {
        <span [class]="cm.alertIconWrapper">
          <span [innerHTML]="statusIconSvg"></span>
        </span>
      }
      <div [class]="cm.alertContent">
        @if (title) {
          <h5 [class]="titleClass">{{ title }}</h5>
        }
        <div [class]="descriptionClass">
          <ng-content></ng-content>
        </div>
      </div>
      @if (dismissible) {
        <button
          type="button"
          (click)="dismiss.emit()"
          [class]="cm.alertDismiss"
          [attr.aria-label]="dismissAriaLabel"
        >
          <span [innerHTML]="dismissIconSvg"></span>
        </button>
      }
    </div>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Alert class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Alert` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeAlert {
  @Input() title?: string
  @Input() status: ColorVariant = 'info'
  @Input() variant?: 'solid' | 'subtle' | 'outline' | 'left-accent'
  @Input() dismissible = false
  @Input() dismissLabel?: string
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  @Output() dismiss = new EventEmitter<void>()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the accessible dismiss button label.
   * @returns The localized aria-label for the dismiss button.
   */
  get dismissAriaLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.dismissLabel || this.i18n.t('ui.alert.dismiss', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Dismiss' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the alert title.
   * @returns The title class name string.
   */
  get titleClass(): string {
    return this.cm.alertTitle
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the alert description.
   * @returns The description class name string.
   */
  get descriptionClass(): string {
    return this.cm.alertDescription
  }

  /**
   * Determines the icon type based on the alert status.
   * @returns The mapped icon type string.
   */
  get iconType(): string {
    return statusVariantMap[this.status] || 'default'
  }

  /**
   * Determines whether the status icon should be shown.
   * @returns True if the icon type is not default.
   */
  get showIcon(): boolean {
    return this.iconType !== 'default'
  }

  private statusIconMap: Record<string, IconName> = {
    info: 'info-circle',
    success: 'check-circle',
    warning: 'exclamation-triangle',
    error: 'x-circle',
  }

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the status icon.
   * @returns The SVG string for the current status icon.
   */
  get statusIconSvg(): string {
    const name = this.statusIconMap[this.iconType]
    return name ? getIconSvg(name, this.cm.iconMd) : ''
  }

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the dismiss icon.
   * @returns The SVG string for the dismiss button icon.
   */
  get dismissIconSvg(): string {
    return getIconSvg('x-mark', this.cm.iconSm)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the alert container.
   * @returns The alert container class name string.
   */
  get alertClasses(): string {
    const cmVariant = statusVariantMap[this.status] || 'default'
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.alert({ variant: cmVariant }), this.className)
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeBadge`

Angular Badge UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-badge',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <span [class]="badgeClasses" [style]="style" [attr.data-testid]="testId">
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    </span>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Badge class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Badge` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeBadge {
  @Input() color: ColorVariant = 'primary'
  @Input() variant: 'solid' | 'outline' | 'subtle' = 'solid'
  @Input() size?: Size
  @Input() rounded = true
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  private get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the badge element.
   * @returns The badge class name string.
   */
  get badgeClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(
      this.cm.badge({ variant: this.color, size: this.size }),
      !this.rounded && this.cm.badgeSquare,
      this.className,
    )
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeButton`

Angular Button UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

React's root is the native `<button>`, so the button element of the
template renders in front of the host (`placeElementBefore`) and the host
itself leaves the page: the rendered tree is React's, with no wrapper
element around the button.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-button',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  template: `
    <button
      #root
      [type]="type"
      [class]="classes"
      [style]="style"
      [attr.data-testid]="testId"
      [disabled]="disabled || loading"
      [attr.aria-busy]="loading"
      (click)="handleClick.emit($event)"
    >
      @if (loading) {
        <span [class]="cm.buttonSpinner"></span>
      }
      @if (loading && loadingText) {
        {{ loadingText }}
      } @else {
        <ng-content></ng-content>
      }
    </button>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Button class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Button` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeButton {
  @Input() variant: ButtonVariant = 'solid'
  @Input() color: ColorVariant = 'primary'
  @Input() size: Size = 'md'
  @Input() loading = false
  @Input() loadingText?: string
  @Input() fullWidth = false
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string
  @Input() disabled = false
  @Input() type: 'button' | 'submit' | 'reset' = 'button'

  @Output() handleClick = new EventEmitter<MouseEvent>()

  /** The native button React renders as the component's root. */
  @ViewChild('root') root?: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /** Renders the button in front of the host, which leaves the page. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement(() => {
      const root = this.root?.nativeElement
      if (!root) return
      placeElementBefore(root, this.host.nativeElement)
      setElementPresent(this.host.nativeElement, false)
    })
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the button element.
   * @returns The button class name string.
   */
  get classes(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(
      this.cm.button({
        variant: this.variant,
        color: this.color,
        size: this.size,
        fullWidth: this.fullWidth,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeCheckbox`

Angular Checkbox UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-checkbox',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div [class]="cm.formFieldWrapper">
      <label [class]="cm.controlLabel">
        <input
          type="checkbox"
          [id]="checkboxId"
          [name]="name"
          [checked]="checked"
          [disabled]="disabled"
          [attr.data-state]="checked ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'"
          [attr.aria-invalid]="!!error"
          [class]="checkboxClasses"
          [style]="style"
          [attr.data-testid]="testId"
          (change)="handleChange.emit($event)"
        />
        @if (label) {
          <span [class]="labelClasses">{{ label }}</span>
        }
      </label>
      @if (error) {
        <p [class]="errorClasses">{{ error }}</p>
      }
    </div>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Checkbox class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Checkbox` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeCheckbox {
  @Input() label?: string
  @Input() checked = false
  @Input() indeterminate = false
  @Input() size?: Size
  @Input() error?: string
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string
  @Input() disabled = false
  @Input() name?: string
  @Input() id?: string

  @Output() handleChange = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Resolves the checkbox input element ID.
   * @returns The resolved ID from the id or name input.
   */
  get checkboxId(): string {
    return this.id || this.name || ''
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the checkbox input.
   * @returns The checkbox class name string.
   */
  get checkboxClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.checkbox({ error: !!this.error }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the checkbox label text.
   * @returns The label class name string.
   */
  get labelClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.controlText, this.disabled && this.cm.controlDisabled)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the error message.
   * @returns The error class name string.
   */
  get errorClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.formError, this.cm.sp('mt', 1))
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeInput`

Angular Input UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-input',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div [class]="cm.inputWrapper">
      @if (label) {
        <label [attr.for]="inputId" [class]="labelClasses">
          {{ label }}
        </label>
      }
      <div [class]="cm.inputInner">
        <input
          [type]="type"
          [id]="inputId"
          [name]="name"
          [value]="value"
          [placeholder]="placeholder"
          [disabled]="disabled"
          [required]="required"
          [readOnly]="readOnly"
          [attr.aria-invalid]="!!error"
          [attr.aria-describedby]="ariaDescribedBy"
          [class]="inputClasses"
          [style]="style"
          [attr.data-testid]="testId"
          [attr.maxlength]="maxLength"
          [attr.minlength]="minLength"
          [attr.pattern]="pattern"
          [attr.autocomplete]="autoComplete"
          (input)="handleInput.emit($event)"
          (change)="handleChange.emit($event)"
          (focus)="handleFocus.emit($event)"
          (blur)="handleBlur.emit($event)"
        />
        @if (clearable && value) {
          <div [class]="cm.inputClearButton">
            <button
              type="button"
              (click)="handleClear.emit()"
              [class]="cm.inputClearButton"
              [attr.aria-label]="clearAriaLabel"
            >
              <span [innerHTML]="clearIconSvg"></span>
            </button>
          </div>
        }
      </div>
      @if (error) {
        <p [id]="inputId + '-error'" [class]="formErrorClass">
          {{ error }}
        </p>
      }
      @if (hint && !error) {
        <p [id]="inputId + '-hint'" [class]="formHintClass">
          {{ hint }}
        </p>
      }
    </div>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Input class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Input` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeInput {
  @Input() type: InputType = 'text'
  @Input() size: Size = 'md'
  @Input() label?: string
  @Input() error?: string
  @Input() hint?: string
  @Input() clearable = false
  @Input() clearLabel?: string
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string
  @Input() disabled = false
  @Input() required = false
  @Input() readOnly = false
  @Input() value?: string | number
  @Input() placeholder?: string
  @Input() name?: string
  @Input() id?: string
  @Input() maxLength?: number
  @Input() minLength?: number
  @Input() pattern?: string
  @Input() autoComplete?: string

  @Output() handleInput = new EventEmitter<Event>()
  @Output() handleChange = new EventEmitter<Event>()
  @Output() handleFocus = new EventEmitter<FocusEvent>()
  @Output() handleBlur = new EventEmitter<FocusEvent>()
  @Output() handleClear = new EventEmitter<void>()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the accessible clear button label.
   * @returns The localized aria-label for the clear button.
   */
  get clearAriaLabel(): string {
    return this.clearLabel || this.i18n.t('ui.input.clear', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Clear' })
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for form error messages.
   * @returns The form error class name string.
   */
  get formErrorClass(): string {
    return this.cm.formError
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for form hint messages.
   * @returns The form hint class name string.
   */
  get formHintClass(): string {
    return this.cm.formHint
  }

  /**
   * Resolves the input element ID.
   * @returns The resolved ID from the id or name input.
   */
  get inputId(): string {
    return this.id || this.name || ''
  }

  /**
   * Computes the aria-describedby attribute value.
   * @returns The ID of the describing element, or null if none.
   */
  get ariaDescribedBy(): string | null {
    if (this.error) return `${this.inputId}-error`
    if (this.hint) return `${this.inputId}-hint`
    return null
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the input label.
   * @returns The label class name string.
   */
  get labelClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.label({ required: this.required }), this.cm.labelBlock)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the input element.
   * @returns The input class name string.
   */
  get inputClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(
      this.cm.input({ error: !!this.error, size: this.size }),
      this.clearable && this.cm.inputPadRight,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the clear button icon.
   * @returns The SVG string for the clear icon.
   */
  get clearIconSvg(): string {
    return getIconSvg('x-mark', this.cm.iconSm)
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeModal`

Angular Modal UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

`centered` (default `true`) vertically centers the dialog; `centered:
false` top-anchors it instead, via `getModalWrapperStyle()`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-modal',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    @if (open) {
      <!-- Overlay background -->
      <div [class]="overlayClass" aria-hidden="true"></div>

      <!-- Centering wrapper -->
      <div [class]="wrapperClass" [style]="wrapperStyle" (click)="onOverlayClick($event)">
        <!-- Content -->
        <div
          role="dialog"
          aria-modal="true"
          [attr.aria-labelledby]="title ? 'modal-title' : null"
          [class]="contentClasses"
          [style]="style"
          [attr.data-testid]="testId"
          (click)="$event.stopPropagation()"
        >
          @if (title || showCloseButton) {
            <div [class]="headerClass">
              @if (title) {
                <h2 id="modal-title" [class]="titleClass">{{ title }}</h2>
              } @else if (showCloseButton) {
                <div></div>
              }
              @if (showCloseButton) {
                <button
                  type="button"
                  (click)="close.emit()"
                  [class]="closeClass"
                  [attr.aria-label]="closeAriaLabel"
                >
                  <span [innerHTML]="closeIconSvg"></span>
                </button>
              }
            </div>
          }

          <div [class]="bodyClass">
            <ng-content></ng-content>
          </div>

          @if (hasFooter) {
            <div [class]="footerClass">
              <ng-content select="[modal-footer]"></ng-content>
            </div>
          }
        </div>
      </div>
    }
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Modal class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Modal` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeModal implements OnInit, OnDestroy, OnChanges {
  @Input() open = false
  @Input() title?: string
  @Input() size: ModalSize = 'md'
  @Input() showCloseButton = true
  @Input() closeLabel?: string
  @Input() closeOnOverlayClick = true
  @Input() closeOnEscape = true
  @Input() centered = true
  @Input() preventScroll = true
  @Input() hasFooter = false
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  @Output() close = new EventEmitter<void>()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the accessible close button label.
   * @returns The localized aria-label for the close button.
   */
  get closeAriaLabel(): string {
    return this.closeLabel || this.i18n.t('ui.modal.close', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Close' })
  }

  private boundEscapeHandler = this.handleEscape.bind(this)

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the modal wrapper.
   * @returns The wrapper class name string.
   */
  get wrapperClass(): string {
    return this.cm.dialogWrapper
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the inline style for the modal centering wrapper.
   * @returns An inline style object, or `undefined` when centered.
   */
  get wrapperStyle(): Record<string, string> | undefined {
    return getModalWrapperStyle(this.centered)
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the modal body.
   * @returns The body class name string.
   */
  get bodyClass(): string {
    return this.cm.dialogBody
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the modal overlay.
   * @returns The overlay class name string.
   */
  get overlayClass(): string {
    return this.cm.dialogOverlay
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the modal header.
   * @returns The header class name string.
   */
  get headerClass(): string {
    return this.cm.dialogHeader
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the modal footer.
   * @returns The footer class name string.
   */
  get footerClass(): string {
    return this.cm.dialogFooter
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the modal title.
   * @returns The title class name string.
   */
  get titleClass(): string {
    return this.cm.dialogTitle
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the close button.
   * @returns The close button class name string.
   */
  get closeClass(): string {
    return this.cm.dialogClose
  }

  private sanitizer = inject(DomSanitizer)

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the close button icon.
   * @returns The sanitized SVG for the close icon.
   */
  get closeIconSvg(): SafeHtml {
    return this.sanitizer.bypassSecurityTrustHtml(getIconSvg('x-mark', this.cm.iconMd))
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the modal content.
   * @returns The modal content class name string.
   */
  get contentClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.modal({ size: this.size }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Initializes event listeners on component creation.
   */
  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.updateListeners()
  }

  /**
   * Handles input property changes.
   * @param changes - The changed input properties.
   */
  ngOnChanges(changes: SimpleChanges): void {
    if (changes['open']) {
      this.updateListeners()
      this.updateBodyScroll()
    }
  }

  /**
   * Cleans up event listeners and body scroll on component destruction.
   */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    document.removeEventListener('keydown', this.boundEscapeHandler)
    if (this.preventScroll) {
      document.body.style.overflow = ''
    }
  }

  /**
   * Adds or removes the keyboard escape listener based on open state.
   */
  private updateListeners(): void {
    if (this.open) {
      document.addEventListener('keydown', this.boundEscapeHandler)
    } else {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', this.boundEscapeHandler)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Updates the document body overflow based on open state.
   */
  private updateBodyScroll(): void {
    if (this.preventScroll) {
      document.body.style.overflow = this.open ? 'hidden' : ''
    }
  }

  /**
   * Handles escape key press to close the modal.
   * @param e - The keyboard event.
   */
  private handleEscape(e: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if (this.closeOnEscape && e.key === 'Escape') {
      this.close.emit()
    }
  }

  /**
   * Handles overlay click to close the modal.
   * @param event - The mouse event from the overlay click.
   */
  onOverlayClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    if (this.closeOnOverlayClick && event.target === event.currentTarget) {
      this.close.emit()
    }
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeRadioGroup`

Angular RadioGroup UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-radio-group',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div
      [class]="className"
      [style]="style"
      [attr.data-testid]="testId"
      role="radiogroup"
      [attr.aria-label]="label"
    >
      @if (label) {
        <div [class]="groupLabelClasses">{{ label }}</div>
      }
      <div [class]="directionClasses">
        @for (option of options; track option.value) {
          <label [class]="getOptionLabelClasses(option)">
            <input
              type="radio"
              [name]="label"
              [value]="option.value"
              [checked]="value === option.value"
              [disabled]="disabled || option.disabled"
              [attr.data-state]="value === option.value ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'"
              [class]="radioClasses"
              (change)="onRadioChange(option.value)"
            />
            <span [class]="cm.controlText">{{ option.label }}</span>
          </label>
        }
      </div>
      @if (error) {
        <p [class]="errorClasses">{{ error }}</p>
      }
    </div>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Radio Group class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `RadioGroup` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeRadioGroup {
  @Input() options: RadioOption<string>[] = []
  @Input() value?: string
  @Input() size?: Size
  @Input() label?: string
  @Input() direction: 'horizontal' | 'vertical' = 'vertical'
  @Input() error?: string
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string
  @Input() disabled = false

  @Output() valueChange = new EventEmitter<string>()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the radio group label.
   * @returns The group label class name string.
   */
  get groupLabelClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.label({}), this.cm.radioGroupLabel)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the layout direction.
   * @returns The direction layout class name string.
   */
  get directionClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.radioGroupLayout(this.direction)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for radio input elements.
   * @returns The radio input class name string.
   */
  get radioClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.radio({ error: !!this.error }), this.cm.cursorPointer)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the error message.
   * @returns The error class name string.
   */
  get errorClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.formError, this.cm.sp('mt', 1))
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for an individual radio option label.
   * @param option - The radio option to compute classes for.
   * @returns The option label class name string.
   */
  getOptionLabelClasses(option: RadioOption<string>): string {
    const isDisabled = this.disabled || option.disabled
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.controlLabel, isDisabled && this.cm.controlDisabled)
  }

  /**
   * Handles radio option selection change.
   * @param optionValue - The value of the selected radio option.
   */
  onRadioChange(optionValue: string): void {
    this.valueChange.emit(optionValue)
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeSelect`

Angular Select UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-select',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div [class]="cm.inputWrapper">
      @if (label) {
        <label [attr.for]="selectId" [class]="labelClasses">
          {{ label }}
        </label>
      }
      <div [class]="cm.inputInner">
        <select
          [id]="selectId"
          [name]="name"
          [value]="value"
          [disabled]="disabled"
          [required]="required"
          [attr.aria-invalid]="!!error"
          [attr.aria-describedby]="ariaDescribedBy"
          [class]="selectClasses"
          [attr.data-testid]="testId"
          (change)="onSelectChange($event)"
        >
          @if (placeholder) {
            <option value="" disabled>{{ placeholder }}</option>
          }
          @if (clearable) {
            <option value="">--</option>
          }
          @for (option of options; track option.value) {
            <option [value]="option.value" [disabled]="option.disabled">
              {{ option.label }}
            </option>
          }
        </select>
      </div>
      @if (error) {
        <p [id]="selectId + '-error'" [class]="formErrorClass">
          {{ error }}
        </p>
      }
      @if (hint && !error) {
        <p [id]="selectId + '-hint'" [class]="formHintClass">
          {{ hint }}
        </p>
      }
    </div>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Select class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Select` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeSelect {
  @Input() options: SelectOption<string>[] = []
  @Input() value?: string
  @Input() size: Size = 'md'
  @Input() label?: string
  @Input() placeholder?: string
  @Input() error?: string
  @Input() hint?: string
  @Input() clearable = false
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() testId?: string
  @Input() disabled = false
  @Input() required = false
  @Input() name?: string
  @Input() id?: string

  @Output() valueChange = new EventEmitter<string>()
  @Output() handleChange = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for form error messages.
   * @returns The form error class name string.
   */
  get formErrorClass(): string {
    return this.cm.formError
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for form hint messages.
   * @returns The form hint class name string.
   */
  get formHintClass(): string {
    return this.cm.formHint
  }

  /**
   * Resolves the select element ID.
   * @returns The resolved ID from the id or name input.
   */
  get selectId(): string {
    return this.id || this.name || ''
  }

  /**
   * Computes the aria-describedby attribute value.
   * @returns The ID of the describing element, or null if none.
   */
  get ariaDescribedBy(): string | null {
    if (this.error) return `${this.selectId}-error`
    if (this.hint) return `${this.selectId}-hint`
    return null
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the select label.
   * @returns The label class name string.
   */
  get labelClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.label({ required: this.required }), this.cm.labelBlock)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the select element.
   * @returns The select class name string.
   */
  get selectClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(
      this.cm.select({ error: !!this.error, size: this.size }),
      this.cm.selectNative,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Handles select value change events.
   * @param event - The change event from the select element.
   */
  onSelectChange(event: Event): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLSelectElement
    this.handleChange.emit(event)
    this.valueChange.emit(target.value)
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeSpinner`

Angular Spinner UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-spinner',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div
      role="status"
      [attr.aria-label]="spinnerLabel"
      [class]="spinnerClasses"
      [style]="computedStyle"
      [attr.data-testid]="testId"
    >
      @if (label) {
        <span [class]="cm.srOnly">{{ label }}</span>
      }
    </div>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Spinner class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Spinner` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeSpinner {
  @Input() size: Size = 'md'
  @Input() color?: ColorVariant | string
  @Input() label?: string
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the accessible spinner label.
   * @returns The localized aria-label for the spinner.
   */
  get spinnerLabel(): string {
    return this.label || this.i18n.t('ui.spinner.loading', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Loading' })
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the spinner element.
   * @returns The spinner class name string.
   */
  get spinnerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.spinner({ size: this.size }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the merged inline styles including custom color.
   * @returns The computed style object with optional custom border color.
   */
  get computedStyle(): Record<string, string> {
    const base: Record<string, string> = { ...this.style }
    if (
      this.color &&
      typeof this.color === 'string' &&
      !['primary', 'secondary', 'success', 'warning', 'error', 'info'].includes(this.color)
    ) {
      base['borderColor'] = this.color
      base['borderTopColor'] = 'transparent'
    }
    return base
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeSwitch`

Angular Switch UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-switch',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <label [class]="wrapperClasses">
      <button
        type="button"
        role="switch"
        [attr.aria-checked]="checked"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.data-state]="state"
        [class]="switchClasses"
        [style]="style"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        (click)="onToggle()"
      >
        <span [attr.data-state]="state" [class]="thumbClasses"></span>
      </button>
      @if (label) {
        <span [class]="cm.controlText">{{ label }}</span>
      }
    </label>
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Switch class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Switch` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeSwitch {
  @Input() label?: string
  @Input() checked = false
  @Input() size: Size = 'md'
  @Input() color?: ColorVariant
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string
  @Input() disabled = false

  @Output() checkedChange = new EventEmitter<boolean>()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the current checked/unchecked state string.
   * @returns The state string, either "checked" or "unchecked".
   */
  get state(): string {
    return this.checked ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the switch wrapper label.
   * @returns The wrapper class name string.
   */
  get wrapperClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.controlLabel, this.disabled && this.cm.controlDisabled)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the switch button.
   * @returns The switch class name string.
   */
  get switchClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.switchBase({ size: this.size }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the CSS classes for the switch thumb indicator.
   * @returns The thumb class name string.
   */
  get thumbClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.switchThumb({ size: this.size })
  }

  /**
   * Handles the switch toggle action.
   */
  onToggle(): void {
    if (!this.disabled) {
      this.checkedChange.emit(!this.checked)
    }
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeToast`

Angular Toast UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-toast',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    @if (isVisible()) {
      <div
        role="alert"
        data-state="open"
        [class]="toastClasses"
        [style]="style"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
      >
        @if (showIcon) {
          <span [class]="cm.toastIconWrapper">
            <span [innerHTML]="statusIconSvg"></span>
          </span>
        }
        <div [class]="cm.toastContentWrapper">
          @if (title) {
            <div [class]="titleClass">{{ title }}</div>
          }
          @if (description) {
            <div [class]="descriptionClass">{{ description }}</div>
          } @else {
            <div [class]="descriptionClass">
              <ng-content></ng-content>
            </div>
          }
        </div>
        @if (dismissible) {
          <button
            type="button"
            (click)="onDismiss()"
            [class]="closeButtonClass"
            [attr.aria-label]="closeAriaLabel"
          >
            <span [innerHTML]="dismissIconSvg"></span>
          </button>
        }
      </div>
    }
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Toast class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Toast` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeToast implements OnInit, OnDestroy {
  @Input() title?: string
  @Input() description?: string
  @Input() status: ColorVariant = 'info'
  @Input() duration = 5000
  @Input() dismissible = true
  @Input() closeLabel?: string
  @Input() position: 'top' | 'top-right' | 'top-left' | 'bottom' | 'bottom-right' | 'bottom-left' =
    'bottom-right'
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  @Output() dismiss = new EventEmitter<void>()

  /** Whether the toast shows; a signal, so the auto-dismiss timer's change renders (OnPush). */
  readonly isVisible = signal(true)
  private timer?: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the accessible close button label.
   * @returns The localized aria-label for the close button.
   */
  get closeAriaLabel(): string {
    return this.closeLabel || this.i18n.t('ui.toast.close', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Close' })
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the toast title.
   * @returns The title class name string.
   */
  get titleClass(): string {
    return this.cm.toastTitle
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the toast description.
   * @returns The description class name string.
   */
  get descriptionClass(): string {
    return this.cm.toastDescription
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the CSS class for the close button.
   * @returns The close button class name string.
   */
  get closeButtonClass(): string {
    return this.cm.toastClose
  }

  /**
   * Determines the icon type based on the toast status.
   * @returns The mapped icon type string.
   */
  get iconType(): string {
    return statusVariantMap[this.status] || 'default'
  }

  /**
   * Determines whether the status icon should be shown.
   * @returns True if the icon type is not default.
   */
  get showIcon(): boolean {
    return this.iconType !== 'default'
  }

  private statusIconMap: Record<string, IconName> = {
    info: 'info-circle',
    success: 'check-circle',
    warning: 'exclamation-triangle',
    error: 'x-circle',
  }

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the status icon.
   * @returns The SVG string for the current status icon.
   */
  get statusIconSvg(): string {
    const name = this.statusIconMap[this.iconType]
    return name ? getIconSvg(name, this.cm.iconMd) : ''
  }

  /**
   * Generates the SVG markup for the dismiss icon.
   * @returns The SVG string for the dismiss button icon.
   */
  get dismissIconSvg(): string {
    return getIconSvg('x-mark', this.cm.iconSm)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the toast container.
   * @returns The toast class name string.
   */
  get toastClasses(): string {
    const variant = statusVariantMap[this.status] || 'default'
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.toast({ variant }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Starts the auto-dismiss timer on component creation.
   */
  ngOnInit(): void {
    if (this.duration > 0) {
      this.timer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.isVisible.set(false)
        this.dismiss.emit()
      }, this.duration)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Clears the auto-dismiss timer on component destruction.
   */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.timer) {
      clearTimeout(this.timer)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Handles the dismiss action by hiding the toast.
   */
  onDismiss(): void {
    this.isVisible.set(false)
    this.dismiss.emit()
  }
}
```

#### `MoleculeTooltip`

Angular Tooltip UI component with UIClassMap-driven styling.

Wraps child content and shows a tooltip on hover/focus. `hasArrow` renders
a small themed pointer at the resolved `placement` edge.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-tooltip',
  standalone: true,
  changeDetection: ChangeDetectionStrategy.OnPush,
  template: `
    <div
      #trigger
      [class]="cm.tooltipTrigger"
      (mouseenter)="show()"
      (mouseleave)="hide()"
      (focus)="show()"
      (blur)="hide()"
    >
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    </div>
    @if (isVisible()) {
      <!--
        Outer positioned wrapper carries the top/left/z-index placement; the
        arrow renders as its SIBLING (not nested inside the visible box),
        because tooltipContent sets overflow-hidden — an arrow meant to poke
        past the box's own edge would be clipped invisible if it were a
        descendant of that element instead.
      -->
      <div [style]="positionStyle()">
        <div
          #tooltipEl
          role="tooltip"
          [class]="tooltipClasses"
          [style]="style"
          [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        >
          {{ content }}
        </div>
        @if (hasArrow) {
          <span aria-hidden="true" [style]="arrowStyle"></span>
        }
      </div>
    }
  `,
})
/**
 * Molecule Tooltip class.
 * @deprecated Use the React-named component `Tooltip` instead.
 */
export class MoleculeTooltip implements OnDestroy {
  @Input() content = ''
  @Input() placement: TooltipPlacement = 'top'
  @Input() delay = 0
  @Input() hasArrow = false
  @Input() className?: string
  @Input() style?: Record<string, string>
  @Input() testId?: string

  @ViewChild('trigger') triggerRef?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('tooltipEl') tooltipRef?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  /** Whether the tooltip shows; a signal, so the delay timer's change renders (OnPush). */
  readonly isVisible = signal(false)
  /** The measured placement; a signal, so the animation frame's measurement renders (OnPush). */
  readonly positionStyle = signal<Record<string, string>>({})
  private timeoutId?: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>

  /**
   * Retrieves the current UI class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap instance for styling.
   */
  get cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the combined CSS classes for the tooltip content.
   * @returns The tooltip class name string.
   */
  get tooltipClasses(): string {
    return this.cm.cn(this.cm.tooltipContent, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Retrieves the inline style for the tooltip's arrow element.
   * @returns Inline style positioning the rotated-square arrow.
   */
  get arrowStyle(): Record<string, string> {
    return getTooltipArrowStyle(this.placement)
  }

  /**
   * Shows the tooltip with optional delay.
   */
  show(): void {
    if (this.delay > 0) {
      this.timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
        this.isVisible.set(true)
        // Measured once the tooltip has rendered.
        requestAnimationFrame(() => this.updatePosition())
      }, this.delay)
    } else {
      this.isVisible.set(true)
      requestAnimationFrame(() => this.updatePosition())
    }
  }

  /**
   * Hides the tooltip and cancels any pending delay.
   */
  hide(): void {
    if (this.timeoutId) {
      clearTimeout(this.timeoutId)
    }
    this.isVisible.set(false)
  }

  /**
   * Clears pending timeouts on component destruction.
   */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    if (this.timeoutId) {
      clearTimeout(this.timeoutId)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Calculates and applies the tooltip position relative to the trigger.
   */
  private updatePosition(): void {
    if (!this.triggerRef?.nativeElement || !this.tooltipRef?.nativeElement) return

    const triggerRect = this.triggerRef.nativeElement.getBoundingClientRect()
    const tooltipRect = this.tooltipRef.nativeElement.getBoundingClientRect()
    const offset = 8

    const { top, left, width, height } = triggerRect
    const tooltipWidth = tooltipRect.width
    const tooltipHeight = tooltipRect.height

    const positions: Record<TooltipPlacement, { top: number; left: number }> = {
      top: {
        top: top + window.scrollY - tooltipHeight - offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width / 2 - tooltipWidth / 2,
      },
      bottom: {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height + offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width / 2 - tooltipWidth / 2,
      },
      left: {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height / 2 - tooltipHeight / 2,
        left: left + window.scrollX - tooltipWidth - offset,
      },
      right: {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height / 2 - tooltipHeight / 2,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width + offset,
      },
      'top-start': {
        top: top + window.scrollY - tooltipHeight - offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX,
      },
      'top-end': {
```
