```typescript
        top: top + window.scrollY - tooltipHeight - offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width - tooltipWidth,
      },
      'bottom-start': {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height + offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX,
      },
      'bottom-end': {
        top: top + window.scrollY + height + offset,
        left: left + window.scrollX + width - tooltipWidth,
      },
    }

    const pos = positions[this.placement]
    this.positionStyle.set({
      position: 'absolute',
      top: `${pos.top}px`,
      left: `${pos.left}px`,
      zIndex: '9999',
    })
  }
}
```

#### `OptimisticToggleButton`

OptimisticToggleButton component.

The host is the `<button>` itself (`button[mol-optimistic-toggle-button]`),
exactly React's root (it renders `Button`). The two labels are node-valued
props, projected as `[slot=activeLabel]` / `[slot=inactiveLabel]`; exactly
one is shown, moved onto the button with no wrapper, matching React's
`{toggle.value ? activeLabel : inactiveLabel}`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-optimistic-toggle-button]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'value',
    'commit',
    'activeLabel',
    'inactiveLabel',
    'onError',
    'variant',
    'color',
    'size',
    'fullWidth',
    'leftIcon',
    'rightIcon',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    // `value` is the loaded server state, which React keeps off the <button>; Angular would also keep
    // a static `value="…"` on the host as the button's form value.
    '[attr.value]': 'null',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.type]': '"button"',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[disabled]': 'disabled',
    '[attr.aria-pressed]': 'state().value ? "true" : "false"',
    '[attr.aria-busy]': 'state().pending ? "true" : "false"',
    '(click)': 'handleClick()',
  },
  template: `<span #leftSlot [class]="cm().buttonIconLeft"
      ><ng-content select="[slot=leftIcon]"></ng-content
      ><ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(leftIcon)"></ng-container
      >{{ nodeText(leftIcon) }}</span
    ><span #activeSlot
      ><ng-content select="[slot=activeLabel]"></ng-content
      ><ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(activeLabel)"></ng-container
      >{{ nodeText(activeLabel) }}</span
    ><span #inactiveSlot
      ><ng-content select="[slot=inactiveLabel]"></ng-content
      ><ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(inactiveLabel)"></ng-container
      >{{ nodeText(inactiveLabel) }}</span
    ><span #rightSlot [class]="cm().buttonIconRight"
      ><ng-content select="[slot=rightIcon]"></ng-content
      ><ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(rightIcon)"></ng-container
      >{{ nodeText(rightIcon) }}</span
    >`,
})
export class OptimisticToggleButton implements DoCheck, OnDestroy, HasElement<HTMLButtonElement> {
  /** The loaded server value. */
  value: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['value'] = false
  /** Saves the desired boolean and returns the server-confirmed boolean. */
  commit?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['commit']
  /** Label when the displayed value is true (node-valued prop). */
  activeLabel!: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['activeLabel']
  /** Label when the displayed value is false (node-valued prop). */
  inactiveLabel!: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['inactiveLabel']
  /** Receives a failed save after the controller has restored the last confirmed value. */
  onError?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['onError']
  /** Button variant. */
  variant?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['variant']
  /** Button color. */
  color?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['color']
  /** Button size. */
  size?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['size']
  /** Whether the button stretches to fill its container. */
  fullWidth?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['fullWidth'] = false
  /** Left icon (node-valued prop). */
  leftIcon?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['leftIcon']
  /** Right icon (node-valued prop). */
  rightIcon?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['rightIcon']
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['className']
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['style']
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['testId']
  /** Disables the button (permissions, never a pending save). */
  disabled?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['disabled'] = false
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`; default `optimistic-toggle`). */
  automationId?: OptimisticToggleButtonProps['automationId']
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /** Template helpers (templates see only class members). */
  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  /** Renders a plain-string/number node value. */
  readonly nodeText = nodeText

  /** The optimistic controller. */
  private readonly controller: OptimisticValue<boolean>
  /** The controller state (a signal so the zoneless view re-checks on every publish). */
  readonly state = signal<OptimisticValueState<boolean>>({
    value: false,
    pending: false,
    error: null,
  })

  /** The left icon wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('leftSlot') leftSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The active-label capture span. */
  @ViewChild('activeSlot') activeSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The inactive-label capture span. */
  @ViewChild('inactiveSlot') inactiveSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The right icon wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('rightSlot') rightSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<button>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)
  private unsubscribe?: () => void
  private lastReceived = false
  private hasLeft = false
  private hasRight = false
  /** The slot whose content is currently shown on the button. */
  private visibleFrom: HTMLSpanElement | null = null

  /** Creates the optimistic controller and mirrors its state into the signal. */
  constructor() {
    this.controller = createOptimisticValue<boolean>({
      value: this.value,
      commit: (next) => this.commit?.(next) ?? Promise.resolve(next),
      onError: (error) => this.onError?.(error),
    })
    this.unsubscribe = this.controller.subscribe((next) => this.state.set({ ...next }))
    this.state.set(this.controller.getState())
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.reconcile(hasContent))
  }

  /** The current class map. */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /** Computes the button class list. */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().button({
        variant: this.variant ?? 'solid',
        color: this.color ?? 'primary',
        size: this.size ?? 'md',
        fullWidth: this.fullWidth,
      }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /** Serializes the inline style. */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  molId(): string {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      'optimistic-toggle'
    )
  }

  /** Flips the displayed value and saves the newest desired value. */
  handleClick(): void {
    this.controller.update((current) => !current)
  }

  /** Reads slot presence and takes the label capture spans out of the DOM, then shows the visible label. */
  private reconcile(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.hasLeft = hasContent(this.leftSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasRight = hasContent(this.rightSlot?.nativeElement)
    setElementPresent(this.activeSlot?.nativeElement, false)
    setElementPresent(this.inactiveSlot?.nativeElement, false)
    setElementPresent(this.leftSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasLeft)
    setElementPresent(this.rightSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasRight)
    this.syncLabels()
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    if (this.value !== this.lastReceived) {
      this.lastReceived = this.value
      this.controller.sync(this.value)
    }
  }

  /** Stops mirroring controller state into the signal (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.unsubscribe?.()
  }

  private syncLabels(): void {
    const state = this.controller.getState()
    const shown = state.value
      ? (this.activeSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
      : (this.inactiveSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
    const hidden = state.value
      ? (this.inactiveSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
      : (this.activeSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
    if (hidden) placeContent(hidden, null)
    // The label goes before the right icon's place (its placeholder while the
    // icon is absent, which placeContent resolves), else at the end.
    if (shown) placeContent(shown, this.host.nativeElement, this.rightSlot?.nativeElement ?? null)
  }
}
```

_(Member comments are shortened to their first sentence, and the 3 longest are left out to fit; the full declaration is in `node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/optimistic-toggle-button.component.d.ts`.)_

#### `PageHeader`

PageHeader component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-page-header]`), exactly React's
root. `actions` is a node-valued prop, projected as `[slot=actions]`; its
wrapper `<div>` renders only when the slot carries nodes (React's
`{actions ? <div>…</div> : null}`), implemented by detaching the wrapper
from the DOM — the projected nodes need an always-mounted capture point.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-page-header]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  inputs: ['title', 'description', 'breadcrumbs', 'className', 'style', 'testId', 'automationId'],
  host: {
    // `title` is this component's heading, which React renders as text; Angular would also keep a
    // static `title="…"` on the host, where the browser shows it as a tooltip.
    '[attr.title]': 'null',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'automationId',
  },
  template: `@if (breadcrumbs && breadcrumbs.length > 0) {
      <nav [class]="cm().pageHeaderBreadcrumbs" [attr.aria-label]="breadcrumbLabel()">
        @for (crumb of breadcrumbs; track $index) {
          @if ($index > 0) {
            <span [class]="cm().pageHeaderBreadcrumbSeparator" aria-hidden="true">/</span>
          }
          @if (crumb.href) {
            <a [href]="crumb.href" [class]="cm().pageHeaderBreadcrumbItem">{{ crumb.label }}</a>
          } @else {
            <span [class]="cm().pageHeaderBreadcrumbItem">{{ crumb.label }}</span>
          }
        }
      </nav>
    }
    <div [class]="cm().flex({ justify: 'between', align: 'center' })">
      <div>
        <h1 [class]="cm().pageHeaderTitle">{{ title }}</h1>
        @if (description) {
          <p [class]="cm().pageHeaderDescription">{{ description }}</p>
        }
      </div>
      <div #actionsSlot [class]="cm().pageHeaderActions">
        <ng-content select="[slot=actions]"></ng-content>
      </div>
    </div>`,
})
export class PageHeader implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** Page title. */
  title = ''
  /** Page description. */
  description?: string
  /** Breadcrumb trail (empty or absent renders no nav). */
  breadcrumbs?: Breadcrumb[]
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id. */
  automationId?: string

  /** The actions wrapper (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('actionsSlot') actionsSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the label on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)
  private hasActions = false

  /** Applies React's `{actions ? <div>…</div> : null}`. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.syncActions(hasContent))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().pageHeader, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The breadcrumb nav's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  breadcrumbLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('ui.pageHeader.breadcrumbs', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Breadcrumb' })
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{actions ? <div>…</div> : null}`.
   *
   * @param hasContent - Reads whether a slot has content.
   */
  private syncActions(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.hasActions = hasContent(this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement)
    setElementPresent(this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasActions)
  }
}
```

#### `PageShell`

PageShell component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-page-shell]`). `sidebar` and
`topbar` are node-valued props projected as `[slot=sidebar]` /
`[slot=topbar]`; each wrapper element (`<aside>`, `<header>`) renders only
when React would render it (`{sidebar ? … : null}`), implemented by
detaching the always-mounted capture element from the DOM.

The sidebar toggle button renders only when the caller passed
`onSidebarToggle` (React's `{onSidebarToggle ? … : null}`); with an
`@Output`, that presence is the output's subscriber count.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-page-shell]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  inputs: ['sidebar', 'topbar', 'sidebarCollapsed', 'className', 'style', 'testId', 'automationId'],
  outputs: ['sidebarToggle'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'automationId',
  },
  template: `<aside #sidebarSlot [class]="sidebarClasses()">
      @if (toggleVisible()) {
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="cm().pageShellSidebarToggle"
          (click)="sidebarToggle.emit($event)"
          [attr.aria-label]="toggleLabel()"
        >
          {{ sidebarCollapsed ? '▶' : '◀' }}
        </button>
      }
      <ng-content select="[slot=sidebar]"></ng-content>
    </aside>
    <div [class]="cm().flex1">
      <header #topbarSlot [class]="cm().pageShellTopbar">
        <ng-content select="[slot=topbar]"></ng-content>
      </header>
      <main [class]="cm().pageShellContent"><ng-content></ng-content></main>
    </div>`,
})
export class PageShell implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** Sidebar content (node-valued prop). */
  sidebar?: unknown
  /** Top bar content (node-valued prop). */
  topbar?: unknown
  /** Whether the sidebar is collapsed. */
  sidebarCollapsed = false
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id. */
  automationId?: string

  /** Fires when the sidebar toggle button is pressed (React's `onSidebarToggle`). */
  @Output() readonly sidebarToggle = new EventEmitter<MouseEvent>()

  /** The sidebar wrapper (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('sidebarSlot') sidebarSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>
  /** The top bar wrapper (always in the template; detached when unused). */
  @ViewChild('topbarSlot') topbarSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)
  private hasSidebar = false
  private hasTopbar = false

  /** Applies React's `{sidebar ? … : null}` / `{topbar ? … : null}`. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.syncWrappers(hasContent))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().pageShell, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Computes the sidebar wrapper class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  sidebarClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().pageShellSidebar,
      this.sidebarCollapsed && this.cm().pageShellSidebarCollapsed,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Whether the toggle button renders (React renders it only when
   * `onSidebarToggle` is given; a bound output means the caller listens).
   * @returns True when a listener is attached.
   */
  toggleVisible(): boolean {
    return this.sidebarToggle.observed
  }

  /**
   * The toggle button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  toggleLabel(): string {
    return this.sidebarCollapsed
      ? this.i18n.t('ui.pageShell.expandSidebar', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Expand sidebar' })
      : this.i18n.t('ui.pageShell.collapseSidebar', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Collapse sidebar' })
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * Applies React's `{sidebar ? … : null}` / `{topbar ? … : null}`.
   *
   * @param hasContent - Reads whether a slot has content.
   */
  private syncWrappers(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.hasSidebar = hasContent(this.sidebarSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasTopbar = hasContent(this.topbarSlot?.nativeElement)
    setElementPresent(this.sidebarSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasSidebar)
    setElementPresent(this.topbarSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasTopbar)
  }
}
```

#### `Pagination`

Pagination component.

The host is the `<nav>` (`nav[mol-pagination]`), exactly React's root.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'nav[mol-pagination]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: [
    'page',
    'totalPages',
    'siblings',
    'boundaries',
    'size',
    'showFirstLast',
    'showPrevNext',
    'labels',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  outputs: ['change'],
  host: {
    role: 'navigation',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'navLabel()',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `<ul [class]="cm().paginationContent">
    @if (showFirstLast) {
      <li>
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="buttonClasses(false)"
          (click)="handlePageChange(1)"
          [disabled]="page === 1 || !!disabled"
          [attr.aria-label]="firstLabel()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="icon('chevrons-left')" />
        </button>
      </li>
    }
    @if (showPrevNext) {
      <li>
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="buttonClasses(false)"
          (click)="handlePageChange(page - 1)"
          [disabled]="page === 1 || !!disabled"
          [attr.aria-label]="previousLabel()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="icon('chevron-left')" />
        </button>
      </li>
    }
    @for (item of range(); track $index) {
      @if (item === 'ellipsis') {
        <li>
          <span [class]="cm().paginationEllipsis" aria-hidden="true"
            ><svg mol-icon [data]="icon('ellipsis-horizontal')"
          /></span>
        </li>
      } @else {
        <li>
          <button
            type="button"
            [class]="buttonClasses(item === page)"
            (click)="handlePageChange(item)"
            [disabled]="!!disabled"
            [attr.aria-label]="pageLabel(item)"
            [attr.aria-current]="item === page ? 'page' : null"
          >
            {{ item }}
          </button>
        </li>
      }
    }
    @if (showPrevNext) {
      <li>
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="buttonClasses(false)"
          (click)="handlePageChange(page + 1)"
          [disabled]="page === totalPages || !!disabled"
          [attr.aria-label]="nextLabel()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="icon('chevron-right')" />
        </button>
      </li>
    }
    @if (showFirstLast) {
      <li>
        <button
          type="button"
          [class]="buttonClasses(false)"
          (click)="handlePageChange(totalPages)"
          [disabled]="page === totalPages || !!disabled"
          [attr.aria-label]="lastLabel()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="icon('chevrons-right')" />
        </button>
      </li>
    }
  </ul>`,
})
export class Pagination implements HasElement<HTMLElement> {
  /** The active page. */
  page = 1
  /** The total number of pages. */
  totalPages = 1
  /** Sibling pages around the active page (default 1). */
  siblings = 1
  /** Boundary pages at the start and end (default 1). */
  boundaries = 1
  /** Button size. */
  size: 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl' = 'md'
  /** Whether the first/last buttons render. */
  showFirstLast = false
  /** Whether the previous/next buttons render (default true). */
  showPrevNext = true
  /** Accessible labels. */
  labels?: PaginationLabels
  /** Extra classes for the nav. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables every button. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /** Fires with the requested page (React's `onChange`). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<number>()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<nav>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().paginationRoot, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the root's inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The generated page range.
   * @returns The range.
   */
  range(): (number | 'ellipsis')[] {
    return generatePaginationRange(this.page, this.totalPages, this.siblings, this.boundaries)
  }

  /**
   * A pagination button's class list.
   * @param isActive - Whether it is the active page.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  buttonClasses(isActive: boolean): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().pagination({ active: isActive, size: this.size }),
      this.cm().paginationInteractive,
    )
  }

  /**
   * A button glyph.
   * @param name - The icon name.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  icon(
    name:
      'chevrons-left' | 'chevron-left' | 'chevron-right' | 'chevrons-right' | 'ellipsis-horizontal',
  ): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon(name, this.cm().iconSm)
  }

  /**
   * The nav's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  navLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.nav ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.nav', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Pagination' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The first-page button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  firstLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.first ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.first', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Go to first page' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The previous-page button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  previousLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.previous ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.previous', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Go to previous page' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The next-page button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  nextLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.next ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.next', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Go to next page' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The last-page button's accessible label.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  lastLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.labels?.last ??
      this.i18n.t('ui.pagination.last', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Go to last page' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * A numbered page button's accessible label (a custom `labels.goToPage`
   * wins, then the localized default).
   *
   * @param item - The page number.
   * @returns The label.
   */
  pageLabel(item: number): string {
    const custom = this.labels?.goToPage?.(item)
    if (custom !== undefined && custom !== null) return custom
    return this.i18n.t(
      'ui.pagination.goToPage',
      { page: item },
      { defaultValue: 'Go to page {{page}}' },
    )
  }

  /**
   * Requests a page change (React's `handlePageChange`).
   * @param newPage - The requested page.
   */
  handlePageChange(newPage: number): void {
    if (newPage >= 1 && newPage <= this.totalPages && newPage !== this.page && !this.disabled) {
      this.change.emit(newPage)
    }
  }
}
```

#### `PanelCloseProvider`

Provides the drawer-close callback to descendants.

Rendered internally by `UserMenu` and `SidebarUserCard` around their panel
content; apps do not normally render it directly. React's context provider
renders its children and no DOM of its own, so the projected children
render in front of the host (`placeContentBefore`) and the host itself
leaves the page. The context is registered on the parent element — the one
whose child list the content now belongs to — so a consumer in the content
still resolves it from its DOM ancestors.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-panel-close-provider',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  inputs: ['close'],
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class PanelCloseProvider implements OnDestroy {
  /** Callback that dismisses the enclosing drawer/modal. */
  close?: PanelCloseProviderProps['close']

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The value consumers read; its closure keeps the CURRENT `close` prop. */
  private readonly value = { close: (): void => this.close?.() }

  /** Renders the children in front of the host and registers the context where they render. */
  constructor() {
    const host = this.host.nativeElement
    PanelCloseDomContext.provide(host, this.value)
    usePlacement(() => {
      placeContentBefore(host, host)
      setElementPresent(host, false)
    })
  }

  /** Removes the context registration (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    PanelCloseDomContext.remove(this.host.nativeElement)
  }
}
```

#### `Progress`

Progress component.

The host is the wrapper div React renders; `aria-valuenow` is clamped into
`[0, max]` to match the clamped visual bar.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-progress]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'value',
    'max',
    'size',
    'color',
    'showValue',
    'label',
    'indeterminate',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `@if (label || showValue) {
      <div [class]="cm().progressLabelContainer">
        @if (label) {
          <span [class]="cm().progressLabelText">{{ label }}</span>
        }
        @if (showValue) {
          <span [class]="cm().progressLabelText"
            ><ng-container>{{ rounded }}</ng-container
            ><ng-container>{{ '%' }}</ng-container></span
          >
        }
      </div>
    }
    <div
      [class]="cm().cn(cm().progress(), cm().progressHeight(size ?? 'md'))"
      role="progressbar"
      [attr.aria-valuenow]="indeterminate ? null : clamped"
      aria-valuemin="0"
      [attr.aria-valuemax]="max"
      [attr.aria-label]="label ?? progressLabel()"
    >
      <div [class]="barClasses()" [attr.style]="barStyle()"></div>
    </div>`,
})
export class Progress implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host wrapper `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Progress value (0–max). */
  value: ProgressProps['value'] = 0
  /** Maximum value. */
  max?: ProgressProps['max'] = 100
  /** Progress size. */
  size?: ProgressProps['size']
  /** Progress color. */
  color?: ProgressProps['color']
  /** Whether the numeric value label renders. */
  showValue?: ProgressProps['showValue']
  /** Accessible label. */
  label?: ProgressProps['label']
  /** Whether the progress is indeterminate. */
  indeterminate?: ProgressProps['indeterminate']
  /** Extra classes joined onto the wrapper classes. */
  className?: ProgressProps['className']
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: ProgressProps['style']
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: ProgressProps['testId']
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: ProgressProps['automationId']
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The percentage, clamped to [0, 100].
   * @returns The percentage.
   */
  get percent(): number {
    const max = this.max ?? 100
    return Math.min(Math.max((this.value / max) * 100, 0), 100)
  }

  /**
   * The rounded percentage shown in the value label.
   * @returns The whole percentage.
   */
  get rounded(): number {
    return Math.round(this.percent)
  }

  /**
   * The clamped aria-valuenow.
   * @returns The clamped value.
   */
  get clamped(): number {
    const max = this.max ?? 100
    return Math.min(Math.max(this.value, 0), max)
  }

  /**
   * The accessible label with the localized default.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  progressLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('ui.progress.label', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Progress' })
  }

  /**
   * Computes the wrapper class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().progressWrapper, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the bar class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  barClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().progressBar(),
      this.cm().progressColor(this.color ?? 'primary'),
      this.indeterminate && this.cm().progressIndeterminate,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the bar's inline style (the translateX fill, unless indeterminate).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  barStyle(): string | null {
    if (this.indeterminate) return null
    return styleText({ transform: `translateX(-${100 - this.percent}%)` })
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }
}
```

#### `PromptDialog`

PromptDialog component.

Composed from `Modal`, `Input` and `Button` exactly as React composes
them. Resolving `onSubmit` closes the dialog and resets it; a rejection
keeps it open with the value intact. React's root is a `Modal`, which
renders a portal while open and nothing while closed, so the host element
leaves the page and the portal renders in `document.body`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-prompt-dialog',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [Button, Input, Modal, MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'open',
    'title',
    'description',
    'placeholder',
    'initialValue',
    'onSubmit',
    'confirmLabel',
    'cancelLabel',
    'destructive',
    'inputMolId',
    'confirmMolId',
    'cancelMolId',
  ],
  outputs: ['close'],
  template: `<mol-modal
    #modal
    [open]="open"
    (close)="onModalClose()"
    [attr.aria-labelledby]="ariaLabelledBy()"
    [attr.aria-describedby]="rendersNode(description) ? descriptionId : null"
    ><form (submit)="handleSubmit($event)">
      <div [class]="cm().stack(3)">
        <h2
          #titleSlot
          [attr.id]="ariaLabelledBy()"
          [class]="cm().cn(cm().textSize('lg'), cm().fontWeight('bold'))"
        >
          @if (isGiven(title)) {
            <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(title)"></ng-container
            >{{ nodeText(title) }}
          } @else {
            <ng-content select="[slot=title]"></ng-content>
          }
        </h2>
        @if (asTemplate(description); as descriptionTemplate) {
          <p [attr.id]="descriptionId" [class]="cm().textSize('sm')">
            <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="descriptionTemplate"></ng-container>
          </p>
        } @else if (rendersNode(description)) {
          <p
            [attr.id]="descriptionId"
            [class]="cm().textSize('sm')"
            [textContent]="nodeText(description)"
          ></p>
        }
        <div
          mol-input
          [value]="value()"
          [placeholder]="placeholder"
          [disabled]="pending()"
          [attr.data-mol-id]="inputMolId"
          (change)="onInput($event)"
        ></div>
        <ng-content></ng-content>
        <div [class]="cm().flex({ justify: 'end', gap: 'sm' })">
          <button
            mol-button
            [type]="'button'"
            [variant]="'ghost'"
            [disabled]="pending()"
            (click)="close.emit($event)"
            [attr.data-mol-id]="cancelMolId"
          >
            @if (isGiven(cancelLabel)) {
              <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(cancelLabel)"></ng-container
              >{{ nodeText(cancelLabel) }}
            } @else {
              {{ cancelText() }}
            }</button
          ><button
            mol-button
            [type]="'submit'"
            [variant]="'solid'"
            [color]="destructive ? 'error' : 'primary'"
            [disabled]="pending()"
            [attr.data-mol-id]="confirmMolId"
          >
            @if (isGiven(confirmLabel)) {
              <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(confirmLabel)"></ng-container
              >{{ nodeText(confirmLabel) }}
            } @else {
              {{ confirmText() }}
            }
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form></mol-modal
  >`,
})
export class PromptDialog implements AfterViewInit, AfterViewChecked, DoCheck {
  /** Whether the dialog is open. */
  open: PromptDialogProps['open'] = false
  /** Dialog heading (node-valued prop). */
  title?: PromptDialogProps['title']
  description?: PromptDialogProps['description']
  /** Input placeholder. */
  placeholder?: PromptDialogProps['placeholder']
  initialValue?: PromptDialogProps['initialValue'] = ''
  onSubmit?: PromptDialogProps['onSubmit']
  confirmLabel?: PromptDialogProps['confirmLabel']
  cancelLabel?: PromptDialogProps['cancelLabel']
  destructive?: PromptDialogProps['destructive'] = false
  /** `data-mol-id` for the text input. */
  inputMolId?: PromptDialogProps['inputMolId']
  /** `data-mol-id` for the confirm button. */
  confirmMolId?: PromptDialogProps['confirmMolId']
  /** `data-mol-id` for the cancel button. */
  cancelMolId?: PromptDialogProps['cancelMolId']

  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  readonly nodeText = nodeText
  /** Whether a node-valued prop was given. */
  readonly isGiven = isGiven
  readonly rendersNode = rendersNode

  @Output() readonly close = new EventEmitter<unknown>()

  @ViewChild('titleSlot') titleSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLHeadingElement>
  @ViewChild(Input) inputComponent?: Input
  @ViewChild('modal', { read: ElementRef }) modalElement?: ElementRef<HTMLElement>

  readonly titleId = nextInstanceId()

  readonly descriptionId = nextInstanceId()

  readonly ariaLabelledBy = signal<string | null>(null)

  /** The current input value. */
  readonly value = signal('')
  /** Whether a submit is in flight. */
  readonly pending = signal(false)

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  private lastOpen = false
  private lastInitial = ''
  private pendingInputFocus = false
  private readonly injector = inject(Injector)

  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => {
      setElementPresent(this.host.nativeElement, false)
      setHostRoot(this.host.nativeElement, this.modalElement?.nativeElement)
      this.ariaLabelledBy.set(
        rendersNode(this.title) || hasContent(this.titleSlot?.nativeElement) ? this.titleId : null,
      )
      this.applyInputLabel(hasContent)
    })
  }

  /** The current class map. */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /** The cancel button's label. */
  cancelText(): string {
    return typeof this.cancelLabel === 'string'
      ? this.cancelLabel
      : this.i18n.t('prompt.cancel', {}, { defaultValue: 'Cancel' })
  }

  /** The confirm button's label. */
  confirmText(): string {
    return typeof this.confirmLabel === 'string'
      ? this.confirmLabel
      : this.i18n.t('prompt.confirm', {}, { defaultValue: 'Confirm' })
  }

  inputLabel(hasContent: SlotReader): string | null {
    const heading = this.titleSlot?.nativeElement
    const nodes = isGiven(this.title)
      ? Array.from(heading?.childNodes ?? [])
      : hasContent.nodes(heading)
    const hasElement = nodes.some((node) => node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE)
    const text = nodes
      .filter((node) => node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE || node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE)
      .map((node) => node.textContent ?? '')
      .join('')
      .trim()
    if (!hasElement && text) return text
    return this.placeholder ?? null
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.lastOpen = !!this.open
    this.lastInitial = this.initialValue ?? ''
    if (this.open) {
      this.value.set(this.initialValue ?? '')
      this.pending.set(false)
      this.pendingInputFocus = true
    }
  }

  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    if (!this.pendingInputFocus) return
    this.pendingInputFocus = false
    afterNextRender(() => this.focusInput(), { injector: this.injector })
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    const open = !!this.open
    if (open !== this.lastOpen || (open && this.initialValue !== this.lastInitial)) {
      this.lastOpen = open
      this.lastInitial = this.initialValue ?? ''
      if (open) {
        this.value.set(this.initialValue ?? '')
        this.pending.set(false)
        this.pendingInputFocus = true
      }
    }
  }

  focusInput(): void {
    this.inputComponent?.inputEl?.nativeElement.focus()
  }

  private applyInputLabel(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    const input = this.inputComponent?.inputEl?.nativeElement
    if (!input) return
    const label = this.inputLabel(hasContent)
    if (label === null) input.removeAttribute('aria-label')
    else input.setAttribute('aria-label', label)
  }

  onInput(event: Event): void {
    this.value.set((event.target as HTMLInputElement).value)
  }

  onModalClose(): void {
    if (this.pending()) return
    this.close.emit()
  }

  handleSubmit(event: Event): void {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (this.pending()) return
    this.pending.set(true)
    void Promise.resolve(this.onSubmit?.(this.value()))
      .then(() => {
        this.pending.set(false)
        this.close.emit()
      })
      .catch((_error: unknown) => {
        this.pending.set(false)
      })
  }
}
```

_(Member comments are shortened to their first sentence, and the 35 longest are left out to fit; the full declaration is in `node_modules/@molecule/app-ui-angular/dist/components/prompt-dialog.component.d.ts`.)_

#### `RadioGroup`

RadioGroup component.

The host is the `role="radiogroup"` div React renders. Selecting an option
emits that option's VALUE (not the DOM event) through the `change` output,
exactly like React's per-option handler.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-radio-group]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolControlledChecked],
  inputs: [
    'options',
    'value',
    'size',
    'label',
    'name',
    'direction',
    'error',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'className',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
    '[attr.role]': '"radiogroup"',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'groupLabel()',
    '[attr.aria-invalid]': 'error ? "true" : null',
    '[attr.aria-describedby]': 'error ? errorId() : null',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <div [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: false }), cm().sp('mb', 2))">{{ label }}</div>
    }
    <div [class]="cm().radioGroupLayout(direction ?? 'vertical')">
      @for (option of options ?? []; track option.value) {
        <label
          [class]="
            cm().cn(cm().controlLabel, (disabled || option.disabled) && cm().controlDisabled)
          "
        >
          <input
            type="radio"
            [attr.name]="groupName()"
            [attr.value]="option.value"
            [molChecked]="value === option.value"
            [disabled]="disabled || option.disabled"
            (change)="select($event)"
            [attr.data-state]="value === option.value ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'"
            [class]="cm().cn(cm().radio({ error: !!error }), cm().cursorPointer)"
          />
          <span [class]="cm().controlText">{{ option.label }}</span>
        </label>
      }
    </div>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="errorId()" [class]="cm().cn(cm().formError, cm().sp('mt', 1))">{{ error }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class RadioGroup implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** The options to render. */
  options?: RadioOption[]
  /** The selected option value. */
  value?: string
  /** Group size (kept for prop parity). */
  size?: unknown
  /** Group label. */
  label?: string
  /** Native radio group name (defaults to a stable generated name). */
  name?: string
  /** Layout direction. */
  direction?: 'vertical' | 'horizontal'
  /** Error message. */
  error?: string
  /** Extra classes for the group root. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables every option. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The native radio group name.
   * @returns The name value.
   */
  groupName(): string {
    return this.name || this.generatedName
  }

  /**
   * The error paragraph id.
   * @returns The id value.
   */
  errorId(): string {
    return `${this.groupName()}-error`
  }

  /**
   * The accessible group label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  groupLabel(): string {
    return (
      this.label ?? this.i18n.t('ui.radioGroup.label', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Radio group' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Emits the selected option's value; the change event stops here so the
   * host-level `change` output binding does not record it a second time. Every
   * radio's checked state is then restored to the controlled `value` — checking
   * one radio natively UNCHECKS the previously selected one, so restoring only
   * the clicked input would leave the group with no selection at all.
   *
   * @param event - The native radio change event.
   */
  select(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    this.change.emit((event.target as HTMLInputElement).value)
    // React always controls radios (checked={value === option.value}); restore.
    const radios = (this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement).querySelectorAll('input[type="radio"]')
    for (const radio of Array.from(radios) as HTMLInputElement[])
      radio.checked = this.value === radio.value
  }

  /** Fires the selected option's value. */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<string>()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `role="radiogroup"` `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** The per-instance native radio-group name when the caller gives none (React's `useId`). */
  private readonly generatedName = nextInstanceId()
}
```

#### `ResponsiveAppShell`

Root of the responsive app shell: owns the breakpoint and drawer state,
closes the drawer on navigation and on desktop-crossing resizes, and
provides everything to the sub-components through the DOM-ancestor
context (React's context provider renders no DOM).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-responsive-app-shell]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['sidebarWidth', 'className', 'dataMolId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'rootStyle()',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class ResponsiveAppShell implements DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  /** Sidebar (and drawer) width: `sm`/`md`/`lg` or an exact pixel number. */
  sidebarWidth?: ResponsiveAppShellProps['sidebarWidth']
  /** Extra classes on the root flex container. */
  className?: ResponsiveAppShellProps['className']
  /** `data-mol-id` for the root (default `shell-root`). */
  dataMolId?: ResponsiveAppShellProps['dataMolId'] = 'shell-root'

  /** Whether the viewport is at the desktop breakpoint. */
  readonly isDesktop = useIsDesktop()
  /** Whether the mobile drawer is open. */
  readonly drawerOpen = signal(false)
  /** Whether a Drawer sub-component is composed. */
  readonly hasDrawer = signal(false)
  /** The drawer panel's DOM id. */
  readonly drawerId = nextInstanceId()
  /** Router state: navigation closes the drawer. */
  readonly router = useRouter()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private lastRoute: string | null = null
  private readonly routeEffect = effect(() => {
    const location = this.router.location()
    const key = `${location.pathname}${location.search}${(location as { hash?: string }).hash ?? ''}`
    if (this.lastRoute === key) return
    this.lastRoute = key
    // React's effect runs on mount too; closing a closed drawer is a no-op.
    this.drawerOpen.set(false)
  })
  private readonly desktopEffect = effect(() => {
    if (this.isDesktop()) this.drawerOpen.set(false)
  })

  /** Registers the shell context on the host before any projected child resolves it. */
  constructor() {
    ShellDomContext.provide(this.host.nativeElement, new ShellContextValueImpl(this))
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The root class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({}),
      this.cm().page,
      this.cm().minH('screen'),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The root's inline style (row on desktop, column below the breakpoint).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  rootStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ flexDirection: this.isDesktop() ? 'row' : 'column' })
  }

  /** Keeps a plain-field drawer width change in sync with the sub-components. */
  ngDoCheck(): void {
    // Nothing to reconcile: every value is read through the context getters.
  }

  /** Removes the context registration (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    ShellDomContext.remove(this.host.nativeElement)
  }
}
```

#### `ResponsiveAppShellTopBar`

The shell's `<header>` landmark: the drawer menu trigger (mobile, when a
Drawer is composed), the brand, and trailing actions.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'header[mol-responsive-app-shell-topbar]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolIconSvg, MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: ['brand', 'actions', 'mobileOnly', 'className', 'dataMolId', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<div
      [class]="cm().cn(cm().flex({ align: 'center', gap: 'sm' }))"
      [style]="'min-width: 0px'"
    >
      @if (shell().hasDrawer && !shell().isDesktop) {
        <button
          type="button"
          (click)="shell().openDrawer()"
          [attr.aria-label]="triggerLabel()"
          [attr.aria-expanded]="shell().drawerOpen ? 'true' : 'false'"
          [attr.aria-controls]="shell().drawerId"
          data-mol-id="shell-drawer-open"
          [class]="triggerClasses()"
          [style]="triggerStyle()"
        >
          <svg mol-icon [data]="menuIcon()" />
        </button>
      }
      <div #brandSlot [style]="'min-width: 0px'">
        <ng-content select="[slot=brand]"></ng-content
        ><ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(brand)"></ng-container>{{ nodeText(brand) }}
      </div>
    </div>
    <div #actionsSlot [class]="cm().cn(cm().flex({ align: 'center', gap: 'sm' }), cm().shrink0)">
      <ng-content select="[slot=actions]"></ng-content
      ><ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(actions)"></ng-container
      >{{ nodeText(actions) }}
    </div>`,
})
export class ResponsiveAppShellTopBar implements OnInit {
  /** Brand/title node (React's `brand`), rendered after the menu trigger. */
  brand?: ResponsiveAppShellTopBarProps['brand']
  /** Trailing actions node (React's `actions`). */
  actions?: ResponsiveAppShellTopBarProps['actions']
  /** Render the top bar only below the breakpoint (no dead DOM on desktop). */
  mobileOnly?: ResponsiveAppShellTopBarProps['mobileOnly'] = false
  /** Extra classes on the `<header>`. */
  className?: ResponsiveAppShellTopBarProps['className']
  /** `data-mol-id` (default `shell-topbar`). */
  dataMolId?: ResponsiveAppShellTopBarProps['dataMolId'] = 'shell-topbar'
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: ResponsiveAppShellTopBarProps['testId']

  /** Template helpers (templates see only class members). */
  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  /** Renders a plain-string/number node value. */
  readonly nodeText = nodeText

  /** The brand wrapper (detached when no brand is given). */
  @ViewChild('brandSlot') brandSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The actions wrapper (detached when no actions are given). */
  @ViewChild('actionsSlot') actionsSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private hasBrand = false
  private hasActions = false

  /** Prepares the header's mount condition (React's `mobileOnly`). */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => {
      // Resolve the shell context while the host is still in the page: a mobileOnly header
      // leaves it on desktop, and its ancestors are the only way to the shell root.
      void this.shell()
      this.syncWrappers(hasContent)
    })
    // Leaves no element in the page while the bar is hidden on desktop (React renders null).
    useHostPresence(() => !(this.mobileOnly && this.shell().isDesktop))
  }

  /**
   * The nearest shell context.
   * @returns The context (its defaults outside the shell).
   */
  shell(): ShellContextValue {
    // Cached: a detached (mobileOnly) header loses its DOM ancestors, so the
    // lookup must not be repeated after the element leaves the tree.
    return this.readContext() ?? contextOrDefault(null)
  }

  /**
   * Reads the shell context from the host's place in the rendered tree.
   * @returns The context, or null outside the shell.
   */
  private readContext(): ShellContextValue | null {
    return ShellDomContext.find(this.host.nativeElement) ?? null
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The header class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'between', gap: 'sm' }),
      this.cm().sp('px', 4),
      this.cm().h(14),
      this.cm().surface,
      this.cm().borderB,
      this.cm().position('sticky'),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The header's inline style (sticky offset and z-index below the drawer).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ top: 0, zIndex: 20 })
  }

  /**
   * The drawer trigger's class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  triggerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'center' }),
      this.cm().shrink0,
      this.cm().roundedFull,
      this.cm().cursorPointer,
    )
  }

  /** The drawer trigger's inline size (44px minimum tap target). */
  triggerStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ width: 44, height: 44 })
  }

  /**
   * The trigger's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  triggerLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('appShell.openMenu', {}, { defaultValue: 'Open navigation menu' })
  }

  /**
   * The menu glyph.
   * @returns The resolved icon.
   */
  menuIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('menu', this.cm().iconMd)
  }

  /**
   * Refuses to render outside a shell (React's `useShellContext` throws).
   */
  ngOnInit(): void {
    requireShell(this.readContext(), 'ResponsiveAppShell.TopBar')
  }

  /** Applies React's conditional brand/actions wrappers. */
  private syncWrappers(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.hasBrand = isGiven(this.brand) || hasContent(this.brandSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.hasActions = isGiven(this.actions) || hasContent(this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement)
    setElementPresent(this.brandSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasBrand)
    setElementPresent(this.actionsSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasActions)
  }
}
```

#### `ResponsiveAppShellSidebar`

The desktop sidebar (`<aside>` + `<nav>` landmarks); renders nothing below
the breakpoint (the Drawer carries the nav there).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'aside[mol-responsive-app-shell-sidebar]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: ['navLabel', 'footer', 'className', 'dataMolId', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<nav [attr.aria-label]="navLabelText()" [class]="cm().cn(cm().flex1, cm().stack(1))">
      <ng-content></ng-content>
    </nav>
    <div
      #footerSlot
      [class]="
        cm().cn(
          cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'between', gap: 'sm' }),
          cm().borderT,
          cm().sp('p', 3),
          cm().shrink0
        )
      "
    >
      <ng-content select="[slot=footer]"></ng-content
      ><ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="asTemplate(footer)"></ng-container>{{ nodeText(footer) }}
    </div>`,
})
export class ResponsiveAppShellSidebar implements OnInit {
  /** Accessible name for the `<nav>` landmark. */
  navLabel?: ResponsiveAppShellSidebarProps['navLabel']
  /** Below-nav content (React's `footer` node value). */
  footer?: ResponsiveAppShellSidebarProps['footer']
  /** Extra classes on the `<aside>`. */
  className?: ResponsiveAppShellSidebarProps['className']
  /** `data-mol-id` (default `shell-sidebar`). */
  dataMolId?: ResponsiveAppShellSidebarProps['dataMolId'] = 'shell-sidebar'
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: ResponsiveAppShellSidebarProps['testId']

  /** Template helpers (templates see only class members). */
  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  /** Renders a plain-string/number node value. */
  readonly nodeText = nodeText

  /** The footer wrapper (detached when no footer is given). */
  @ViewChild('footerSlot') footerSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private hasFooter = false

  /** Prepares the aside's mount condition (React's breakpoint check). */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => {
      // Resolve the shell context while the host is still in the page: below the breakpoint
      // it leaves the page, and its ancestors are the only way to the shell root.
      void this.shell()
      this.syncWrapper(hasContent)
    })
    // Leaves no element in the page below the breakpoint (React renders null).
    useHostPresence(() => this.shell().isDesktop)
  }

  /**
   * The nearest shell context.
   * @returns The context (its defaults outside the shell).
   */
  shell(): ShellContextValue {
    // Cached: a detached (mobile) sidebar loses its DOM ancestors.
    return this.readContext() ?? contextOrDefault(null)
  }

  /**
   * Reads the shell context from the host's place in the rendered tree.
   * @returns The context, or null outside the shell.
   */
  private readContext(): ShellContextValue | null {
    return ShellDomContext.find(this.host.nativeElement) ?? null
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The aside class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ direction: 'col' }),
      this.cm().shrink0,
      this.cm().surface,
      this.cm().borderR,
      this.cm().h('screen'),
      this.cm().position('sticky'),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The aside's inline style (exact width, sticky top, internal scroll).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ top: 0, width: this.shell().sidebarWidthPx, overflowY: 'auto' })
  }

  /**
   * The nav's accessible label.
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  navLabelText(): string {
    return (
      this.navLabel ??
      this.i18n.t('appShell.primaryNav', {}, { defaultValue: 'Primary navigation' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * Refuses to render outside a shell (React's `useShellContext` throws).
   */
  ngOnInit(): void {
    requireShell(this.readContext(), 'ResponsiveAppShell.Sidebar')
  }

  /** Applies React's conditional footer wrapper. */
  private syncWrapper(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.hasFooter = isGiven(this.footer) || hasContent(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement)
    setElementPresent(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasFooter)
  }
}
```

#### `ResponsiveAppShellDrawer`

The mobile nav drawer (<768px): a left-anchored dialog panel over a
backdrop, portaled into `document.body`.

Projected content is created with the consumer's view, so a closed drawer's content exists while
hidden: its components, effects and fetches run. React mounts it only while shown, and again each
time it shows. Write content with state as `*molLazy` (`MolLazy` from `@molecule/app-angular`):
it is created when it shows and destroyed when it hides, as React's is.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-responsive-app-shell-drawer',
  standalone: true,

              cm().borderT,
              cm().sp('p', 3),

export class ResponsiveAppShellDrawer implements OnInit, AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy {
  header?: ResponsiveAppShellDrawerProps['header']
  navLabel?: ResponsiveAppShellDrawerProps['navLabel']
  footer?: ResponsiveAppShellDrawerProps['footer']
  showCloseButton?: ResponsiveAppShellDrawerProps['showCloseButton'] = true
  className?: ResponsiveAppShellDrawerProps['className']
  dataMolId?: ResponsiveAppShellDrawerProps['dataMolId'] = 'shell-drawer'
  closeDataMolId?: ResponsiveAppShellDrawerProps['closeDataMolId'] = 'shell-drawer-close'
  testId?: ResponsiveAppShellDrawerProps['testId']

  readonly asTemplate = asTemplate
  readonly nodeText = nodeText

  @ViewChild('overlay') overlay?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('wrapper') wrapper?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('panel') panel?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('headerRow') headerRow?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('headerSlot') headerSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('footerSlot') footerSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  readonly headerPresent = signal(false)

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly injector = inject(Injector)
  private readonly environmentInjector = inject(EnvironmentInjector)
  private readonly scrollLock = createScrollLock()
  private hasFooter = false
  private locked = false
  private lastOpen = false
  private previouslyFocused: HTMLElement | null = null
  private listeners: (() => void)[] = []

  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => {
      void this.shell()
      this.syncWrappers(hasContent)
      this.syncOpen()
    })
  }

  shell(): ShellContextValue {
    return this.readContext() ?? contextOrDefault(null)
  }

  private readContext(): ShellContextValue | null {
    return ShellDomContext.find(this.host.nativeElement) ?? null
  }

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  panelClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().flex({ direction: 'col' }), this.cm().surface, this.className)
  }

  panelStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({
      width: this.shell().sidebarWidthPx,
      maxWidth: '85vw',
      overflowY: 'auto',
    })
  }

  wrapperStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ zIndex: 41 })
  }

  closeClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'center' }),
      this.cm().w(10),
      this.cm().h(10),
      this.cm().shrink0,
      this.cm().roundedFull,
      this.cm().cursorPointer,
      this.cm().textMuted,
    )
  }

  navLabelText(): string {
    return (
      this.navLabel ??
      this.i18n.t('appShell.primaryNav', {}, { defaultValue: 'Primary navigation' })
    )
  }

  closeLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('appShell.closeMenu', {}, { defaultValue: 'Close navigation menu' })
  }

  closeIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconMd)
  }

  handleBackdropClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    if (event.target !== event.currentTarget) {
      event.stopPropagation()
      return
    }
    this.shell().closeDrawer()

  ngOnInit(): void {
    requireShell(this.readContext(), 'ResponsiveAppShell.Drawer')
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.shell().registerDrawer(true)
    const open = this.shell().drawerOpen && !this.shell().isDesktop
    this.lastOpen = open
    if (open) this.openPanel()
    else this.detachPortal()
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    this.syncOpen()
  }

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.shell().registerDrawer(false)
    this.removeListeners()
    const toRestore = this.lastOpen ? this.previouslyFocused : null
    if (toRestore && !this.environmentInjector.destroyed)
      afterNextRender(
        () => {
          if (document.contains(toRestore)) toRestore.focus()
        },
        { injector: this.environmentInjector },
      )
    if (this.locked) {
      this.scrollLock.unlock()
      this.locked = false
    }
  }

  private syncWrappers(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.headerPresent.set(isGiven(this.header) || hasContent(this.headerSlot?.nativeElement))
    this.hasFooter = isGiven(this.footer) || hasContent(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement)
    setElementPresent(
      this.headerRow?.nativeElement,
      this.headerPresent() || (this.showCloseButton ?? true),
    )
    setElementPresent(this.headerSlot?.nativeElement, this.headerPresent())
    setElementPresent(this.footerSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasFooter)
    setElementPresent(this.host.nativeElement, false)
  }

  private syncOpen(): void {
    const open = this.shell().drawerOpen && !this.shell().isDesktop
    if (open === this.lastOpen) return
    this.lastOpen = open
    if (open) {
      this.openPanel()
    } else {
      this.removeListeners()
      this.detachPortal()
      if (this.locked) {
        this.scrollLock.unlock()
        this.locked = false
      }
      const toRestore = this.previouslyFocused
      if (toRestore)
        afterNextRender(
          () => {
            if (document.contains(toRestore)) toRestore.focus()
          },
          { injector: this.injector },
        )
    }
  }
```

#### `ResponsiveAppShellContent`

The shell's `<main>` landmark.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'main[mol-responsive-app-shell-content]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['className', 'style', 'dataMolId', 'testId'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'dataMolId',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
  },
  template: `<ng-content></ng-content>`,
})
export class ResponsiveAppShellContent {
  /** Extra classes on the `<main>`. */
  className?: ResponsiveAppShellContentProps['className']
  /** Inline styles on the `<main>`. */
  style?: ResponsiveAppShellContentProps['style']
  /** `data-mol-id` (default `shell-content`). */
  dataMolId?: ResponsiveAppShellContentProps['dataMolId'] = 'shell-content'
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: ResponsiveAppShellContentProps['testId']

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The main class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().flex1, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The main's inline style (`min-width: 0` plus the caller's style).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({ minWidth: 0, ...this.style })
  }
}
```

#### `Select`

Select component.

The host is the `div.inputWrapper` React renders. React's select value is
written through the value PROPERTY (a `select` has no value attribute);
a controlled `value` is restored after a change exactly like React's
controlled-input state restore.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-select]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolIconSvg],
  inputs: [
    'options',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'size',
    'label',
    'placeholder',
    'error',
    'hint',
    'clearable',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'required',
    'name',
    'id',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'onFocus',
    'onBlur',
    'onKeyDown',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().inputWrapper',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <label
        [attr.for]="selectId()"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: !!required }), cm().labelBlock)"
        >{{ label }}</label
      >
    }
    <div [class]="cm().inputInner">
      <select
        [attr.id]="selectId()"
        [attr.name]="name"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.required]="required ? '' : null"
        [attr.aria-invalid]="error || fieldContext()?.invalid ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [attr.aria-describedby]="mergedDescribedBy()"
        [class]="classes()"
        [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        [attr.data-mol-id]="molId()"
        (change)="handleChange($event)"
        (focusin)="onFocus?.($event)"
        (focusout)="onBlur?.($event)"
        (keydown)="onKeyDown?.($event)"
        #selectEl
      >
        @if (placeholder) {
          <option value="" disabled>{{ placeholder }}</option>
        }
        @if (clearable && !placeholder) {
          <option value="">{{ clearText() }}</option>
        }
        @for (entry of optionGroups(); track $index) {
          @if (entry.group === '') {
            @for (option of entry.options; track option.value) {
              <option [attr.value]="option.value" [attr.disabled]="option.disabled ? '' : null">
                {{ option.label }}
              </option>
            }
          } @else {
            <optgroup [attr.label]="entry.group">
              @for (option of entry.options; track option.value) {
                <option [attr.value]="option.value" [attr.disabled]="option.disabled ? '' : null">
                  {{ option.label }}
                </option>
              }
            </optgroup>
          }
        }
      </select>
      <div [class]="cm().inputRightElement" [attr.style]="'pointer-events: none'">
        <svg mol-icon [data]="chevron()" />
      </div>
    </div>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="selectId() + '-error'" [class]="cm().formError">{{ error }}</p>
    }
    @if (hint && !error) {
      <p [attr.id]="selectId() + '-hint'" [class]="cm().formHint">{{ hint }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class Select implements AfterViewInit, AfterViewChecked, HasElement<HTMLSelectElement> {
  /** The options to render. */
  options?: SelectPropsOption[]
  /** Controlled value. */
  value?: string
  /** Uncontrolled initial value. */
  defaultValue?: string
  /** Select size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Field label. */
  label?: string
  placeholder?: string
  error?: string
  /** Hint text (shown without an error). */
  hint?: string
  clearable?: boolean
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the select. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the select. */
  disabled?: boolean
  required?: boolean
  /** Form field name. */
  name?: string
  /** Explicit select id. */
  id?: string
  automationId?: string
  dataMolId?: string

  onFocus?: FocusEventHandler
  onBlur?: FocusEventHandler
  onKeyDown?: KeyboardEventHandler

  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()
  /** Fires the selected option's value. */
  @Output() readonly valueChange = new EventEmitter<string>()

  private selectEl?: ElementRef<HTMLSelectElement>
  @ViewChild('selectEl')
  set selectElQuery(ref: ElementRef<HTMLSelectElement> | undefined) {
    this.selectEl = ref
    this.inner.set(ref?.nativeElement ?? null)
  }

  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLSelectElement | null>(null)
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly generatedId = nextInstanceId()

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private restDescribedBy: string | null = null
  private restDescribedByCaptured = false
  private externalMolId: string | undefined

  /** The current class map. */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  selectId(): string {
    return this.id || this.fieldContext()?.id || this.name || this.generatedId
  }

  describedBy(): string | null {
    if (this.error) return `${this.selectId()}-error`
    if (this.hint) return `${this.selectId()}-hint`
    return null
  }
  fieldContext(): FormFieldContext | null {
    return fieldContext(this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement)
  }

  mergedDescribedBy(): string | null {
    const target = this.fieldContext()?.describedBy
    if (target) return [this.restDescribedBy, target].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
    return this.restDescribedBy ?? this.describedBy()
  }

  /** Computes the select class list. */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().select({ error: !!this.error, size: this.size ?? 'md' }),
      this.cm().selectNative,
      this.cm().inputPadRight,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /** The clear option's label. */
  clearText(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('ui.select.clear', undefined, { defaultValue: '--' })
  }

  chevron(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('chevron-down', this.cm().cn(this.cm().iconSm, this.cm().textMuted))
  }

  optionGroups(): { group: string; options: SelectPropsOption[] }[] {
    const acc = new Map<string, SelectPropsOption[]>()
    for (const option of this.options ?? []) {
      const group = option.group || ''
      const list = acc.get(group)
      if (list) list.push(option as SelectPropsOption)
      else acc.set(group, [option as SelectPropsOption])
    }
    return [...acc.entries()].map(([group, options]) => ({ group, options }))
  }

  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  molId(): string | undefined {
    return this.automationId ?? this.dataMolId ?? this.externalMolId
  }

  handleChange(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    this.change.emit(event)
    this.valueChange.emit((event.target as HTMLSelectElement).value)
    this.applyValue(false)
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    if (!this.restDescribedByCaptured) {
      this.restDescribedBy = this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('aria-describedby')
      this.restDescribedByCaptured = true
    }
    adoptHostDataMolId(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      this.selectEl?.nativeElement,
      (id) => {
        this.externalMolId = id
      },
      () => this.molId(),
    )
    moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, this.selectEl?.nativeElement)
    this.applyValue(true)
  }

  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    void this.i18n.locale()
    adoptHostDataMolId(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      this.selectEl?.nativeElement,
      (id) => {
        this.externalMolId = id
      },
      () => this.molId(),
    )
    reapplyRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, this.selectEl?.nativeElement)
    this.applyValue(false)
  }

  private applyValue(initial: boolean): void {
    const select = this.selectEl?.nativeElement
    if (!select) return
    if (this.value !== undefined) {
      const next = String(this.value)
      if (select.value !== next && Array.from(select.options).some((o) => o.value === next))
        select.value = next
    } else if (initial) {
      if (this.defaultValue !== undefined) {
        const target = String(this.defaultValue)
        for (const option of Array.from(select.options)) {
          if (option.value === target) option.setAttribute('selected', '')
          else option.removeAttribute('selected')
        }
        if (select.value !== target) select.value = target
      } else if (select.multiple) {
        select.selectedIndex = -1
      }
    }
  }
}
```
