#### `Separator`

Separator component.

Decorative separators render `role="none"` and no orientation; a semantic
one (`decorative={false}`) renders `role="separator"` plus
`aria-orientation`.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-separator]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'orientation',
    'decorative',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[attr.role]': 'decorative ? "none" : "separator"',
    '[attr.aria-orientation]': 'decorative ? null : orientation',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: '',
})
export class Separator implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Orientation of the separator. */
  orientation: SeparatorProps['orientation'] = 'horizontal'
  /** Decorative separators are purely visual. */
  decorative: SeparatorProps['decorative'] = true
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: SeparatorProps['className']
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: SeparatorProps['style']
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: SeparatorProps['testId']
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: SeparatorProps['automationId']
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the separator class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().separator({ orientation: this.orientation }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }
}
```

#### `SidebarUserCard`

SidebarUserCard component.

React's root is a FRAGMENT (the trigger button plus a drawer), so the trigger
button renders in front of the host (`placeElementBefore`) and the host
itself leaves the DOM. Identity comes from `useAuth()` unless the
matching props override it.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-sidebar-user-card',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Modal, PanelCloseProvider],
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  inputs: [
    'name',
    'secondaryLine',
    'avatarUrl',
    'ariaLabelKey',
    'ariaLabelDefault',
    'dataMolId',
    'className',
  ],
  template: `<button
      #trigger
      type="button"
      (click)="isOpen.set(true)"
      [attr.aria-label]="ariaLabel()"
      [attr.data-mol-id]="dataMolId"
      [class]="triggerClasses()"
    >
      @if (displayAvatar(); as avatar) {
        <img [src]="avatar" alt="" [class]="avatarClasses()" />
      } @else {
        <span aria-hidden="true" [class]="initialsClasses()" [style]="initialsStyle()">{{
          initials()
        }}</span>
      }
      <span [class]="cm().cn(cm().minW(0), cm().flex1)"
        ><span [class]="nameClasses()">{{ displayName() }}</span>
        @if (displaySecondary(); as secondary) {
          <span [class]="secondaryClasses()">{{ secondary }}</span>
        }
      </span></button
    ><mol-modal
      [open]="isOpen()"
      (close)="isOpen.set(false)"
      [className]="cm().drawer"
      [attr.aria-label]="drawerLabel()"
      ><mol-panel-close-provider [close]="closePanel"
        ><ng-content></ng-content></mol-panel-close-provider
    ></mol-modal>`,
})
export class SidebarUserCard {
  /** Display name override (falls back to the auth user, then the guest label). */
  name?: SidebarUserCardProps['name']
  /** Secondary line under the name (falls back to the auth user's email). */
  secondaryLine?: SidebarUserCardProps['secondaryLine']
  /** Avatar image URL override. */
  avatarUrl?: SidebarUserCardProps['avatarUrl']
  /** i18n key for the trigger's aria-label (default `sidebarUserCard.open`). */
  ariaLabelKey?: SidebarUserCardProps['ariaLabelKey']
  /** Fallback aria-label (default `Open account menu`). */
  ariaLabelDefault?: SidebarUserCardProps['ariaLabelDefault']
  /** `data-mol-id` for the trigger (default `sidebar-user-card`). */
  dataMolId?: SidebarUserCardProps['dataMolId'] = 'sidebar-user-card'
  /** Extra classes for the trigger. */
  className?: SidebarUserCardProps['className']

  /** Whether the drawer is open. */
  readonly isOpen = signal(false)
  /** Auth state (identity defaults). */
  readonly auth = useAuth<AccountUserShape>()
  /** Router state: a route change closes the drawer. */
  readonly router = useRouter()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /** The trigger button (adopted beside the host). */
  @ViewChild('trigger') trigger?: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  constructor() {
    usePlacement(() => {
      const trigger = this.trigger?.nativeElement
      if (!trigger) return
      placeElementBefore(trigger, this.host.nativeElement)
      setElementPresent(this.host.nativeElement, false)
    })
  }
  private readonly routeEffect = effect(() => {
    const location = this.router.location()
    void `${location.pathname}${location.search}`
    this.isOpen.set(false)
  })

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /** The trigger's accessible label. */
  ariaLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.ariaLabelKey ?? 'sidebarUserCard.open',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.ariaLabelDefault ?? 'Open account menu',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * The drawer's accessible label. The drawer is a title-less Modal (its
   * panel content carries its own headings), so it is named with the
   * localized `sidebarUserCard.drawer` label: the binding on `<mol-modal>`
   * lands on its host element, which Modal's rest-attribute move copies onto
   * the dialog `<div>` — screen readers announce "Account" instead of an
   * unnamed dialog (axe `aria-dialog-name`).
   * @returns The label text.
   */
  drawerLabel(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('sidebarUserCard.drawer', {}, { defaultValue: 'Account' })
  }

  /**
   * The resolved display name (React's fallback chain).
   * @returns The name.
   */
  displayName(): string {
    return (
      this.name ??
      this.auth.user()?.name ??
      this.auth.user()?.email ??
      this.i18n.t('sidebarUserCard.guest', {}, { defaultValue: 'Guest' })
    )
  }

  /**
   * The resolved secondary line (prop override, else the auth email).
   * @returns The line, or undefined.
   */
  displaySecondary(): string | undefined {
    return this.secondaryLine ?? this.auth.user()?.email ?? undefined
  }

  /**
   * The resolved avatar URL (prop override, else the auth user's).
   * @returns The URL, or undefined.
   */
  displayAvatar(): string | undefined {
    return this.avatarUrl ?? this.auth.user()?.avatarUrl ?? undefined
  }

  /** The name/email initials. */
  initials(): string {
    return getInitials(this.displayName())
  }

  /** The trigger's class list. */
  triggerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', gap: 'sm' }),
      this.cm().sp('px', 3),
      this.cm().sp('py', 3),
      this.cm().sidebarUserCardTrigger,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /** The avatar image class list. */
  avatarClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().w(10),
      this.cm().h(10),
      this.cm().roundedFull,
      this.cm().shrink0,
      this.cm().objectCover,
    )
  }

  /** The initials badge class list. */
  initialsClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().w(10),
      this.cm().h(10),
      this.cm().roundedFull,
      this.cm().flex({ align: 'center', justify: 'center' }),
      this.cm().textSize('sm'),
      this.cm().fontWeight('bold'),
      this.cm().shrink0,
    )
  }

  /** The initials badge's inline style. */
  initialsStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText({
      backgroundColor: 'var(--color-primary)',
      color: 'var(--color-on-primary, #ffffff)',
    })
  }

  /** The display-name class list. */
  nameClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().textSize('sm'),
      this.cm().fontWeight('semibold'),
      this.cm().displayBlock,
      this.cm().textTruncate,
    )
  }

  /** The secondary-line class list. */
  secondaryClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().textSize('xs'),
      this.cm().displayBlock,
      this.cm().textTruncate,
      this.cm().textMuted,
    )
  }

  /** Closes the drawer (the panel-close context's callback). */
  closePanel = (): void => {
    this.isOpen.set(false)
  }
}
```

#### `Skeleton`

Skeleton loading placeholder.

The host is the skeleton `<div>`; width/height/borderRadius accept React's
number-or-string shapes (numbers become px), and a circle skeleton without
a height uses the width for both, exactly like React.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-skeleton]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'width',
    'height',
    'circle',
    'borderRadius',
    'animation',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: '',
})
export class Skeleton implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Width (number = px, or any CSS value). */
  width?: number | string
  /** Height (number = px, or any CSS value). */
  height?: number | string
  /** Whether the skeleton is a circle. */
  circle = false
  /** Border radius override (number = px). */
  borderRadius?: number | string
  /** Animation style. */
  animation: 'pulse' | 'wave' | 'none' = 'pulse'
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the skeleton class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().skeleton(),
      this.circle && this.cm().skeletonCircle,
      this.animation === 'none' && this.cm().skeletonNone,
      this.animation === 'wave' && this.cm().skeletonWave,
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const computed: Record<string, unknown> = {
      ...this.style,
      width: typeof this.width === 'number' ? `${this.width}px` : this.width,
      height: typeof this.height === 'number' ? `${this.height}px` : this.height,
      borderRadius: this.circle
        ? '9999px'
        : typeof this.borderRadius === 'number'
          ? `${this.borderRadius}px`
          : this.borderRadius,
    }
    // If circle, make width and height equal.
    if (this.circle && this.width && !this.height) {
      computed.height = computed.width
    }
    return styleText(computed)
  }
}
```

#### `SkeletonText`

Skeleton text lines (a container with N skeleton bars; the last one is 60%).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-skeleton-text]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Skeleton],
  inputs: ['lines', 'className'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
  },
  template: `@for (line of lineList; track $index) {
    <div mol-skeleton [height]="16" [width]="$index === lines - 1 ? '60%' : '100%'"></div>
  }`,
})
export class SkeletonText implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Number of skeleton lines. */
  lines = 3
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * An array as long as the line count (template iteration).
   * @returns Placeholder items.
   */
  get lineList(): number[] {
    return Array.from({ length: this.lines })
  }

  /**
   * Computes the container class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().skeletonTextContainer, this.className)
  }
}
```

#### `SkeletonCircle`

Skeleton circle (avatar placeholder).

React renders `<Skeleton circle width={size} height={size} className/>` —
a single div; the host IS that div (no wrapper).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-skeleton-circle]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: ['size', 'className'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
  },
  template: '',
})
export class SkeletonCircle implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>` (the skeleton itself). */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Circle diameter in pixels. */
  size = 40
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * Computes the class list (a circle skeleton).
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().skeleton(), this.cm().skeletonCircle, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the circle inline style.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const px = typeof this.size === 'number' ? `${this.size}px` : this.size
    return styleText({ width: px, height: px, borderRadius: '9999px' })
  }
}
```

#### `Spinner`

Spinner component.

The host element is the `<div role="status">` React renders. A raw CSS
color (not a known color name) is expressed as inline border colors, and
`data-*`/`aria-*` rest props land on the host (React's spread target).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-spinner]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'size',
    'color',
    'label',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
  ],
  host: {
    '[attr.role]': '"status"',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'ariaLabel()',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
    <span [class]="cm().srOnly">{{ label }}</span>
  }`,
})
export class Spinner implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div role="status">`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** Spinner size. */
  size: Size = 'md'
  /** Named color variant or a raw CSS color. */
  color?: string
  /** Accessible label; also renders a screen-reader-only text node. */
  label?: string
  /** Extra classes joined onto the spinner classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The accessible label (localized default when not given).
   * @returns The aria-label text.
   */
  ariaLabel(): string {
    return this.label || this.i18n.t('ui.spinner.loading', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Loading' })
  }

  /**
   * Computes the spinner class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().spinner({ size: this.size }), this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the merged inline style (caller style plus a raw-color border
   * override when `color` is a CSS color rather than a named variant).
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    const colorStyle =
      this.color &&
      typeof this.color === 'string' &&
      !NAMED_COLORS.includes(this.color as (typeof NAMED_COLORS)[number])
        ? { borderColor: this.color, borderTopColor: 'transparent' }
        : undefined
    return styleText({ ...this.style, ...colorStyle })
  }
}
```

#### `Switch`

Switch component.

React's root is a fragment (hidden native checkbox + label): both elements
render in front of the host, which leaves the page, so the rendered tree is
React's — no wrapper element around the pair. Clicking the visible button
clicks the hidden checkbox, whose GENUINE native `change` event is
forwarded as the `change` output.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-switch',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolControlledChecked],
  inputs: [
    'label',
    'checked',
    'size',
    'color',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'onClick',
  ],
  template: `<input
      type="checkbox"
      [molChecked]="!!checked"
      [disabled]="disabled"
      aria-hidden="true"
      tabindex="-1"
      [class]="cm().srOnly"
      (change)="handleChange($event)"
      #hiddenInput
    />
    <label [class]="labelClasses()">
      <button
        type="button"
        role="switch"
        [attr.aria-checked]="checked ? 'true' : 'false'"
        [disabled]="disabled"
        [attr.data-state]="state()"
        (click)="handleClick($event)"
        [class]="classes()"
        [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        [attr.data-mol-id]="molId()"
        #trigger
      >
        <span [attr.data-state]="state()" [class]="cm().switchThumb({ size: size ?? 'md' })"></span>
      </button>
      <span [class]="cm().controlText" #labelSlot
        >{{ label }}<ng-content select="[slot=label]"></ng-content
      ></span>
    </label>`,
})
export class Switch implements AfterViewChecked, HasElement<HTMLButtonElement> {
  /** Switch label (text, or projected `[slot=label]` nodes). */
  label?: unknown
  /** Whether the switch is on. */
  checked?: boolean
  /** Switch size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Switch color. */
  color?: ColorVariant
  /** Extra classes joined onto the switch classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the button. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the switch. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  /** A `data-mol-id` given as a plain rest attribute (React's `rootProps` precedence). */
  dataMolId?: string

  /**
   * A caller's click handler (React's `onClick` prop). Runs BEFORE the toggle
   * and can veto it with `preventDefault()`.
   */
  onClick?: MouseEventHandler

  /** The genuine native `change` event of the hidden checkbox. */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /** The hidden native checkbox. */
  @ViewChild('hiddenInput') hiddenInput?: ElementRef<HTMLInputElement>
  /** The visible trigger button (React's `...rest` spread target). */
  private trigger?: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>
  /** The trigger button view query; also feeds the caller's ref element. */
  @ViewChild('trigger')
  set triggerQuery(ref: ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement> | undefined) {
    this.trigger = ref
    this.inner.set(ref?.nativeElement ?? null)
  }

  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLButtonElement | null>(null)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the `role="switch"` button. */
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()
  /** The label text wrapper. */
  @ViewChild('labelSlot') labelSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)
  private hasLabel = false
  /** The `data-mol-id` a caller bound on the host (React spreads it onto the trigger). */
  private externalMolId: string | undefined

  /**
   * Renders the host's content (the checkbox and the label) in front of the
   * host, which leaves the page, and applies React's `{!!label && <span>}`
   * label condition.
   */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => {
      const host = this.host.nativeElement
      placeContentBefore(host, host)
      setElementPresent(host, false)
      // React spreads the rest props on the switch button.
      setHostRoot(host, this.trigger?.nativeElement, { except: ['data-mol-id'] })
      this.hasLabel = !!this.label || hasContent(this.labelSlot?.nativeElement)
      setElementPresent(this.labelSlot?.nativeElement, this.hasLabel)
    })
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The data-state of the switch and thumb.
   * @returns `checked` or `unchecked`.
   */
  state(): 'checked' | 'unchecked' {
    return this.checked ? 'checked' : 'unchecked'
  }

  /**
   * Computes the switch button class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().switchBase({ size: this.size ?? 'md', color: this.color }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the label class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  labelClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().controlLabel, this.disabled && this.cm().controlDisabled)
  }

  /**
   * Serializes the inline style for the button.
   * @returns The style attribute text.
   */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /**
   * The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`).
   * @returns The attribute value.
   */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return this.automationId ?? this.dataMolId ?? this.externalMolId
  }

  /**
   * Clicking the button runs the caller's `onClick` first (it may veto the
   * toggle with `preventDefault()`), then clicks the hidden checkbox (unless
   * disabled or the event was vetoed), exactly like React's handler.
   *
   * @param event - The button click event.
   */
  handleClick(event: MouseEvent): void {
    this.onClick?.(event)
    if (!this.disabled && !event.defaultPrevented) this.hiddenInput?.nativeElement.click()
  }

  /**
   * Forwards the hidden checkbox's genuine native `change` event (React
   * forwards the raw browser event, not a React synthetic one) and then
   * restores the controlled value, like React's controlled-input state
   * restore (the input mirrors the `checked` prop, never free user state).
   * The event's propagation stops here so the host-level `change` binding
   * does not record it a second time.
   *
   * @param event - The native change event.
   */
  handleChange(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    this.change.emit(event)
    const input = this.hiddenInput?.nativeElement
    if (input) input.checked = !!this.checked
  }

  /**
   * Moves rest attributes onto the button after queries resolve.
   */
  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    adoptHostDataMolId(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      this.trigger?.nativeElement,
      (id) => {
        this.externalMolId = id
      },
      () => this.molId(),
    )
  }
}
```

#### `Table`

Table component.

The host is the wrapper `<div>` (`div[mol-table]`), exactly React's root;
the `<table>` carries the className/style/testId/rest. Column render
functions are templates (with `a0`–`a2` = the render function's arguments)
or plain functions returning text.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-table]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolIconSvg, MolTemplateOutlet, Spinner],
  inputs: [
    'data',
    'columns',
    'rowKey',
    'bordered',
    'striped',
    'hoverable',
    'size',
    'emptyContent',
    'loading',
    'sort',
    'onSort',
    'onRowClick',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'id',
    'title',
  ],
  host: {
    '[attr.id]': 'null',
    '[attr.title]': 'null',
    '[class]': 'cm().tableWrapper',
  },
  template: `<span #emptySlot><ng-content select="[slot=emptyContent]"></ng-content></span>
    @if (loading) {
      <div [class]="cm().tableLoadingOverlay"><div mol-spinner [size]="'lg'"></div></div>
    }
    <table
      #tableEl
      [class]="tableClasses()"
      [style]="tableStyle()"
      [attr.data-testid]="testId"
      [attr.data-mol-id]="molId()"
      [attr.id]="id"
      [attr.title]="title"
    >
      <thead [class]="cm().tableHeader">
        <tr [class]="cm().tableRow">
          @for (column of columns; track column.key) {
            <th
              [class]="headClasses(column)"
              [style]="columnStyle(column)"
              (click)="handleSort(column)"
              [attr.aria-sort]="ariaSort(column)"
            >
              <div [class]="cm().tableSortWrapper">
                @if (asTemplate(column.header); as headerTpl) {
                  <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="headerTpl"></ng-container>
                } @else {
                  {{ nodeText(column.header) }}
                }
                @if (column.sortable && sort?.key === column.key) {
                  <span [class]="cm().tableSortIcon"><svg mol-icon [data]="sortIcon()" /></span>
                }
              </div>
            </th>
          }
        </tr>
      </thead>
      <tbody [class]="cm().tableBody">
        @if (data.length === 0) {
          <tr>
            <td [attr.colspan]="columns.length" [class]="cm().tableEmptyCell" #emptyCell>
              @if (!hasEmpty()) {
                {{ emptyText() }}
              }
            </td>
          </tr>
        } @else {
          @for (row of data; track rowKeyOf(row, $index); let rowIdx = $index) {
            <tr [class]="rowClasses($index)" (click)="handleRowClick(row, $index)">
              @for (column of columns; track column.key) {
                <td [class]="cellClasses(column)">
                  @if (column.render) {
                    @if (asTemplate(column.render); as cellTpl) {
                      <ng-container
                        [molTemplateOutlet]="cellTpl"
                        [molTemplateOutletContext]="cellContext(row, column, rowIdx)"
                      ></ng-container>
                    } @else {
                      {{ renderText(row, column, $index) }}
                    }
                  } @else {
                    {{ cellText(row, column) }}
                  }
                </td>
              }
            </tr>
          }
        }
      </tbody>
    </table>`,
})
export class Table implements AfterViewInit, AfterViewChecked, HasElement<HTMLTableElement> {
  data: Record<string, unknown>[] = []
  columns: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>[] = []
  rowKey?: string | ((row: Record<string, unknown>) => string | number)
  bordered?: boolean
  striped?: boolean
  hoverable = true
  size?: string
  emptyContent?: unknown
  loading?: boolean
  sort?: { key: string; direction: 'asc' | 'desc' }
  onSort?: (key: string, direction: 'asc' | 'desc') => void
  onRowClick?: (row: Record<string, unknown>, index: number) => void
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string
  automationId?: string
  dataMolId?: string
  id?: string
  title?: string

  @ViewChild('emptySlot') emptySlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  @ViewChild('emptyCell') emptyCell?: ElementRef<HTMLTableCellElement>
  @ViewChild('tableEl')
  set tableElQuery(ref: ElementRef<HTMLTableElement> | undefined) {
    this.inner.set(ref?.nativeElement ?? null)
  }

  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLTableElement | null>(null)
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()

  readonly hasEmpty = signal(false)

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.reconcile(hasContent))
  }

  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private externalMolId: string | undefined

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  tableClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().table, this.bordered && this.cm().tableBordered, this.className)
  }

  tableStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  molId(): string | undefined {
    return this.automationId ?? this.dataMolId ?? this.externalMolId
  }

  headClasses(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tableHead,
      column.align === 'center' && this.cm().textCenter,
      column.align === 'right' && this.cm().textRight,
      column.sortable && this.cm().tableHeadSortable,
    )
  }

  columnStyle(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string | null {
    return styleText({ width: column.width })
  }

  cellClasses(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tableCell,
      column.align === 'center' && this.cm().textCenter,
      column.align === 'right' && this.cm().textRight,
    )
  }

  rowClasses(index: number): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tableRow,
      this.striped && index % 2 === 1 && this.cm().tableRowStriped,
      this.hoverable && this.cm().tableRowHoverable,
      !!this.onRowClick && this.cm().tableRowClickable,
    )
  }

  ariaSort(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string | null {
    if (!column.sortable) return null
    if (this.sort?.key !== column.key) return 'none'
    return this.sort.direction === 'asc' ? 'ascending' : 'descending'
  }

  sortIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon(
      this.sort?.direction === 'asc' ? 'chevron-up' : 'chevron-down',
      this.cm().iconSm,
    )
  }

  emptyText(): string {
    return this.i18n.t('ui.table.empty', undefined, { defaultValue: 'No data available' })
  }

  nodeText(value: unknown): string {
    return typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''
  }

  asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return value instanceof TemplateRef ? value : null
  }

  rowKeyOf(row: Record<string, unknown>, index: number): string | number {
    if (typeof this.rowKey === 'function') return this.rowKey(row)
    if (this.rowKey && row[this.rowKey] !== undefined) return row[this.rowKey] as string | number
    return index
  }

  cellContext(
    row: Record<string, unknown>,
    column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>,
    index: number,
  ): Record<string, unknown> {
    const value = row[column.key as string]
    return { $implicit: value, a0: value, a1: row, a2: index }
  }

  renderText(
    row: Record<string, unknown>,
    column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>,
    index: number,
  ): string {
    const render = column.render as unknown
    if (typeof render !== 'function') return ''
    const value = row[column.key as string]
    const result = (render as (v: unknown, r: unknown, i: number) => unknown)(value, row, index)
    return typeof result === 'string' ? result : ''
  }

  cellText(row: Record<string, unknown>, column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): string {
    const value = row[column.key as string]
    return value === undefined || value === null ? '' : String(value)
  }

  handleSort(column: TableColumn<Record<string, unknown>>): void {
    if (!column.sortable || !this.onSort) return
    const key = column.key as string
    const currentDirection = this.sort?.key === key ? this.sort.direction : undefined
    this.onSort(key, currentDirection === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc')
  }
```

#### `Tabs`

Tabs component.

The host is the root `<div>` (`div[mol-tabs]`). Implements the WAI-ARIA APG
"automatic activation" tabs pattern: roving tabindex, Left/Right/Home/End
move both focus and the active tab, and disabled tabs are skipped. Item
`icon` and `content` are node values (templates); `label` is a string or a
node value.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-tabs]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'items',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'variant',
    'size',
    'fitted',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'disabled',
  ],
  outputs: ['change'],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'className',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
  },
  template: `<div [class]="listClasses()" role="tablist">
      @for (item of items; track item.value) {
        <button
          #tabBtn
          [id]="tabId(item.value)"
          type="button"
          role="tab"
          [attr.aria-selected]="item.value === activeValue() ? 'true' : 'false'"
          [attr.aria-controls]="controlsId(item)"
          [attr.data-state]="item.value === activeValue() ? 'active' : 'inactive'"
          [attr.tabindex]="item.value === activeValue() ? 0 : -1"
          [disabled]="!!item.disabled || disabled"
          (click)="selectTab(item.value)"
          (keydown)="handleKeyDown($event)"
          [class]="triggerClasses()"
        >
          @if (item.icon) {
            <span [class]="cm().tabTriggerIcon">
              @if (asTemplate(item.icon); as iconTpl) {
                <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="iconTpl"></ng-container>
              } @else {
                {{ nodeText(item.icon) }}
              }
            </span>
          }
          @if (asTemplate(item.label); as labelTpl) {
            <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="labelTpl"></ng-container>
          } @else {
            {{ nodeText(item.label) }}
          }
        </button>
      }
    </div>
    @if (hasContent()) {
      @if (activeContent(); as contentTpl) {
        <div
          role="tabpanel"
          [id]="panelId(activeValue() ?? '')"
          [attr.aria-labelledby]="tabId(activeValue() ?? '')"
          [class]="contentClasses()"
        >
          <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="contentTpl"></ng-container>
        </div>
      } @else {
        <div
          role="tabpanel"
          [id]="panelId(activeValue() ?? '')"
          [attr.aria-labelledby]="tabId(activeValue() ?? '')"
          [class]="contentClasses()"
          [textContent]="contentText()"
        ></div>
      }
    }`,
})
export class Tabs implements OnInit, HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** The tabs. */
  items: TabItem<string>[] = []
  /** Controlled active value. */
  value?: string
  /** Initial active value for the uncontrolled form. */
  defaultValue?: string
  /** Tab-list visual variant. */
  variant?: 'line' | 'enclosed' | 'soft-rounded' | 'solid-rounded'
  /** Tab size. */
  size?: 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl'
  /** Whether tabs stretch to fill the list. */
  fitted?: boolean
  /** Extra classes for the root. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  dataMolId?: string
  /** Disables every tab, whatever an item says (React's `disabled`). */
  disabled?: boolean

  /** Fires with the selected tab's value (React's `onChange`). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<string>()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.host)

  /** The tab buttons (for focus movement). */
  @ViewChildren('tabBtn') tabButtons?: QueryList<ElementRef<HTMLButtonElement>>
  /** The per-instance id base (the normalizer renumbers ids). */
  readonly idBase = nextInstanceId()
  /** The uncontrolled active value. */
  private readonly internalValue = signal<string | undefined>(undefined)

  /** The current class map. */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  ngOnInit(): void {
    this.internalValue.set(this.defaultValue || this.items[0]?.value)
  }

  activeValue(): string | undefined {
    if (this.value !== undefined) return this.value
    return this.internalValue()
  }

  /** The tab button id for a value. */
  tabId(value: string): string {
    return `${this.idBase}-tab-${value}`
  }

  /** The tabpanel id for a value. */
  panelId(value: string): string {
    return `${this.idBase}-tabpanel-${value}`
  }

  controlsId(item: TabItem<string>): string | null {
    return item.value === this.activeValue() && !!item.content ? this.panelId(item.value) : null
  }

  /** The tab-list class list. */
  listClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tabsList({ variant: this.variant, size: this.size }),
      this.fitted && this.cm().tabsFitted,
    )
  }

  /** The tab-button class list. */
  triggerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().tabsTrigger({ variant: this.variant, size: this.size }),
      this.fitted && this.cm().tabTriggerFitted,
    )
  }

  /** The tabpanel class list. */
  contentClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().tabsContent({ variant: this.variant, size: this.size })
  }

  /** Serializes the root's inline style. */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  /** The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`). */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return (
      this.automationId ??
      this.dataMolId ??
      this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ??
      undefined
    )
  }

  /** Narrows a node-valued prop to a TemplateRef. */
  asTemplate(value: unknown): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return value instanceof TemplateRef ? value : null
  }

  /** A plain-string node value's text. */
  nodeText(value: unknown): string {
    return typeof value === 'string' ? value : ''
  }

  /** Whether the active tab has content (React renders no panel without it). */
  hasContent(): boolean {
    const active = this.items.find((item) => item.value === this.activeValue())
    return !!active?.content
  }

  /** The active tab's content as a template. */
  activeContent(): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    const active = this.items.find((item) => item.value === this.activeValue())
    return active ? this.asTemplate(active.content) : null
  }

  /** The active tab's plain-string content. */
  contentText(): string {
    const active = this.items.find((item) => item.value === this.activeValue())
    return this.nodeText(active?.content)
  }

  /** Selects a tab and notifies the caller (React's `selectTab`). */
  selectTab(tabValue: string): void {
    if (this.value === undefined) this.internalValue.set(tabValue)
    this.change.emit(tabValue)
  }

  /** The APG keyboard model: Left/Right/Home/End move focus AND selection. */
  handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if (this.disabled) return
    const enabled = this.items.filter((item) => !item.disabled)
    if (enabled.length === 0) return
    const active = this.activeValue()
    const currentIndex = enabled.findIndex((item) => item.value === active)
    let nextIndex: number
    switch (event.key) {
      case 'ArrowRight':
        nextIndex = currentIndex === -1 ? 0 : (currentIndex + 1) % enabled.length
        break
      case 'ArrowLeft':
        nextIndex = currentIndex === -1 ? 0 : (currentIndex - 1 + enabled.length) % enabled.length
        break
      case 'Home':
        nextIndex = 0
        break
      case 'End':
        nextIndex = enabled.length - 1
        break
      default:
        return
    }
    event.preventDefault()
    const nextValue = enabled[nextIndex].value
    this.selectTab(nextValue)
    this.focusTab(nextValue)
  }

  /** Moves focus to the tab with the given value (React's `tabRefs.focus()`). */
  private focusTab(value: string): void {
    const match = this.tabButtons?.find((ref) => ref.nativeElement.id === this.tabId(value))
    match?.nativeElement.focus()
  }
}
```

#### `Textarea`

Textarea component.

The host is the `div.inputWrapper` React renders. React's textarea
`onChange` fires on `input` events; a controlled `value` is restored after
user input exactly like React's controlled-input state restore.
`autoResize` recomputes the inline height after every check and on real
`input` events (matching React's effect + native listener pair).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-textarea]',
  standalone: true,
  inputs: [
    'size',
    'label',
    'error',
    'hint',
    'autoResize',
    'minRows',
    'maxRows',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'disabled',
    'required',
    'value',
    'defaultValue',
    'placeholder',
    'name',
    'id',
    'rows',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'onFocus',
    'onBlur',
    'onKeyDown',
  ],
  host: {
    '[class]': 'cm().inputWrapper',
  },
  template: `@if (label) {
      <label
        [attr.for]="textareaId()"
        [class]="cm().cn(cm().label({ required: !!required }), cm().labelBlock)"
        >{{ label }}</label
      >
    }
    <textarea
      [attr.id]="textareaId()"
      [attr.name]="name"
      [attr.placeholder]="placeholder"
      [disabled]="disabled"
      [attr.required]="required ? '' : null"
      [attr.rows]="rows || minRows || 3"
      [attr.aria-invalid]="error || fieldContext()?.invalid ? 'true' : 'false'"
      [attr.aria-describedby]="mergedDescribedBy()"
      [class]="classes()"
      [attr.style]="hostStyle()"
      [attr.data-testid]="testId"
      [attr.data-mol-id]="molId()"
      (input)="handleInput($event)"
      (change)="handleChange($event)"
      (focusin)="onFocus?.($event)"
      (focusout)="onBlur?.($event)"
      (keydown)="onKeyDown?.($event)"
      #textareaEl
    ></textarea>
    @if (error) {
      <p [attr.id]="textareaId() + '-error'" [class]="cm().formError">{{ error }}</p>
    }
    @if (hint && !error) {
      <p [attr.id]="textareaId() + '-hint'" [class]="cm().formHint">{{ hint }}</p>
    }`,
})
export class Textarea implements AfterViewInit, AfterViewChecked, HasElement<HTMLTextAreaElement> {
  /** Textarea size. */
  size?: Size
  /** Field label. */
  label?: string
  /** Error message (associates via aria-describedby). */
  error?: string
  /** Hint text (shown without an error). */
  hint?: string
  /** Whether the box resizes to its content. */
  autoResize?: boolean
  /** Minimum rendered rows (autoResize floor). */
  minRows?: number
  /** Maximum rendered rows (autoResize ceiling). */
  maxRows?: number
  /** Extra classes joined onto the textarea classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid` for the textarea. */
  testId?: string
  /** Disables the textarea. */
  disabled?: boolean
  /** Marks the textarea required (label styling). */
  required?: boolean
  /** Controlled value. */
  value?: string
  /** Uncontrolled initial value. */
  defaultValue?: string
  /** Placeholder text. */
  placeholder?: string
  /** Form field name. */
  name?: string
  /** Explicit textarea id. */
  id?: string
  /** Explicit rows attribute. */
  rows?: number
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  dataMolId?: string

  onFocus?: FocusEventHandler
  onBlur?: FocusEventHandler
  onKeyDown?: KeyboardEventHandler

  /** Fires the raw `input` event (React's textarea onChange). */
  @Output() readonly change = new EventEmitter<Event>()

  /** The native textarea (React's `...rest` spread target). */
  private textareaEl?: ElementRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>
  /** The native textarea view query; also feeds the caller's ref element. */
  @ViewChild('textareaEl')
  set textareaElQuery(ref: ElementRef<HTMLTextAreaElement> | undefined) {
    this.textareaEl = ref
    this.inner.set(ref?.nativeElement ?? null)
  }

  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLTextAreaElement | null>(null)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the native `<textarea>`. */
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()

  private readonly host = inject(ElementRef)

  private readonly generatedId = nextInstanceId()

  /** Reactive translation, read in the injection context. */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /** The `aria-describedby` the caller spread onto the host (React's rest). */
  private restDescribedBy: string | null = null
  /** Whether the rest-describedby value was captured. */
  private restDescribedByCaptured = false
  private externalMolId: string | undefined

  /** The current class map. */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  textareaId(): string {
    return this.id || this.fieldContext()?.id || this.name || this.generatedId
  }

  /** The aria-describedby target (error or hint paragraph). */
  describedBy(): string | null {
    if (this.error) return `${this.textareaId()}-error`
    if (this.hint) return `${this.textareaId()}-hint`
    return null
  }
  /** The nearest enclosing `FormField` context (React's cloned props). */
  fieldContext(): FormFieldContext | null {
    return fieldContext(this.host.nativeElement as HTMLElement)
  }

  mergedDescribedBy(): string | null {
    const target = this.fieldContext()?.describedBy
    if (target) return [this.restDescribedBy, target].filter(Boolean).join(' ')
    return this.restDescribedBy ?? this.describedBy()
  }

  /** Computes the textarea class list. */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(
      this.cm().textarea({ error: !!this.error, size: this.size }),
      this.className,
    )
  }

  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.autoResize ? { minHeight: 0, resize: 'none', ...this.style } : this.style)
  }

  /** The `data-mol-id` value (`automationId` wins over a rest `data-mol-id`). */
  molId(): string | undefined {
    return this.automationId ?? this.dataMolId ?? this.externalMolId
  }

  handleInput(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
    this.change.emit(event)
    const textarea = this.textareaEl?.nativeElement
    if (this.value !== undefined && textarea && textarea.value !== this.value)
      textarea.value = this.value
    this.resize()
  }

  handleChange(event: Event): void {
    event.stopPropagation()
  }

  resize(): void {
    const element = this.textareaEl?.nativeElement
    if (!this.autoResize || !element) return
    element.style.height = 'auto'
    const lineHeight = parseInt(window.getComputedStyle(element).lineHeight) || 20
    const minHeight = (this.minRows ?? 3) * lineHeight
    const maxHeight = this.maxRows ? this.maxRows * lineHeight : Infinity
    const newHeight = Math.min(Math.max(element.scrollHeight, minHeight), maxHeight)
    element.style.height = `${newHeight}px`
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    if (!this.restDescribedByCaptured) {
      this.restDescribedBy = this.host.nativeElement.getAttribute('aria-describedby')
      this.restDescribedByCaptured = true
    }
    adoptHostDataMolId(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      this.textareaEl?.nativeElement,
      (id) => {
        this.externalMolId = id
      },
      () => this.molId(),
    )
    moveRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, this.textareaEl?.nativeElement)
    this.applyValue(true)
    this.resize()
  }

  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    // Registering this view against the locale: a switch marks it for check,
    // so this hook runs and the reapply below moves the caller's re-bound
    // rest attribute onto the textarea. Without the read, zoneless change
    // detection never re-checks this view — the parent's re-bound
    // `[attr.aria-label]` would land on the host and stay there.
    void this.i18n.locale()
    adoptHostDataMolId(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      this.textareaEl?.nativeElement,
      (id) => {
        this.externalMolId = id
      },
      () => this.molId(),
    )
    reapplyRestAttributes(this.host.nativeElement, this.textareaEl?.nativeElement)
    this.applyValue(false)
    this.resize()
  }

  private applyValue(initial: boolean): void {
    const textarea = this.textareaEl?.nativeElement
    if (!textarea) return
    if (this.value !== undefined) {
      const next = String(this.value)
      if (textarea.value !== next) textarea.value = next
    } else if (initial && this.defaultValue !== undefined && textarea.value === '') {
      textarea.value = String(this.defaultValue)
    }
  }
}
```

#### `ThemeToggle`

ThemeToggle component.

Button that flips the wired theme bond between light and dark. The host is
the `<button>` itself (`button[mol-theme-toggle]`), matching React's root
exactly. Ships React's conventions out of the box: `data-mol-id`,
`data-mode`, `aria-pressed` and the `theme.toggle` i18n `aria-label`.
Extra attributes (`data-*`, `title`, …) land on the host — React's spread
target — and unknown props like `disabled` bind the host attribute
directly, so the browser suppresses the click exactly as React does.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'button[mol-theme-toggle]',
  standalone: true,
  imports: [Icon],
  inputs: ['ariaLabelKey', 'ariaLabelDefault', 'darkIcon', 'lightIcon', 'iconSize', 'className'],
  host: {
    type: 'button',
    'data-mol-id': 'theme-toggle',
    '[attr.data-mode]': 'mode()',
    '[attr.aria-pressed]': 'mode() === "dark" ? "true" : "false"',
    '[attr.aria-label]': 'labelText()',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '(click)': 'theme.toggleTheme()',
  },
  template: `@if (mode() === 'dark') {
      <svg mol-icon-root [name]="darkIcon ?? 'moon'" [size]="iconSizePx()"></svg>
    } @else {
      <svg mol-icon-root [name]="lightIcon ?? 'sun'" [size]="iconSizePx()"></svg>
    }`,
})
export class ThemeToggle {
  /** i18n key for the `aria-label` (default `theme.toggle`). */
  ariaLabelKey?: ThemeToggleProps['ariaLabelKey']
  /** Fallback `aria-label` if the i18n key is missing. */
  ariaLabelDefault?: ThemeToggleProps['ariaLabelDefault']
  /** Icon name rendered in dark mode (default `moon`). */
  darkIcon?: ThemeToggleProps['darkIcon']
  /** Icon name rendered in light mode (default `sun`). */
  lightIcon?: ThemeToggleProps['lightIcon']
  /** Pixel size for the rendered icon (default 20). */
  iconSize?: ThemeToggleProps['iconSize']
  /** Extra classes joined onto the toggle classes. */
  className?: ThemeToggleProps['className']

  /** Theme state and actions, read in the component's injection context. */
  readonly theme = useTheme()

  /**
   * Reactive translation, read in the injection context. Its `t` reads the
   * locale, so the view re-renders the text on a locale switch (the static `t`
   * does not).
   */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  /**
   * The current theme mode.
   * @returns `'light'` or `'dark'`.
   */
  mode(): 'light' | 'dark' {
    return this.theme.mode()
  }

  /**
   * The localized `aria-label`.
   * @returns The translated label text.
   */
  labelText(): string {
    return this.i18n.t(
      this.ariaLabelKey ?? 'theme.toggle',
      {},
      {
        defaultValue: this.ariaLabelDefault ?? 'Toggle theme',
      },
    )
  }

  /**
   * Computes the toggle class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    const cm: UIClassMap = getClassMap()
    return cm.cn(cm.themeToggleButton, this.className)
  }

  /**
   * The rendered icon pixel size.
   * @returns The size (default 20 when a later-bound write left it unset).
   */
  iconSizePx(): number {
    return this.iconSize ?? 20
  }
}
```

#### `Toast`

Toast component.

The host is the toast `<div>` (`div[mol-toast]`), exactly React's root. The
auto-dismiss countdown pauses on hover AND focus (WCAG 2.2.1) and resumes
with the remaining time; the announced role follows `status`
(`warning`/`error` assertive, everything else polite).

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'div[mol-toast]',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [MolIconSvg, MolTemplateOutlet],
  inputs: [
    'title',
    'description',
    'status',
    'duration',
    'dismissible',
    'closeLabel',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'position',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'id',
  ],
  outputs: ['dismiss'],
  host: {
    // static `title="…"` on the host, where the browser shows it as a tooltip.
    '[attr.title]': 'null',
    '[class]': 'classes()',
    '[style]': 'hostStyle()',
    '[attr.data-testid]': 'testId',
    '[attr.data-mol-id]': 'molId()',
    '[attr.id]': 'id',
    '[attr.role]': 'role()',
    '[attr.data-state]': 'visible() ? "open" : null',
    '(mouseenter)': 'pauseTimer()',
    '(mouseleave)': 'resumeTimer()',
    '(focusin)': 'pauseTimer()',
    '(focusout)': 'resumeTimer()',
  },
  template: `@if (statusIcon(); as icon) {
      <span [class]="cm().toastIconWrapper"><svg mol-icon [data]="icon" /></span>
    }
    <div [class]="cm().toastContentWrapper">
      @if (title) {
        <div [class]="cm().toastTitle">{{ title }}</div>
      }
      <div #descriptionSlot [class]="cm().toastDescription">
        @if (descriptionText(); as descriptionTextValue) {
          {{ descriptionTextValue }}
        }
        <ng-container [molTemplateOutlet]="descriptionTemplate()"></ng-container
        ><span #childrenSlot><ng-content></ng-content></span>
      </div>
    </div>
    @if (dismissible) {
      <button
        type="button"
        [class]="cm().toastClose"
        [attr.aria-label]="closeText()"
        (click)="handleDismiss()"
      >
        <svg mol-icon [data]="closeIcon()" />
      </button>
    }`,
})
export class Toast implements AfterViewInit, DoCheck, OnDestroy, HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** The toast title. */
  title?: string
  description?: unknown
  /** The toast status. */
  status: ColorVariant = 'info'
  /** Auto-dismiss delay in ms (`0` keeps it until dismissed). */
  duration = 5000
  /** Whether the close button renders. */
  dismissible = true
  /** The close button's accessible label. */
  closeLabel?: string
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string
  /** Inline style object (React camelCase). */
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  /** `data-testid`. */
  testId?: string
  /** Accepted for prop parity; positioning is the container's job. */
  position?: ToastPosition
  /** `data-mol-id` automation id (React's `automationId`). */
  automationId?: string
  dataMolId?: string
  id?: string

  @Output() readonly dismiss = new EventEmitter<void>()

  @ViewChild('descriptionSlot') descriptionSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The children capture point. */
  @ViewChild('childrenSlot') childrenSlot?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>

  /** Whether the toast is still rendered (React's `isVisible`). */
  readonly visible = signal(true)

  private readonly el = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly hostRef: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the host `<div>`. */
  readonly element = hostElement(this.hostRef)

  /** Reactive translation, read in the injection context. */
  private readonly i18n = useTranslation()

  private readonly host: HTMLElement = this.el.nativeElement
  private remaining: number
  private startedAt: number | null = null
  private timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  private lastDuration: number
  private hasChildren = false

  /** Prepares the countdown state (the timer starts at view init). */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement((hasContent) => this.reconcile(hasContent))
    useHostPresence(() => this.visible())
    this.remaining = this.duration
    this.lastDuration = this.duration
  }

  /** The current class map. */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /** The mapped class-map variant. */
  variant(): 'default' | 'success' | 'warning' | 'error' | 'info' {
    return STATUS_VARIANT[this.status] || 'default'
  }

  /** The toast class list. */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().toast({ variant: this.variant() }), this.className)
  }

  /** Serializes the inline style. */
  hostStyle(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  molId(): string | undefined {
    return this.automationId ?? this.dataMolId ?? this.host.getAttribute('data-mol-id') ?? undefined
  }

  /** The announced role: assertive for warnings/errors, polite otherwise. */
  role(): 'alert' | 'status' {
    return this.variant() === 'warning' || this.variant() === 'error' ? 'alert' : 'status'
  }

  /** The status glyph, when the variant has one. */
  statusIcon(): ResolvedIcon | null {
    const name = STATUS_ICON[this.variant()]
    return name ? resolveIcon(name, this.cm().iconMd) : null
  }

  /** The close button's X glyph. */
  closeIcon(): ResolvedIcon {
    return resolveIcon('x-mark', this.cm().iconSm)
  }

  /** The string description, when the description is a string. */
  descriptionText(): string | null {
    return typeof this.description === 'string' ? this.description : null
  }

  /** The description as a template, when it is a node value. */
  descriptionTemplate(): TemplateRef<unknown> | null {
    return this.description instanceof TemplateRef ? this.description : null
  }

  /** The close button's accessible label. */
  closeText(): string {
    return this.closeLabel ?? this.i18n.t('ui.toast.close', undefined, { defaultValue: 'Close' })
  }

  /** Dismisses the toast and calls the callback (React's `handleDismiss`). */
  handleDismiss(): void {
    this.visible.set(false)
    this.dismiss.emit()
  }

  /** Pauses the countdown, keeping the time left (React's `pauseTimer`). */
  pauseTimer(): void {
    if (this.startedAt === null) return
    const elapsed = Date.now() - this.startedAt
    this.remaining = Math.max(0, this.remaining - elapsed)
    this.clearTimer()
  }

  resumeTimer(): void {
    if (this.remaining > 0) this.startTimer(this.remaining)
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    this.restart()
  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    if (this.duration !== this.lastDuration) {
      this.lastDuration = this.duration
      this.restart()
    }
  }

  /** Stops the countdown (React's unmount cleanup). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    this.clearTimer()
  }

  /** React's effect body: reset the remaining time and start counting. */
  private restart(): void {
    this.remaining = this.duration
    this.startTimer(this.duration)
  }

  /** Clears the running timer (React's `clearTimer`). */
  private clearTimer(): void {
    if (this.timer) {
      clearTimeout(this.timer)
      this.timer = undefined
    }
    this.startedAt = null
  }

  /** Starts the countdown (React's `startTimer`). */
  private startTimer(ms: number): void {
    this.clearTimer()
    if (ms <= 0) return
    this.startedAt = Date.now()
    this.timer = setTimeout(() => {
      this.visible.set(false)
      this.dismiss.emit()
    }, ms)
  }

  private reconcile(hasContent: SlotReader): void {
    this.hasChildren = hasContent(this.childrenSlot?.nativeElement)
    this.adoptChildren()
  }

  private adoptChildren(): void {
    const span = this.childrenSlot?.nativeElement
    if (!span) return
    const wrapper = this.descriptionSlot?.nativeElement
    const descWins = this.descriptionText() !== null || this.descriptionTemplate() !== null
    setElementPresent(span, false)
    if (wrapper && !descWins && this.hasChildren) placeContent(span, wrapper)
    else placeContent(span, null)
    this.syncDescription()
  }

  /** Applies React's `{!!(description || children) && <div>…</div>}`. */
  private syncDescription(): void {
    const wrapper = this.descriptionSlot?.nativeElement
    if (!wrapper) return
    const hasDescription = this.descriptionText() !== null || this.descriptionTemplate() !== null
    setElementPresent(wrapper, hasDescription || this.hasChildren)
  }
}
```

#### `ToastContainer`

Toast container: positions toasts on screen via a body portal.

React's root is a portal: the container `<div>` is moved into
`document.body` and the host element leaves the page, so nothing is left at
the component's place.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-toast-container',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  inputs: ['position', 'className'],
  template: `<div #container [class]="classes()"><ng-content></ng-content></div>`,
})
export class ToastContainer implements HasElement<HTMLDivElement> {
  /** The container position (default `bottom-right`). */
  position: ToastPosition = 'bottom-right'
  /** Extra classes. */
  className?: string

  /** The container element (moved to `document.body`). */
  private container?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  /** The container view query; also feeds the caller's ref element. */
  @ViewChild('container')
  set containerQuery(ref: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement> | undefined) {
    this.container = ref
    this.inner.set(ref?.nativeElement ?? null)
  }

  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  /** The element a caller's ref receives: the positioned container `<div>`. */
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)

  /** Renders the container in `document.body` (React's portal) and takes the host out of the page. */
  constructor() {
    usePlacement(() => {
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined')
        placeElement(this.container?.nativeElement, document.body)
      setElementPresent(this.host.nativeElement, false)
    })
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The container class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  classes(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().toastContainer({ position: this.position }), this.className)
  }
}
```

#### `ToastProvider`

ToastProvider: manages global toast state and renders children plus a
positioned toast container (React's context provider + portal).

The context value is registered on a DOM element (React context flows
through the rendered tree, which Angular DI cannot reach for projected
content); `useToast()` reads it through the DOM ancestors. React's provider
renders its children and the container's portal, no element of its own, so
the children render in front of the host and the host leaves the page; the
context is registered on the parent element, the one whose child list the
children now belong to.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-toast-provider',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  imports: [Toast],
  inputs: ['position'],
  template: `<span #childrenCapture><ng-content></ng-content></span>
    <div #container [class]="containerClasses()">
      @for (toast of toasts(); track toast.id) {
        <div
          mol-toast
          [title]="toast.title"
          [description]="toast.description"
          [status]="toast.status ?? 'info'"
          [duration]="toast.duration ?? 5000"
          [dismissible]="toast.dismissible ?? true"
          [closeLabel]="toast.closeLabel"
          [className]="toast.className"
          [style]="toast.style"
          [testId]="toast.testId"
          [automationId]="toast.automationId"
          [id]="toast.id"
          (dismiss)="removeToast(toast.id)"
        ></div>
      }
    </div>`,
})
export class ToastProvider implements OnDestroy {
  /** The default position for toasts (default `bottom-right`). */
  position: ToastPosition = 'bottom-right'

  /** The children capture point (removed once children are adopted). */
  @ViewChild('childrenCapture') childrenCapture?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  /** The toast container (moved to `document.body`). */
  @ViewChild('container') container?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  /** The current toasts. */
  readonly toasts = signal<(ToastProps & { id: string })[]>([])

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  /** The value consumers read; its closures keep the live toast state. */
  private readonly value = new ToastContextImplementation(this)

  /**
   * Renders the children in front of the host (which leaves the page),
   * registers the context where they render, and portals the container.
   */
  constructor() {
    ToastDomContext.provide(this.host.nativeElement, this.value)
    usePlacement(() => {
      const capture = this.childrenCapture?.nativeElement
      const host = this.host.nativeElement
      if (capture) {
        placeContentBefore(capture, host)
        setElementPresent(capture, false)
      }
      setElementPresent(host, false)
      if (typeof document !== 'undefined')
        placeElement(this.container?.nativeElement, document.body)
    })
  }

  /**
   * The current class map.
   * @returns The UIClassMap.
   */
  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  /**
   * The container class list.
   * @returns The class string.
   */
  containerClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().toastContainer({ position: this.position })
  }

  /**
   * Adds a toast and returns its handle (React's `addToast`). A caller's `id`
   * is the handle and the toast element's id; otherwise one is generated.
   * @param toast - The toast to add.
   * @returns The id.
   */
  addToast(toast: Omit<ToastProps, 'onDismiss'>): string {
    const id = toast.id ?? `toast-${++toastCounter}`
    this.toasts.update((prev) => [...prev, { ...toast, id }])
    return id
  }

  /**
   * Removes a toast by id (React's `removeToast`).
   * @param id - The toast's id.
   */
  removeToast(id: string): void {
    this.toasts.update((prev) => prev.filter((toast) => toast.id !== id))
  }

  /** Removes the context registration (unmount). */
  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    ToastDomContext.remove(this.host.nativeElement)
  }
}
```

#### `Tooltip`

Tooltip component.

React's root is a FRAGMENT (a trigger wrapper `<div>` plus a portal), so
the trigger wrapper renders in front of the host (`placeElementBefore`),
the host itself leaves the DOM, and the portal element renders in
`document.body` while visible: the rendered tree and focus paths are
React's. A single-element trigger receives `aria-describedby` while the
tooltip shows; show/hide are coordinated across instances exactly like
React's module-level `activeTooltips` map.

Projected content is created with the consumer's view, so a tooltip's `[slot=content]` exists
while hidden: its components, effects and fetches run. React mounts it only while shown, and
again each time it shows. Write content with state as `*molLazy` (`MolLazy` from
`@molecule/app-angular`): it is created when it shows and destroyed when it hides, as React's is.

```typescript
@Component({
  selector: 'mol-tooltip',
  standalone: true,
  hostDirectives: [MolPlacement],
  inputs: [
    'content',
    'placement',
    'delay',
    'hasArrow',
    'className',
    'style',
    'testId',
    'automationId',
    'dataMolId: data-mol-id',
    'id',
    'title',
    'onDoubleClick',
  ],
  template: `<div
      #triggerWrap
      [class]="cm().tooltipTrigger"
      role="presentation"
      (mouseover)="show()"
      (mouseout)="scheduleHide()"
      (focusin)="handleFocus($event)"
      (focusout)="hide()"
      (keydown)="handleKeyDown($event)"
      (click)="hide()"
    >
      <span #childrenCapture><ng-content></ng-content></span>
    </div>
    <div #portalWrap [style]="portalStyleText()">
      <div
        #contentEl
        role="tooltip"
        [id]="boxId()"
        [class]="contentClasses()"
        [style]="contentStyleText()"
        [attr.data-testid]="testId"
        [attr.data-mol-id]="molId()"
        [attr.title]="title"
        (dblclick)="onDoubleClick?.($event)"
        (mouseover)="keepVisible()"
        (mouseout)="scheduleHide()"
      >
        <ng-content select="[slot=content]"></ng-content>
      </div>
      @if (hasArrow) {
        <span aria-hidden="true" [style]="arrowStyleText()"></span>
      }
    </div>`,
})
export class Tooltip
  implements AfterViewInit, AfterViewChecked, DoCheck, OnDestroy, HasElement<HTMLDivElement>
{
  content?: unknown
  placement: TooltipPlacement = 'top'
  delay = 0
  hasArrow = false
  className?: string
  style?: Record<string, unknown>
  testId?: string
  automationId?: string
  dataMolId?: string
  id?: string
  title?: string
  onDoubleClick?: MouseEventHandler

  readonly tooltipId = nextInstanceId()
  boxId(): string {
    return this.id ?? this.tooltipId
  }

  molId(): string | undefined {
    return this.automationId ?? this.dataMolId ?? this.externalMolId
  }
  readonly visible = signal(false)
  private readonly position = signal<Position>({ top: 0, left: 0 })

  @ViewChild('triggerWrap') triggerWrap?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('childrenCapture') childrenCapture?: ElementRef<HTMLSpanElement>
  @ViewChild('portalWrap') portalWrap?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>
  @ViewChild('contentEl') contentEl?: ElementRef<HTMLDivElement>

  private readonly host: ElementRef<HTMLElement> = inject(ElementRef)
  private readonly inner = signal<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
  readonly element = this.inner.asReadonly()
  private readonly cdr = inject(ChangeDetectorRef)
  private showTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  private hideTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined
  private readonly hideListener = (): void => this.hide()
  private readonly escapeListener = (event: KeyboardEvent): void => {
    if (event.key === 'Escape') this.hide()
  }
  private escapeBound = false
  private lastVisible = false
  private lastContentText: string | null = null
  private lastPlacement: TooltipPlacement | null = null
  private associateEl: Element | null = null
  private externalMolId: string | undefined

  constructor() {
    afterEveryRender(() => this.repositionForContentChange())
    usePlacement((hasContent) => {
      const wrap = this.triggerWrap?.nativeElement
      const capture = this.childrenCapture?.nativeElement
      if (!wrap || !capture) return
      placeElementBefore(wrap, this.host.nativeElement)
      setElementPresent(this.host.nativeElement, false)
      setHostRoot(this.host.nativeElement, this.contentEl?.nativeElement, {
        except: ['data-mol-id'],
      })
      placeContent(capture, wrap)
      setElementPresent(capture, false)
      const children = hasContent.nodes(capture)
      const only = children.length === 1 ? slotElement(children[0]) : null
      if (only === this.associateEl) return
      if (this.visible()) {
        this.associateEl?.removeAttribute('aria-describedby')
        only?.setAttribute('aria-describedby', this.boxId())
      }
      this.associateEl = only
    })
  }

  private repositionForContentChange(): void {
    const text = this.contentEl?.nativeElement.textContent ?? null
    if (text === this.lastContentText && this.placement === this.lastPlacement) return
    this.lastContentText = text
    this.lastPlacement = this.placement
    if (this.visible()) this.updatePosition()
  }

  cm(): UIClassMap {
    return getClassMap()
  }

  contentClasses(): string {
    return this.cm().cn(this.cm().tooltipContent, this.className)
  }

  contentStyleText(): string | null {
    return styleText(this.style)
  }

  portalStyleText(): string | null {
    return styleText({
      position: 'absolute',
      top: this.position().top,
      left: this.position().left,
      zIndex: 9999,
    })
  }

  arrowStyleText(): string | null {
    return styleText(arrowStyle(this.placement))
  }

  show(): void {
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined') {
      const previous = activeTooltips.get(document)
      if (previous !== this.hideListener) previous?.()
      activeTooltips.set(document, this.hideListener)
    }
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
    clearTimeout(this.showTimer)
    if (this.delay > 0) {
      this.showTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        this.visible.set(true)
        this.cdr.markForCheck()
      }, this.delay)
    } else {
      this.visible.set(true)
      this.cdr.markForCheck()
    }
  }

  hide(): void {
    clearTimeout(this.showTimer)
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && activeTooltips.get(document) === this.hideListener) {
      activeTooltips.delete(document)
    }
    this.visible.set(false)
    this.cdr.markForCheck()
  }

  scheduleHide(): void {
    clearTimeout(this.showTimer)
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
    this.hideTimer = setTimeout(() => this.hide(), 150)
  }

  keepVisible(): void {
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
  }

  handleFocus(event: FocusEvent): void {
    const target = event.target as HTMLElement
    try {
      if (typeof target.matches === 'function' && target.matches(':focus-visible')) this.show()
    } catch (_error) {
    }
  }

  handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent): void {
    if (event.key === 'Escape') this.hide()
  }

  ngAfterViewInit(): void {
    setElementPresent(this.portalWrap?.nativeElement, false)
    this.lastVisible = false
  }

  ngAfterViewChecked(): void {
    adoptHostDataMolId(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      this.contentEl?.nativeElement,

        this.externalMolId = id
      },
      () => this.molId(),

  }

  ngDoCheck(): void {
    adoptHostDataMolId(
      this.host.nativeElement,
      this.contentEl?.nativeElement,
      (id) => {
        this.externalMolId = id

      () => this.molId(),

    if (visible === this.lastVisible) return
    this.lastVisible = visible
    if (visible) this.attachPortal()
    else this.detachPortal()

  ngOnDestroy(): void {
    clearTimeout(this.showTimer)
    clearTimeout(this.hideTimer)
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && activeTooltips.get(document) === this.hideListener) {
      activeTooltips.delete(document)
    }
    if (this.escapeBound) document.removeEventListener('keydown', this.escapeListener)
    this.associateEl = null
  }

  private attachPortal(): void {
    const portal = this.portalWrap?.nativeElement
    if (!portal) return
    placeElement(portal, document.body)
    this.inner.set(this.contentEl?.nativeElement ?? null)
    this.updatePosition()
    this.associateEl?.setAttribute('aria-describedby', this.boxId())
    if (!this.escapeBound) {
      document.addEventListener('keydown', this.escapeListener)
      this.escapeBound = true
    }
  }
```
